<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50073879' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50073879' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50073879'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 4 Argon is the latest in Google&#x27;s Gemini lineup, offering stronger reasoning, higher factual accuracy, and more reliable tool usage across tasks. It signals continued maturation of high-end multilingual agents aimed at developers and enterprises, with an emphasis on safety, efficiency, and real-world usefulness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 4 Argon 是谷歌 Gemini 系列的最新版本，提升了推理能力、事实准确性和跨任务的工具使用稳定性。该发布体现高端多语言代理的持续成熟，重点放在安全性、效率与实际应用价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50048312' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50048312' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passkeys are a trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Passkeys 是陷阱。请不要使用它们。</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1093</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 559</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50048312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems. It cautions readers to consider trade-offs and to explore alternatives like hardware-backed keys or password managers. The piece invites debate about modern authentication design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。强调权衡取舍并探讨硬件密钥、密码管理工具等替代方案，鼓励对现代认证设计的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122800' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122800' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/ecdsa-curve-origins' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude reveals ECDSA curve origins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 揭示 ECDSA 曲线的来源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1044</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 386</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122800'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented. That provenance matters because the trustworthiness and backdoor risk of standard curves depend on transparent, auditable generation procedures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。曲线生成的可追溯性很重要，直接关系到标准曲线是否值得信任以及是否存在后门风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50091788' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50091788' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.csoonline.com/article/4234854/ai-agents-arent-going-rogue-frontier-labs-are-skipping-security-basics.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Containment and monitoring failures are to blame for AI hacking reports: experts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>专家称AI黑客报道源于封控与监控失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 945</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 616</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50091788'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security experts attribute AI hacking reports to containment and monitoring failures rather than rogue AI behavior. The piece underscores the need for stronger containment, better monitoring, and robust incident-response practices when deploying AI agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全专家将 AI 黑客报道归因于封控与监控失败，而非 AI 自主失控。文章强调在部署 AI 代理时需加强封控、改进监控，并建立健全的事件响应机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.csoonline.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/4234854-0-28632700-1792004597-shutterstock_2472843149.jpg?quality=50&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1024' data-full='https://www.csoonline.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/4234854-0-28632700-1792004597-shutterstock_2472843149.jpg?quality=50&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50050553' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50050553' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mapwriting.substack.com/p/dentists' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do dentists know what they&#x27;re doing?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>牙医真的知道自己在做什么吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50050553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 934</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50050553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 658</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50050553'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners. It urges patients to seek second opinions and clinicians to adopt clearer guidelines and transparency to reduce unnecessary procedures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。作者建议患者寻求第二诊疗意见，并呼吁行业采纳更清晰的指南与透明度以减少不必要的手术。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash-1017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek-v4.1-Flash-1017</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊如何抬升全网价格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 912</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 708</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50084757'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of how Amazon&#x27;s pricing power propagates across the web, driving price increases on rival sites and in affiliate networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析亚马逊定价力量如何在全网扩散，推动竞争对手网站和分销网络的价格上涨。文章揭示平台驱动的定价动态及对构建价格敏感型应用或市场的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Spark 开源发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50107379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 911</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50107379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 471</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50107379'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta AI has open-sourced Muse Spark, a high-performance model designed for multimodal tasks. The release aims to broaden access to advanced AI capabilities, with tutorials and benchmarks to help developers evaluate and deploy locally.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta AI 将 Muse Spark 开源，提供面向多模态任务的高性能模型。此次发布旨在降低门槛，并附带教程与基准，帮助开发者在本地评估与部署。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/opencode/status/2111516673953935833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenCode Hacked: User credentials leaked</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenCode 被黑：用户凭据泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50133401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 898</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50133401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50133401'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenCode suffered a breach with user credentials exposed; the incident underscores the importance of credential hygiene, breach response, and potential follow-up security measures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenCode 遭遇数据泄露，部分用户凭据被曝光。这一事件凸显了凭据安全、应急响应以及后续加强身份与访问管理的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50073633' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50073633' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/redesigning-source-control' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Redesigning source control at Anthropic</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50055304' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50055304' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/unprecedented-cyberattack-cripples-supermarket-fridges-across-europe-as-hackers-activate-automatic-defrosting-delivery-and-store-prices-also-impacted-by-incident' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers activate automatic defrosting in supermarket fridges across Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客在欧洲多国超市冷藏设备上启用自动除霜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 871</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 971</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50055304'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock. The incident underscores how IoT-enabled retail devices can be manipulated at scale, highlighting security gaps in supply chains and the importance of robust device hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。这一事件凸显了零售物联网设备的安全脆弱性，以及对供应链安全治理的迫切需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/o7hTqTruL84oHxvg6pYS3K-2000-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/o7hTqTruL84oHxvg6pYS3K-2000-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50065859' data-tags='Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50065859' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sjNpfz4UzqU' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Woman who rescued injured crow keeps getting &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from birds [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>救助受伤乌鸦的女子持续收到鸟儿送来的‘感谢礼物’（视频）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 839</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50065859'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A heartwarming clip shows a woman who rescued an injured crow continuing to receive &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from the birds, illustrating surprising reciprocity in wildlife behavior. The video captures the quirks of animal gratitude and the human-animal bond that forms around care. A light note on how small acts can ripple through nature in endearing ways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位救助受伤乌鸦的女子的视频记录了鸟儿持续给她送来‘谢礼’，揭示了人鸟关系中的意外互动和动物行为的趣味一面。这个温情故事体现了善举在自然界里可能引发的连锁反应。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50105647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neural networks can be kept clean through careful data handling and privacy-preserving training, addressing concerns about data leakage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过谨慎的数据处理与隐私保护训练，可以让神经网络保持“干净”，降低数据泄露的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50054092' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50054092' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/ledger-confirms-hardware-wallet-spy-implant-behind-crypto-thefts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ledger confirms spy chip implant behind wallet crypto thefts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ledger 证实硬件钱包存在间谍芯片植入，涉多起加密盗窃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 777</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 491</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50054092'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware. The disclosure adds to a wary narrative around provenance, tamper-resistance, and firmware integrity in popular wallets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。此事凸显源头可追溯性、抗篡改能力与固件完整性在热销钱包中的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/10/12/Ledger.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/10/12/Ledger.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50071017'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple announces HomeView, a new feature aimed at simplifying how users view and control smart devices in the home. It underscores Apple&#x27;s continuing effort to deepen the Home ecosystem with privacy-conscious design and tighter device interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布了 HomeView，旨在简化家庭中智能设备的查看与控制。此举体现苹果继续扩展家居生态的决心，强调隐私设计与设备互操作性的提升。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50048642' data-tags='Privacy,Data,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50048642' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fingerprints are the new cookies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>指纹识别成为新的网络追踪手段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 738</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 360</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50048642'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites. It underscores the ongoing tension between usability and privacy online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。文章讨论隐私影响及可能的对策，包括反指纹技术与浏览器保护措施，强调在线可用性与隐私之间的持续张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imgs.xkcd.com/comics/password_strength.png' data-full='https://imgs.xkcd.com/comics/password_strength.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50070302' data-tags='Web,Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50070302' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.firefox.com/en-US/enterprise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox Enterprise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla 推出 Firefox 企业版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 732</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 315</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50070302'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features. The move broadens Firefox&#x27;s appeal in corporate IT, as organizations seek privacy-first browsers with managed configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。此举有助于 Firefox 在企业环境中的竞争力，满足组织对隐私与可控配置的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.firefox.com/media/img/firefox/flare/og.4d50d9b1674b.png' data-full='https://www.firefox.com/media/img/firefox/flare/og.4d50d9b1674b.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://typeof.net/Iosevka/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iosevka is an open-source, sans-serif and slab-serif monospace font</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Iosevka 开源的等宽字体家族，涵盖无衬线与方衬线风格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 731</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50084240'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iosevka is an open-source monospace font family that blends sans-serif and slab-serif variants. It offers broad customization and a cohesive aesthetic for developers across editors, terminals, and UI elements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Iosevka 是一个开源的等宽字体家族，覆盖无衬线和方衬线等多种风格。它为代码编辑器、终端和界面提供单一且可定制的字体方案，兼顾可读性与紧凑字形。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50069499' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50069499' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>11 year old unsoundness bugs found in Rust borrow checker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 借用检查器揭露11年前的不健全性漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 731</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50069499'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered. The find underscores the enduring complexity of guaranteeing memory safety in a sophisticated type system and could drive targeted fixes or improved diagnostics in future releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。此发现可能推动针对性修复与改进诊断能力，影响未来版本的安全性保障。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fe8008382407d6e93a041f1acbbbf6e11006c29cd139f134de4e5a810647bb3c/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fe8008382407d6e93a041f1acbbbf6e11006c29cd139f134de4e5a810647bb3c/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。文章主张建立更清晰的基准、公开的能力陈述和统一的评估标准。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs. It challenges conventional language choices and invites reconsideration of full-stack approach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。它挑战了传统的语言选择观，鼓励重新考虑全栈开发的路径。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 工程师因在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 650</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 539</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50078141'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Federal authorities arrested a former OpenAI engineer over allegations that he planted a listening device in an executive conference room. The case underscores ongoing concerns about insider risk and corporate security in AI labs, especially given the sensitivity of leadership-level discussions. If proven, it could invite scrutiny of internal controls and monitoring policies in fast-moving tech companies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前 OpenAI 工程师因涉嫌在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕。此案突显企业内部风险与安保的重要性，尤其在处理敏感策略和产品开发信息的 AI 研究机构。若指控成立，可能促使公司加强内部控制与员工监控政策。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50131256' data-tags='Energy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50131256' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pv-magazine.com/2026/09/29/solar-surpasses-nuclear-power-globally-for-first-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solar surpasses nuclear power globally for first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 408</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50131256'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solar power has overtaken nuclear as the world&#x27;s largest source of electricity for the first time, marking a milestone in the shift to renewables. The growth reflects rapid installations, cheaper PV hardware, and supportive policies, while grid integration and storage remain challenges. The development underscores a decarbonization trend with wide implications for utilities and energy markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球太阳能首次超过核电，成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着可再生能源转型的里程碑。快速的装机增长、日益降低的光伏组件成本以及利好政策推动起作用，但电网接入与储能仍然是挑战。此趋势强化了去碳化的长期走向，对公用事业和能源市场具有广泛影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/hans-ott-4e4ZKnPbZAI-unsplash-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/hans-ott-4e4ZKnPbZAI-unsplash-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 637</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jev faces the classic trolley problem, using it to explore how humans and AI systems approach moral trade-offs in split-second decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jev 面对经典的电车难题，用以探讨在瞬间决策中人类与 AI 如何权衡道德取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50088935' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50088935' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scotthelme.co.uk/passkeys-are-not-a-trap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passkeys are not a trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Passkeys 不是陷阱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 629</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 778</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088935'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys offer phishing-resistant, passwordless authentication and smoother sign-ins across devices. The piece argues that concerns about risks are overstated and emphasizes device-bound trust with robust recovery options, supporting a broader shift to modern web authentication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys 提供防钓鱼的无密码认证和跨设备的顺畅登录。文章认为对其风险的担忧被高估，强调设备绑定的信任关系与可行的恢复机制，从而推动向现代化、无密码的网页认证过渡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/passkeys-trap.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/passkeys-trap.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122878' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122878' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexwang.ai/posts/associative-memory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You could have invented transformers from memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你本可以凭记忆发明 Transformer</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 621</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122878'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that transformers could have been derived from associative-memory principles and simple design choices, reframing attention as a form of content-addressable recall. That perspective clarifies why transformers generalize well and suggests alternative conceptual paths that might have yielded the architecture earlier.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张可以从联想记忆的原理和若干简单设计中推导出 Transformer，把注意力机制视为一种基于内容的检索。这个视角解释了 Transformer 的良好泛化性，并暗示有其他概念路径可能更早带来相似架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexwang.ai/images/share-card_hu_ba29a66ace5a286c.jpg' data-full='https://alexwang.ai/images/share-card_hu_ba29a66ace5a286c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50054207' data-tags='Legal,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50054207' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c64g1l5389kvo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deloitte falsified documents to evade tax on Abramovich&#x27;s wealth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德勤伪造文件以逃税阿布拉莫维奇财富</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50054207'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deloitte allegedly falsified documents to evade taxes on Roman Abramovich&#x27;s wealth, according to UK investigations. The revelation adds to the scrutiny around professional services firms and tax avoidance practices, with potential implications for audits, liability, and regulatory oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据英国调查称，德勤伪造文件以规避罗曼·阿布拉莫维奇财富的税务。这一披露凸显专业服务机构在税收规避中的争议，引发对审计责任、合规与监管监管的潜在影响的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/abc3/live/b06f9880-c621-11f1-bc2e-018d645d8d21.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/abc3/live/b06f9880-c621-11f1-bc2e-018d645d8d21.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50121179' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50121179' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mercor.com/research/labor-index/benchmark-map/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>90% of US working hours are spent on tasks not covered well by AI benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国90%工作时间花在AI基准覆盖不足的任务上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 557</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50121179'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings from Mercor&#x27;s labor-index benchmark map show that about 90% of US working hours involve tasks not well captured by existing AI benchmarks. This misalignment means AI metrics may overstate automation gains and underrepresent where humans still outpace automated tools. The result matters for product teams planning automation and policymakers evaluating AI impact on the workforce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercor 的劳动指数显示，美国约90%的工作时间涉及的任务并未被现有 AI 基准很好覆盖。这一错位可能导致对自动化收益的高估，并低估人类在关键工作中的优势。对需要部署自动化的团队和评估 AI 对劳动力影响的决策者来说，这一洞察意义重大。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mercor.com/mercor-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.mercor.com/mercor-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50060694' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50060694' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex CLI installs a persistent, self-updating background daemon by default</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex CLI 默认安装一个持久且自更新的后台守护进程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50060694'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence. Critics warn this can create a form of &#x27;auto-update&#x27; risk and complicate auditing. This change could influence how developers package CLIs and how users trust automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。有人担心这类自动更新会带来可追溯性和审计难题。这一变动可能影响开发者在打包 CLI 时的做法以及用户对自动化工具的信任。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9b0a6027cc5e4c770ea04e498aa39431b1d931bd069147955e5c17c622ddee4a/openai/codex/issues/48195' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9b0a6027cc5e4c770ea04e498aa39431b1d931bd069147955e5c17c622ddee4a/openai/codex/issues/48195' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50040547' data-tags='DevOps,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50040547' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.yandex.cloud/en/incidents/2136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>3rd Yandex Cloud Data Center Was Hit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yandex 云数据中心再度受击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 580</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 707</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50040547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A third Yandex Cloud data center was hit, highlighting ongoing fragility in regional cloud deployments. The incident tests customers&#x27; expectations for availability and the provider&#x27;s incident response and redundancy across sites. Implication: multi-region resilience and fast failover are essential for IaaS users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。事件考验客户对可用性和云厂商跨区域冗余/应急响应的期望。意义在于强调对 IaaS 用户而言，多区域容错和快速故障转移至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.yandexcloud.net/cloud-www-assets/statusboard/share/main.en.png' data-full='https://storage.yandexcloud.net/cloud-www-assets/statusboard/share/main.en.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50089921' data-tags='Data,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50089921' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dataforprogress.org/blog/2026/9/29/americans-social-activity-has-steeply-declined-in-the-past-30-years' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Americans&#x27; social activity has steeply declined in the past 30 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过去三十年美国人的社交活动显著下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 576</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 567</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50089921'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital. The trend raises questions about mental health, community resilience, and how digital life shapes social behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。此趋势引发对心理健康、社区韧性以及数字生活如何塑造社交行为的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5aa9be92f8370a24714de593/t/6aba933e8ee596665cfd1976/1790612286756/Copy+of+Poll_+Personally+Knowing+a+Transgender+Person+Correlates+With+Increased+Sympathy+for+Transgender+People+and+Support+for+LGBTQ%2B+Issues.png?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5aa9be92f8370a24714de593/t/6aba933e8ee596665cfd1976/1790612286756/Copy+of+Poll_+Personally+Knowing+a+Transgender+Person+Correlates+With+Increased+Sympathy+for+Transgender+People+and+Support+for+LGBTQ%2B+Issues.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50077020' data-tags='Show HN,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50077020' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://guitoro.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Guitoro, a virtual guitar you strum with your keyboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Guitoro——用键盘弹拨的虚拟吉他</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50077020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 564</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50077020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50077020'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Guitoro is a web-based virtual guitar you can strum using keyboard input, demonstrating playful exploration of alternative interaction models for making music online. It highlights how lightweight, browser-based instruments can lower barriers to musical experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Guitoro 是一个通过键盘输入来弹拨的网页虚拟吉他，展示了在浏览器中尝试另一种互动方式进行音乐创作的可能性。该项目证明轻量级的浏览器乐器也能降低音乐探索的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://guitoro.com/brand/guitoro-social.png?v=gold-cutout-medium' data-full='https://guitoro.com/brand/guitoro-social.png?v=gold-cutout-medium' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50045946' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50045946' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nyc.gov/main/click-to-cancel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NYC&#x27;s &quot;Click to Cancel&quot; rule is now in effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市的“点选取消”规则现已生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 550</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 276</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50045946'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions. The policy pushes platforms to simplify cancellations and clarify terms, potentially reshaping consumer rights online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市的点选取消规则正式生效，要求订阅服务提供商提供更便捷的取消路径和更清晰的条款。这一规定可能改变数字服务的用户体验和合规成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50093180' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50093180' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/news/2026/10/13/suicide-rates-highest-ever-among-middle-aged-men/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Suicide rates highest ever among middle-aged men</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中年男性自杀率创历史新高</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50093180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50093180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 771</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50093180'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public health data show suicide rates among middle-aged men reach record highs, prompting discussions on mental health access, economic stressors, and targeted intervention strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公共卫生数据显示中年男性自杀率达到历史新高，引发关于心理健康服务获取、经济压力源以及针对性干预策略的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/content/dam/briefs/2026/10/13/TELEMMGLPICT000507183811_17919022221720_trans_NvBQzQNjv4BqrnykcIhNBTQGIhNzmTaT-cLZq-j_VIcNfiYtpwBx7zI.jpeg?impolicy=OG-Standard' data-full='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/content/dam/briefs/2026/10/13/TELEMMGLPICT000507183811_17919022221720_trans_NvBQzQNjv4BqrnykcIhNBTQGIhNzmTaT-cLZq-j_VIcNfiYtpwBx7zI.jpeg?impolicy=OG-Standard' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50043026' data-tags='Science,Education,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50043026' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/slides/math-2-0-caltech-2026.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terence Tao: Math 2.0 [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>陶哲轩：Math 2.0 [PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50043026'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress. The slides outline practical implications for proof, education, and research infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。讲义暗示对证明方法、教育和研究基础设施的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50126253' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50126253' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-03266-x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bleached corals thrive within days when touching healthy neighbour</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>接触健康邻居后，白化珊瑚数日内快速恢复</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 540</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50126253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals. The interaction likely involves beneficial chemical signals or metabolite exchange that jump-starts recovery and resilience. The finding highlights potential reef restoration strategies that leverage proximity or graft-like approaches to accelerate recovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。这一现象可能来自健康珊瑚释放的有益信号分子或代谢物的转移，帮助受损珊瑚重新获得活性。此发现为珊瑚礁修复提供了新的思路，如通过近邻效应或嫁接等方法加速康复。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-03266-x/d41586-026-03266-x_53851622.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-03266-x/d41586-026-03266-x_53851622.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50132193' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50132193' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pele.run/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pele – Mermaid diagrams as themeable SVG</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pele：主题化的 Mermaid 图表 SVG</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50132193'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pele offers themeable SVG support for Mermaid diagrams, allowing consistent styling across charts without losing their semantic structure. This makes it easier to embed readable, branded diagrams in docs and apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pele 为 Mermaid 图表提供可主题化的 SVG 输出，便于在文档和应用中保持风格一致且不牺牲图表语义。通过自定义主题，可以实现品牌化且易读的图表呈现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pele.run/social.png' data-full='https://pele.run/social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50136881' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50136881' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://meidasnews.com/news/zuckerbergs-meta-takes-down-no-kings-instagram-during-massive-protest-day' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zuckerberg&#x27;s Meta Takes Down No Kings Instagram During Protest Day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格的Meta在抗议日下线No Kings的Instagram账号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 533</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50136881'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta removed the Instagram account linked to the No Kings group on a day of mass protests, showcasing how platform moderation intersects with civil-society voices during mobilizations. The move fuels debate over censorship, safety policies, and what counts as legitimate content during contentious events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta在大规模抗议日移除了与No Kings相关的Instagram账户，凸显社交平台在动员时期对内容的把控与公民声音之间的矛盾。此举引发关于审查、安全策略以及在有争议事件中何种内容应被允许的辩论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50090246' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50090246' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prospect.org/2026/10/14/ice-planning-nightmare-domestic-spy-program-ero-one/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICE Planning $100M &#x27;Nightmare&#x27; Domestic Spy Program</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE计划1亿美元的Nightmare国内间谍计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 531</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090246'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICE plans a $100 million domestic surveillance program codenamed Nightmare, signaling a bold expansion of law-enforcement data-gathering at home. The initiative raises civil-liberties concerns and could accelerate how agencies aggregate and analyze personal data across agencies. Its life cycle will hinge on oversight and privacy safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICE 计划1亿美元的Nightmare 国内监控项目，标志着国内执法数据收集能力的重大扩张。此举引发公民自由方面的担忧，或加速多机构之间个人数据的聚合与分析。项目的成败将取决于问责与隐私保护的平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/prospect.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Wimbish-ERO-ONE-101426.jpg?fit=2145%2C1398&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/prospect.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Wimbish-ERO-ONE-101426.jpg?fit=2145%2C1398&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50138531' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50138531' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/guides/search/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek search and fetch – $0.75 per 1000 successful requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 搜索与获取服务：每千次请求 0.75 美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 528</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50138531'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek launches a &#x27;search and fetch&#x27; API priced at $0.75 per 1000 successful requests. This pricing positions the service as a cost-efficient option for building search-heavy apps and data pipelines, potentially enabling more frequent queries at scale. It could shake up budget planning for developers who rely on external search endpoints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 推出名为 &#x27;search and fetch&#x27; 的 API，按每成功请求 1000 次收取 0.75 美元。该定价面向需要大量检索的应用，提供更具成本效益的查询方案，可能改变开发者在数据检索与管线中的预算和架构选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' data-full='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50129121' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrouter.ai/deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek: DeepSeek v4.1 Flash 1017</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek v4.1 Flash 1017 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 514</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50129121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek releases v4.1 Flash 1017, a new build of its on-device AI/search toolkit. The update focuses on performance refinements and stability for local inference workloads, helping lighter devices run faster queries with lower latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 发布 v4.1 Flash 1017，针对本地搜索/AI 工具链的新版本。此次更新提升性能与稳定性，帮助轻量设备在本地推理场景下实现更快的查询响应。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50090349' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50090349' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.androidauthority.com/google-auto-phone-number-verification-android-3723117/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google is silently linking your phone number across all signed-in accounts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌悄悄将你的电话号码链接到所有已登录账户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 513</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090349'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s automatic phone-number verification appears to be linking a number across all accounts a user is signed into, potentially collapsing account separation and exposing contact information across profiles. This behavior raises privacy concerns for people who use different accounts for work, personal use, or anonymity and expect phone numbers to remain account-specific.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌的自动电话号码验证似乎把同一号码关联到用户所有已登录的账户上，可能破坏账户之间的隔离并让联系信息在多个资料间可见。这种做法对同时使用工作、个人或匿名账户的人来说构成隐私隐患，因为他们通常期望电话号码保持与特定账户绑定。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50103000' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50103000' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/oct/15/israeli-firm-digital-ads-enabling-installation-of-zero-click-spyware-researchers-say' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Online ads weaponised to deliver ‘zero-click’ spyware, researchers say</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究称 在线广告被武器化用于投放零点击间谍软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50103000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 511</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50103000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50103000'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reveal that online ads can deliver spyware without any user clicks by exploiting zero-click flaws. The technique leverages the ad ecosystem to silently compromise devices, dramatically increasing reach and stealth. The finding underscores the need for stronger vetting of ad networks and better defenses in browsers and apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员披露，某些在线广告可利用零点击漏洞在用户不点击的情况下安装间谍软件。这种方法通过广告生态系统实现隐蔽渗透，显著扩大攻击覆盖面与隐蔽性。此发现凸显加强广告网络安全、以及浏览器和应用防护的紧迫性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/3035c44f5ae09be7cb6b5806453ff11e9db3e89f/401_440_2305_1844/master/2305.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=934fb19a9352c330577175c1f90d49df' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/3035c44f5ae09be7cb6b5806453ff11e9db3e89f/401_440_2305_1844/master/2305.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=934fb19a9352c330577175c1f90d49df' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50083130' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50083130' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/android/ndk/issues/2260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AOSP Source Releases Are So Late Developers Disassemble Libraries to Report Bugs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AOSP 源码发布太晚，开发者解构库以报告漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50083130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 496</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50083130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50083130'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Late AOSP source releases are spurring developers to reverse-engineer libraries to report bugs, underscoring the tension between open-source transparency and practical debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AOSP 源码发布延迟，迫使开发者通过反汇编库来定位并报告漏洞，凸显开源透明度与实际调试之间的矛盾与挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/faa19897607d84aad613f66ea165a6363ff4d54160d84554507628d175f561b1/android/ndk/issues/2260' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/faa19897607d84aad613f66ea165a6363ff4d54160d84554507628d175f561b1/android/ndk/issues/2260' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50101813' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50101813' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizenlab.ca/research/how-discover-tech-turned-targeted-ads-into-a-pipeline-for-spyware/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AdFection: Targeted ads turned into a pipeline for zero-click mercenary spyware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AdFection：定向广告如何成为零点击就可投放雇佣型间谍软件的管道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50101813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 487</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50101813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 640</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50101813'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AdFection reveals how targeted advertising ecosystems can be repurposed as a pipeline for deploying mercenary spyware with zero-click delivery. The investigation highlights privacy and security risks in adtech and how attackers leverage data and real-time bidding to enable covert surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AdFection 揭示定向广告生态如何被逆向利用，成为零点击就能投放雇佣型间谍软件的渠道。调查凸显广告技术中的隐私与安全风险，以及攻击者如何利用数据与实时竞价实现隐蔽监控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://citizenlab.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/discover-report_updated-01-e1792025505984.png' data-full='https://citizenlab.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/discover-report_updated-01-e1792025505984.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122951' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122951' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.dev/blog/solving-code-review-at-anthropic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Claude Code team does code review</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 团队如何进行代码审查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 465</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 466</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122951'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Claude Code team describes a hybrid code-review workflow that combines automated checks from Claude with human reviewers: the model surfaces likely issues and refactors, humans validate correctness and tests. The process speeds reviews and catches routine problems, but relies on human oversight to avoid model errors and ensure design intent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 团队介绍了一种结合 Claude 自动检查与人工审查的混合代码评审流程：模型提出潜在问题与重构建议，人工负责验证正确性与测试。该流程能加快审查并发现常见问题，但仍依赖人工把关以避免模型失误并保障设计意图。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claude.dev/blog/solving-code-review-at-anthropic/og.png' data-full='https://claude.dev/blog/solving-code-review-at-anthropic/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50111852' data-tags='AI,Legal,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50111852' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/metas-new-commercial-terms-will-allow-the-scraping-of-news-sites-legal-experts-say/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta’s new commercial terms will allow the scraping of news sites: experts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 新的商业条款将允许抓取新闻网站：专家观点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50111852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50111852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50111852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s updated commercial terms open a path for scraping news sites to train models and power AI capabilities. Legal experts weigh the policy&#x27;s potential to seed large-scale data corpora against copyright and licensing concerns, and publishers&#x27; revenue models. The change could recalibrate who pays for access and how data ecosystems evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 更新的商业条款可能为新闻网站数据抓取用于训练模型铺平道路。专家认为这或将推动大规模数据集的出现，同时引发版权、许可与出版商收入方面的担忧，或迫使数据访问和定价模式重估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/Meta-stock-image-700x467.jpg' data-full='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/Meta-stock-image-700x467.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50069329' data-tags='Legal,Finance,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50069329' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/features/2026-10-13/cftc-staff-told-to-retract-analysis-of-suspicious-oil-trades-on-iran' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CFTC Staff Were Told to Retract Analysis of Suspicious Oil Trades</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CFTC 员工被要求撤回对可疑石油交易分析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50069329'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Regulatory staff were instructed to retract analysis of suspicious oil trades, suggesting external pressure influencing enforcement work. The episode highlights concerns about independence and transparency in market surveillance and regulatory credibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，美国商品期货交易委员会的工作人员被指示撤回对可疑石油交易的分析，显示监管工作可能受到外部压力的影响。此事引发对执法独立性、透明度与市场监管可信度的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50072913' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50072913' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.thunderbird.net/2026/10/thunderbird-for-ios-a-first-look-at-the-native-iphone-email-app-in-development/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thunderbird for iOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Thunderbird iOS 版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 453</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50072913'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thunderbird is sharing a first look at a native iOS email app in development, signaling a major milestone for the open-source project. The iOS app aims to deliver a privacy-conscious, performant experience in the Apple ecosystem while staying aligned with Thunderbird&#x27;s cross-platform ethos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Thunderbird 正在展示其原生 iOS 邮件应用的初步样子，标志着这家开源项目的一大里程碑。iOS 版本计划在 Apple 生态中提供注重隐私、性能出色的邮件体验，同时坚持 Thunderbird 的跨平台理念。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.thunderbird.net/files/2026/10/Cover2-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://blog.thunderbird.net/files/2026/10/Cover2-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50048638' data-tags='Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50048638' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/oct/06/google-datacentres-finland-temporary-halt-environmental-impact-assessment' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google told to halt work on datacentres in Finland over environmental concerns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌被要求暂停芬兰数据中心建设，因环境担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 451</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50048638'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Regulators ordered Google to halt construction of planned Finland datacenters pending an environmental impact assessment. The pause highlights growing scrutiny of data-center footprints in energy-rich regions and could affect Google&#x27;s regional cloud expansion timeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>监管机构要求谷歌暂停芬兰数据中心建设，直至完成环境影响评估。这一暂停凸显对数据中心环境足迹日益严格的审查，可能影响谷歌在该区域的云基础设施扩张计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/5a426a4caa58ecb7baabf8e19319bb4ee7c46a53/669_0_3162_2531/master/3162.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=dcad87901dee69ab635a10fa637a6bc3' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/5a426a4caa58ecb7baabf8e19319bb4ee7c46a53/669_0_3162_2531/master/3162.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=dcad87901dee69ab635a10fa637a6bc3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50094694' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sunshowers.io/posts/anthropic-mononoke/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s Scion is heavily inspired by Mononoke</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的 Scion 很大程度上受 Mononoke 启发</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50094694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50094694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50094694'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s Scion borrows heavily from ideas associated with Mononoke, sharpening focus on long-horizon planning and memory-inspired components. The comparison helps frame where Scion might excel and where safety considerations could evolve as models grow more capable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的 Scion 在设计上大量借鉴了与 Mononoke 相关的理念，强调长时规划与记忆驱动的组件。此对比有助于理解 Scion 可能的强项，以及在模型能力提升时安全性考量方面的演变。</p>
