<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50069971' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eg2.haku.sh/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We turned EmbeddingGemma 2 into a speech-to-text model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 已改造成语音转文本模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50069971'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EmbeddingGemma 2 is repurposed as a speech-to-text model, demonstrating that a robust embedding backbone can be adapted for ASR without building from scratch. The approach highlights the flexibility of embedding-based architectures and could offer cost-effective, multilingual transcription options in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EmbeddingGemma 2 被改造为语音转文本模型，展示了以强大嵌入骨干为基础也能用于语音识别，而无需从头构建声学模型。该方法凸显了嵌入式架构的灵活性，并有望带来更具成本效益的多语言转写方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eg2.haku.sh/speed-table.png' data-full='https://eg2.haku.sh/speed-table.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereallo.dev/blog/someone-on-torrent-sites-kept-fucking-with-my-sonarr-setup' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone on Torrent Sites Kept Fucking with My Sonarr</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有人在 torrent 网站上持续干扰我的 Sonarr</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 418</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50108872'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author details ongoing interference with their Sonarr setup by actors on torrent sites, revealing risk to self-hosted media stacks. The post offers practical steps for hardening home-media setups and defending against misconfigurations and tampering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者讲述自己的 Sonarr 设置持续被 torrent 网站上的人干扰，揭示自托管媒体系统可能面临的攻击风险。文章给出加强家用媒体环境的实用对策，帮助防范误配置与篡改。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目展示如何将 WiFi 7 路由器改造成支持 320 Msps 的软件定义无线电接收机，展示了用普通硬件进行高带宽无线信号采集的可行性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexwennerberg.com/blog/2026-10-13-long.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is every rationalist blog post way too long?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么每篇理性主义者博客都这么长？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50083028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50083028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 412</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50083028'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of why rationalist blogs tend to be long-winded, exploring writing patterns, audience expectations, and the trade-off between rigor and conciseness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析理性主义者博客为何普遍冗长，探讨写作风格、读者预期以及严格性与简洁性之间的权衡。提出提高表达效率的思路。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50055220' data-tags='Design,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50055220' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c620r33plgppo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Citroen revives the 2CV for an electric age</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Citroën 将 2CV 在电动时代重新诠释</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 460</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50055220'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Citroën revives the 2CV design with an electric powertrain, blending retro styling with modern EV tech. The move showcases how nostalgic models can be reimagined for sustainability, while presenting challenges in preserving iconic character and efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Citroën 将 2CV 的经典设计与电动动力系统相结合，展现了在可持续性背景下对传统车型的再造。此举既保留了怀旧风格，也带来在保持标志性特征与效率之间的平衡挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/b9db/live/c47fea70-c3f4-11f1-97a9-2d4b5e26e282.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/b9db/live/c47fea70-c3f4-11f1-97a9-2d4b5e26e282.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50109375' data-tags='Business,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50109375' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.withings.com/hc/en-us/community/posts/51887047154577-Unacceptable-Apple-Health-Sync-is-now-behind-a-99-year-paywall-for-new-accounts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Withings Users Must Pay $99/Year for Apple Health Syncing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新用户必须付年费99美元才能同步 Apple Health</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 282</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50109375'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Withings announces a $99/year fee for new users to sync data with Apple Health, triggering backlash over paywalls for interoperability. The move highlights tensions between device makers, platform APIs, and user expectations of seamless data sharing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Withings 对新用户推出 Apple Health 同步需要年费 99 美元的政策，引发关于互操作性的付费墙争议。这一举动凸显了设备厂商、平台 API 与用户对无缝数据共享期望之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://support.withings.com/hc/theming_assets/01J3K4N64TS6GRYHKG5XD2MPKX' data-full='https://support.withings.com/hc/theming_assets/01J3K4N64TS6GRYHKG5XD2MPKX' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50136536' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50136536' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/new-york-rep-announces-game-ownership-bill-that-would-require-consumer-ownership-rather-than-sale-of-licenses-would-also-require-publishers-to-keep-the-game-functional-after-support-ends' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NY announces game ownership bill requiring ownership rather than licensing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约宣布游戏所有权法案，要求消费者拥有游戏而非许可</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50136536'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York proposes a bill requiring consumer ownership of digital games rather than licenses and obligating publishers to keep titles functional after support ends. The move could reshape digital rights, DRM debates, and post-sale software stewardship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约提出的法案要求数字游戏以实际所有权形式转让给消费者，而不仅仅是许可，并要求发行商在停止支持后仍维持游戏功能。此举可能重塑数字权利、数字版权管理与售后软件维护的格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/mKeveqd9S8n3CfPhhZqGcc-970-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/mKeveqd9S8n3CfPhhZqGcc-970-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50041540' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50041540' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ircv3.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IRCv3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IRCv3 规范</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 393</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50041540'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers. It aims to make IRC more usable and interoperable in a world of feature-rich clients. The spec underpins improvements like extended joins and better identity handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。它旨在让 IRC 在功能丰富的客户端时代更易用、互操作性更强。该规范支撑了扩展加入、身份处理等改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ircv3.net/img/logo-forwhite.svg' data-full='https://ircv3.net/img/logo-forwhite.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50072994' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50072994' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ourworldindata.org/data-insights/electricity-generation-per-person-in-the-united-kingdom-has-fallen-by-a-third-from-its-early-2000s-peak' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The United Kingdom generates less electricity per person than in the 1970s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国人均发电量已低于1970年代水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 390</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 649</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50072994'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New data shows UK electricity generation per person fallen well below 1970s levels, even as total output changes with policy shifts and energy efficiency. The piece underscores how energy transitions and demand management shape the per-capita footprint and policy incentives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国人均发电量已低于1970年代水平，折射出能源结构调整、需求管理与政策激励对人均用电的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ourworldindata.org/cdn-cgi/imagedelivery/qLq-8BTgXU8yG0N6HnOy8g/14a83be2-f915-4488-06af-fb7eea080400/public' data-full='https://ourworldindata.org/cdn-cgi/imagedelivery/qLq-8BTgXU8yG0N6HnOy8g/14a83be2-f915-4488-06af-fb7eea080400/public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50132870' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50132870' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.numismaticnews.net/gold-now-top-u-s-export' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gold is now the top U.S. export</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黄金成为美国第一大出口商品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50132870'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gold shipments have overtaken oil as the top U.S. export by value, underscoring the precious metal&#x27;s central role in global trade. The shift reflects strong demand for gold as an asset class and changes in commodity flows, with potential implications for currency markets and trade balances.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>金条等黄金制品如今已成为美国最大的出口商品，出口值超过原油等传统巨头。全球对黄金的需求旺盛以及投资者对黄金的偏好改变了贸易结构，可能影响货币市场和贸易平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.numismaticnews.net/uploads/2026/05/01-09-2026-scaled.jpeg?auto=webp&amp;width=2000&amp;height=1335' data-full='https://www.numismaticnews.net/uploads/2026/05/01-09-2026-scaled.jpeg?auto=webp&amp;width=2000&amp;height=1335' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50042219' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50042219' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/10/i-paid-people-to-try-and-follow-my-readme/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I paid people to try and follow my README</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我花钱让人尝试按我的 README 操作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50042219'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author paid participants to try following his README, turning documentation into a live test. The results reveal ambiguities and blockers that real users encounter, highlighting gaps in how information is communicated. It offers practical lessons on writing clearer, testable onboarding docs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者花钱让人尝试按他的 README 做事，将文档变成一次真实测试。结果暴露出真实用户在执行步骤时遇到的歧义和阻碍，凸显文档传达中的不足。为编写更清晰、可测试的上手文档提供了可操作的经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Storm-Trooper-Chocolate-Money.jpg' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Storm-Trooper-Chocolate-Money.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50100994' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50100994' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://furifont.antfu.me/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Furifont, a Japanese font that shows furigana</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Furifont：一款能显示振り仮名的日文字体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 387</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50100994'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Furifont is a Japanese font that displays furigana (ruby readings) inline to aid pronunciation. It can simplify reading complex kanji for learners and UI text, potentially improving accessibility in apps and educational materials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Furifont 是一款在日文文本中原生显示振り仮名的字体，便于读者标注读音。对于日语学习者和需要清晰注音的应用界面来说，它可以提升可读性与无障碍性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://furifont.antfu.me/og.png' data-full='https://furifont.antfu.me/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50092730' data-tags='Security,Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50092730' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dpa-international.com/politics/urn:newsml:dpa.com:20090101:261014-930-839530/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fire at Starlink site in Bulgaria after suspected sabotage in Poland</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>波兰嫌疑破坏后，保加利亚 Starlink 场所发生火灾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50092730'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Starlink facility in Bulgaria caught fire following a suspected sabotage incident linked to a prior event in Poland. Investigators are examining connections between regional attacks on communications infrastructure and broader geopolitical risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在波兰事件相关的嫌疑破坏后，保加利亚的 Starlink 场所发生火灾，调查人员正在评估区域性通信基础设施攻击的关联与更广泛的地缘风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dpa-international.com/img/logo-dpa-international.svg' data-full='https://dpa-international.com/img/logo-dpa-international.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50054751' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50054751' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>QGIS enacts moratorium on AI contributions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>QGIS 对 AI 贡献实施暂停</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 384</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50054751'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed. The move signals open-source communities grappling with risk and quality control as AI tooling becomes more integrated. It may slow AI-assisted features in the GIS project but could set precedent for other OSS projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。此举显示开源社区在 AI 工具日益整合的环境下面临的风险与质量控制挑战，可能放慢该 GIS 项目中 AI 助力的功能部署，但也可能为其他开源项目树立先例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ecd8d0f901eee0f806d831e96afb55ccec8a03b57832f9afe745d5fb8238f56c/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ecd8d0f901eee0f806d831e96afb55ccec8a03b57832f9afe745d5fb8238f56c/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50092664' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50092664' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://startupfortune.com/banks-are-now-trying-to-offload-the-ai-data-center-debt-they-just-underwrote/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Banks are now trying to offload the AI data center debt they just underwrote</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>银行正试图处置他们刚承销的AI数据中心债务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50092664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Banks are trying to offload AI data-center debt they just underwrote, moving risk off their books. The move signals fragility in AI infrastructure financing and a potential shift toward securitization of tech debt as demand cools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>银行正试图处置他们刚承销的AI数据中心债务，将风险从自家账面转移出去。这一举动暴露出AI基础设施融资的脆弱性，以及在需求降温时科技债务可能被证券化的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://startupfortune.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/oracle-jpmorgan-man-group.jpg' data-full='https://startupfortune.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/oracle-jpmorgan-man-group.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50103630' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50103630' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/i-know-why-the-mathematicians-weep' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I know why the mathematicians weep</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我知道为什么数学家会哭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50103630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 379</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50103630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 636</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50103630'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Derek Thompson argues about the emotional and intellectual toll in mathematics, using history and anecdotes to explain why mathematicians weep. The piece links math’s beauty with its stubborn hard problems and reminds tech readers of the human side of theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德里克·汤普森以历史和趣闻讲述数学的情感与智力代价，解释为何数学家会为难题而落泪。文章将数学之美与顽固难题联系起来，提醒科技读者理论背后的人性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!oJ0i!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2f5d4086-5119-48b4-93bd-c1c7cc96fe16_1080x501.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!oJ0i!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2f5d4086-5119-48b4-93bd-c1c7cc96fe16_1080x501.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50067414' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50067414' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thehistoryblog.com/archives/77107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hittite royal archive found in Turkey</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>土耳其发现的赫梯王室档案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50067414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50067414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50067414'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists uncovered a royal archive from the Hittite civilization in Turkey, revealing tablets and inscriptions that illuminate ancient governance, diplomacy, and daily life. The find could reshape understanding of Bronze Age Anatolia and offer new data for linguistics and history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家在土耳其发现了赫梯王朝的皇家档案，出土的泥板与铭文有助于揭示当时的统治、外交与日常生活。此发现可能重塑对安纳托利亚青铜时代的认识，并为语言学和历史学提供新材料。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thehistoryblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/01/mygravatar.png' data-full='https://www.thehistoryblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/01/mygravatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50047055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theonion.com/archivehunt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So, you’d like to sell your print issues of The Onion?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50047055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of selling vintage print issues of The Onion via an Archive Hunt-like platform, highlighting the quirks of media ephemera markets. It touches on archiving culture, copyright questions, and the tension between physical artifacts and digital availability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探索通过类似 Archive Hunt 的平台出售《The Onion》的印刷旧刊，揭示媒体浮游文献市场的怪异现象。涉及档案文化、版权问题，以及实体与数字可用性之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://itsallaboutthebit.com/i-am-sorry-but-you-still-have-to-think/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m sorry, but you still have to think</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50043689'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that human thinking remains essential even as AI tools proliferate. It stresses critical reasoning, architectural planning, and ethical considerations as core skills for developers and teams.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴特勒里安圣战</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50127274'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解读《沙丘》中的巴特勒里安圣战——反对思考机器的关键历史事件，以及它与当下对人工智能自治与控制的担忧之间的关联。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122431' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122431' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cobanov/PS5LM' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Llama.cpp on a jailbroken PS5: run any GGUF model natively on the console</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在越狱 PS5 上运行 Llama.cpp：在主机本地运行任何 GGUF 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122431'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware. This demonstrates a practical path for local, low-cost model deployment on high-performance consumer hardware—at the cost of requiring a jailbroken device and dealing with memory/thermal limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区移植了 llama.cpp 到越狱的 PS5，允许在主机硬件上本地运行 GGUF 格式的模型。该项目展示了用高性能消费级设备实现本地低成本推理的可行性，但需要越狱设备并需应对内存与散热等限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a750316244a16ee0f85ba0fdca3d9afffb0e6750c1399c5c873b2ac58b32808e/cobanov/PS5LM' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a750316244a16ee0f85ba0fdca3d9afffb0e6750c1399c5c873b2ac58b32808e/cobanov/PS5LM' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50059523' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50059523' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/ai/2026/10/ai-coding-agents-generate-more-code-but-not-more-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI coding agents generate more code, but not more software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 编码代理生成更多代码，但并未带来更多软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50059523'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI coding agents can generate more code, but this doesn&#x27;t translate into more usable software due to quality and integration hurdles. The article underscores the need for better tooling and governance in AI-assisted development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 编码代理确实能产生更多代码，但由于质量、模块化和集成方面的挑战，未能带来更多可用软件。此现象强调在 AI 辅助开发中需要更好的工具链、标准与治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-2264559974-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-2264559974-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jamesfmackenzie.com/2026/10/13/i-love-software-rendering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I love software rendering</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50117746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 356</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50117746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50117746'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A defender of software rendering argues for its simplicity, correctness, and portability compared to GPU-centric pipelines, sharing examples and lessons from building software renderers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者赞赏软件渲染的简洁性、正确性与可移植性，强调其相较于以 GPU 为中心的渲染管线的优势，并分享构建软件渲染器的经验与要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50137620' data-tags='Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50137620' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.readtheprofile.com/p/elizabeth-holmes-documentary-you-can-see-everything' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Elizabeth Holmes documentary “You Can See Everything” is deceiving</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊丽莎白·霍尔姆斯纪录片《You Can See Everything》为何会产生误导</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50137620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 353</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50137620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 495</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50137620'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Critics argue the documentary leverages selective storytelling that omits crucial context and data, painting Theranos in a more favorable light than the full record warrants. The piece invites viewers to scrutinize business documentaries for balance and truth, especially in biotech and startup narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评论者认为该纪录片采用选择性叙事，省略关键背景与数据，对 Theranos 的描绘比完整记录更有利。该文章呼吁观众对科技与创业题材的纪录片保持批判性，关注叙事平衡与真实之间的差距。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!3ARQ!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F060f86f0-a951-4a8e-8e6c-e92a3165e240_1920x1080.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!3ARQ!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F060f86f0-a951-4a8e-8e6c-e92a3165e240_1920x1080.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50105648' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50105648' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.krea.ai/krea-3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Krea 3 – An image model built for editing at scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Krea 3：面向大规模编辑的图像模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 346</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50105648'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Krea 3 is an image model designed for scalable editing, enabling batch edits, style transfers, and iterative refinements at enterprise scale. It aims to streamline creative workflows while preserving quality and consistency across large media datasets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Krea 3 是为大规模编辑而设计的图像模型，支持批量编辑、风格迁移和迭代优化，面向企业级工作流。该模型旨在加速创意流程，在大规模媒体数据集上保持质量与一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.krea.ai/krea-3-og-boxes-3.webp' data-full='https://s.krea.ai/krea-3-og-boxes-3.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50118320' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118320' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://machinelearning.apple.com/research/mlx-distributed-inference' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple ML Research: Muse Spark Open with MLX on Mac Studio</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果 ML 研究：Muse Spark 与 MLX 在 Mac Studio 开放用于分布式推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 346</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118320'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s ML Research team open-sources Muse Spark and demonstrates MLX distributed inference on Mac Studio. The move showcases how Apple aims to scale on-device and edge ML workloads, potentially improving performance and privacy for developers in the Apple ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布将 Muse Spark 开源，并在 Mac Studio 上演示 MLX 的分布式推理，显示其在本地和边缘场景的可扩展性与隐私保护潜力。此举为 Apple 生态内的开发者带来更高效的分布式推理流程和优化机会。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mlr.cdn-apple.com/media/MLX_icon_F001_TC_2048_2600_6439f2ba0f.png' data-full='https://mlr.cdn-apple.com/media/MLX_icon_F001_TC_2048_2600_6439f2ba0f.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50059204' data-tags='Education,Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50059204' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.spinellis.gr/blog/20261008/?li261008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thinking will become a hobby</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>思考将成为一种爱好</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 482</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50059204'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With AI taking over much routine reasoning, thinking might become a hobby rather than everyday work, the post argues. It encourages deliberate practice of critical thinking, problem framing, and creative exploration to stay valuable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 接管大量常规推理，思考可能从日常工作转变为一种爱好。文章鼓励有意识地练习批判性思维、问题框定与创造性探索，以保持自身价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.spinellis.gr/images/dds-logo.png' data-full='https://www.spinellis.gr/images/dds-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50070625' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50070625' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.dfb.sh/blog/eap770-root-for-two-zeros/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wanted to download an ISO, ended up writing a kernel exploit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本想下载一个 ISO，结果直接写出内核漏洞利用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50070625'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>While attempting to download an ISO, the author ends up discovering and crafting a kernel exploit, turning a routine download into hands-on vulnerability research. The tale underscores how even everyday software activities can expose low-level flaws and how careful debugging can reveal security risks worth sharing with the community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本篇讲述在尝试下载 ISO 时，作者意外发现并构建了一个内核漏洞利用，把常规下载变成了对安全问题的实战研究。该故事强调了日常软件操作也可能暴露底层缺陷，以及通过调试揭示风险的价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.dfb.sh/og.jpg' data-full='https://blog.dfb.sh/og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50105635' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50105635' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://respawnfirst.com/steam-client-is-now-faster-than-epics-new-vibe-coded-launcher/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Steam Client is now faster than Epic&#x27;s new vibe coded launcher</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steam 客户端现比 Epic 的新启动器更快</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 341</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 352</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50105635'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A recent benchmark claims Steam Client loads faster than Epic’s newly released launcher, underscoring Steam’s continued optimization edge and the importance of launcher performance for user experience. The result adds a talking point in the ongoing platform performance competition among PC storefronts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新基准称 Steam 客户端的启动速度比 Epic 新推出的启动器更快，凸显 Steam 在优化方面的持续领先，以及启动器性能对用户体验的重要性。此结果成为 PC 应用商店之间性能竞争的新话题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://respawnfirst.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/New-Steam-client-is-faster.webp' data-full='https://respawnfirst.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/New-Steam-client-is-faster.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50071310' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/10/apple-unveils-the-new-apple-tv-4k/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Apple TV 4K</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新款 Apple TV 4K</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 340</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 635</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50071310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils the new Apple TV 4K, featuring a faster chip, enhanced HDR, and a refreshed remote. The update tightens integration with Apple services and smart-home features, signaling stronger living-room performance for media, gaming, and HomeKit devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果公布新款 Apple TV 4K，配备更快的处理器、改进的 HDR 与全新遥控器。升级进一步增强与 Apple 服务及智能家居的整合，为家庭娱乐、游戏和 HomeKit 设备带来更强的表现。</p>

</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50041866' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50041866' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jsomers.net/e-coli-chemotaxis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The baffling intelligence of a single cell (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单细胞的惊人智能（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 340</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50041866'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2024 exploration of how a single cell behaves with remarkable autonomy and information processing, exemplified by E. coli chemotaxis. It challenges assumptions about intelligence and decision-making in simple organisms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 2024 年的文章探讨单细胞在没有神经系统的情况下如何展现出近乎智能的行为，以大肠杆菌趋化为例，揭示简单生物也具备复杂的信息处理能力。它挑战了关于智慧与决策的传统认知。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jsomers.net/e-coli-chemotaxis/articleImages/hero.jpg' data-full='https://jsomers.net/e-coli-chemotaxis/articleImages/hero.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50131213' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50131213' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com/p/making-chicken-transparent-using' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making chicken transparent using Dorito dust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用多力多滋粉让鸡肉变透明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50131213'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky optics experiment uses Dorito cheese powder to illustrate light-scattering effects on a chicken sample. The setup highlights how additive powders can alter visibility under certain lighting, offering a playful take on visualization of internal structure. It&#x27;s a novelty demonstration rather than a cooking technique.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个离奇的光学实验用多力多滋奶酪粉来演示鸡肉样本的光散射效应。该装置展示了添加剂粉末在特定光照下如何改变可见度，提供了对内部结构可视化的趣味演示。它不是做饭技巧，而是对光学原理的趣味展示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50073221' data-tags='Security,DevOps,AI,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50073221' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sammystraus.com/#i-posted-to-hn-and-then-my-website-was-flooded-with-ai-bot-traffic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I posted to HN, and then my website was flooded with AI bot traffic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 Hacker News 发帖后，网站突然被 AI 机器人流量淹没</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50073221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50073221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50073221'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Posting on Hacker News triggered a flood of automated AI bot traffic to the site, overwhelming servers and analytics. The incident highlights how quickly a link can attract mass bot activity and the need for basic protections like rate limiting and bot detection when you go viral. It also offers a case study in resilience for small sites facing sudden traffic bursts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Hacker News 发帖后，网站突然遭遇大量 AI 机器人流量，导致服务器和分析工具被压垮。事件凸显热门链接可能迅速吸引大规模自动访问，以及小站点在流量激增时需要的限流与机器人识别等防护措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sammystraus.com/images/ClojureDocs_before.png' data-full='https://www.sammystraus.com/images/ClojureDocs_before.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50126312' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50126312' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/dam-removals-are-bringing-fish-back-across-the-northeastern-united-states-striking-down-one-tiny-barrier-at-a-time-180989679/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dam removals are bringing fish back across the northeastern United States</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>水坝拆除让东北部地区的鱼类回归</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50126312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Removing dams across the northeastern United States is restoring river connectivity, enabling migratory fish to repopulate historic habitats. The restorations boost ecosystem health and fisheries, though results vary by river and ongoing management is needed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拆除水坝正在重新打开河道连通性，让回游性鱼类重新占据历史栖息地，提升生态与渔业收益。成效因河流而异，需持续监测与治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/b3oqmRf3D8nwqLYWEldX1tDx0JE=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1200x800:1201x801)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/16/00/1600753a-4935-46f8-b80a-87852b71eb12/header-little-dams.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/b3oqmRf3D8nwqLYWEldX1tDx0JE=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1200x800:1201x801)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/16/00/1600753a-4935-46f8-b80a-87852b71eb12/header-little-dams.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50095353' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50095353' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://typesafe.ai/blog/jev-1-14' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We’re releasing Jev 1.14.0 along with experimental features for dreamers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jev 1.14.0 发布及实验性特性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50095353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 330</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50095353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50095353'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jev 1.14.0 is released alongside experimental features aimed at early adopters. The update signals ongoing experimentation and invites feedback on new capabilities, even if some features may be unstable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jev 1.14.0 已发布，并附带面向早期尝试者的实验性功能。此次更新显示出持续探索新特性的意图，并鼓励用户提供反馈，尽管部分功能可能不稳定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/KBTvCTXoQaZeABcI7te5PTQw7Q.png?width=1574&amp;height=996' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/KBTvCTXoQaZeABcI7te5PTQw7Q.png?width=1574&amp;height=996' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50057278' data-tags='Legal,Security,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50057278' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mortoray.com/why-arent-ai-companies-being-charged-for-hacking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why aren&#x27;t AI companies being charged for hacking?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 AI 公司没有因为黑客行为被起诉？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 363</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50057278'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that charging AI companies for hacking is structurally difficult: attribution, intent, and the role of autonomous agents muddle liability, and current laws don’t map cleanly onto AI-enabled wrongdoing. Until regulators clarify what counts as negligence or complicity, prosecutions against AI firms remain rare even when their tech is involved in breaches; the piece calls for clearer accountability frameworks to avoid chilling innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，针对AI公司因黑客行为提起指控在法律上存在结构性困难：归责、意图与自动化代理的作用使责任难以界定，现行法难以直接套用到AI相关的违法行为。除非监管框架明确企业在何种情形承担共犯或疏忽责任，否则相关起诉往往难以推进。作者呼吁建立更清晰的问责规则，以避免对创新造成过度约束。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50068036' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50068036' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.character.ai/an-update-on-character-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Character.ai has initiated voluntary Chapter 11 bankruptcy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Character.ai 已自愿进入第十一章破产保护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50068036'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Character.ai has voluntarily filed for Chapter 11 bankruptcy to restructure its debt while continuing operations. The move aims to stabilize the business and preserve user access, though it raises questions about platform uptime and long-term AI feature plans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Character.ai 自愿申请第十一章破产保护以进行债务重组，同时继续运营。此举意在稳定业务、维持用户访问，但也让人关注平台的持续运行与长期 AI 功能计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ad/34/ad34888f-5b63-4d5f-a358-18cf16489b83/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/oct-blog-header.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ad/34/ad34888f-5b63-4d5f-a358-18cf16489b83/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/oct-blog-header.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50118981' data-tags='Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118981' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waymo.com/blog/2026/10/waymo-in-toronto/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Waymo in Toronto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Waymo 进军多伦多</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 444</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118981'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Waymo announced expansion into Toronto, moving its autonomous driving operations into a major Canadian city and signaling progress on cross-border deployment. Such expansions highlight work on local mapping, regulatory compliance and public safety partnerships needed to scale robo-taxi services outside their initial U.S. markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waymo 宣布在多伦多展开业务，将自动驾驶运营带入加拿大大城市，显示其在跨境部署方面取得进展。此类扩张凸显了本地地图构建、合规与公共安全合作等环节对将自动驾驶出租车服务规模化的关键作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/oT69lnzKavWqyUu7BiCQCRwO5_quNkMhToRgNG_txw-llzetBvuR1663he58eSjZiEfPPVIzga1NGPvOJSlF-PbJfiAQVSboho1oVbK3Q6phCM2t' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/oT69lnzKavWqyUu7BiCQCRwO5_quNkMhToRgNG_txw-llzetBvuR1663he58eSjZiEfPPVIzga1NGPvOJSlF-PbJfiAQVSboho1oVbK3Q6phCM2t' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50120224' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50120224' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jakearchibald.com/2026/jpeg-xl-vs-avif/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JPEG XL vs. AVIF for web use-cases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JPEG XL 与 AVIF 在网络使用场景中的对比</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50120224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 324</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50120224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50120224'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jake Archibald weighs JPEG XL and AVIF for web use-cases, comparing compression efficiency, decoding speed, and feature support. The takeaway is that choice hinges on tooling and browser support; JPEG XL often wins on quality at similar bitrates, while AVIF&#x27;s wider support keeps it relevant.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jake Archibald 对比了 JPEG XL 与 AVIF 在网页场景中的表现，关注压缩效率、解码速度和功能支持。结论是选择取决于工具链和浏览器支持，JPEG XL 在相近比特率下通常提供更高质量，但 AVIF 的更广泛支持也很重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jakearchibald.com/c/img-B1T1kkPz.jpg' data-full='https://jakearchibald.com/c/img-B1T1kkPz.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50141076' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50141076' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron/wiki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RepRapMicron</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RepRapMicron：开放源代码的微型3D打印平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141076'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision. The project offers mechanical designs, electronics, and firmware to enable precision micro-manufacturing at a hobbyist level. It showcases how open hardware can democratize advanced fabrication capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。该项目提供机械设计、电子和固件，面向爱好者层面的微型制造。它展示了开源硬件如何让高端加工能力走向大众。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/917851e063645a0adabf17ac1f2af1e51269f897b75681d18cce1c0f7e29cb1c/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/917851e063645a0adabf17ac1f2af1e51269f897b75681d18cce1c0f7e29cb1c/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50047091' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50047091' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cartographers-nk.wonyoung.so/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cartographers of North Korea (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>北朝鲜的制图师们</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50047091'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>North Korea&#x27;s cartography reveals how the state wields geography as a tool of control. The piece compares official maps with open data to highlight how borders and place names encode political messaging. It shows why map data matters for understanding information control and geopolitics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦北朝鲜的地图制作，揭示国家如何通过地图来塑造边界与地名的叙事。将官方地图与开放数据的差异进行对比，体现地理信息背后的信息控制与隐性外交。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cartographers-nk.wonyoung.so/assets/og-img.png' data-full='https://cartographers-nk.wonyoung.so/assets/og-img.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cphiri.dev/presentations/recreational-programming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recreational Programming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>娱乐性编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50079865'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recreational Programming explores programming as a leisure activity, arguing that playful, experimental approaches can drive creativity and deeper understanding of programming concepts. The talk surveys languages, tools, and practices that make coding enjoyable while still productive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《娱乐性编程》探讨将编程视为一种休闲活动，主张通过玩乐、实验性的方法来激发创造力并加深对编程概念的理解。演讲梳理了让编程更有乐趣同时保持生产力的语言、工具与实践。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.engadget.com/2288729/mapquest-will-now-show-you-where-flock-cameras-are-in-your-neighborhood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MapQuest will now show you where Flock cameras are in your neighborhood</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MapQuest将显示你所在社区周边的Flock摄像头位置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50129947'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MapQuest is adding a feature that reveals the locations of Flock cameras in the user’s vicinity, making it easier to see where automated surveillance is deployed. The change highlights privacy and civil-liberties trade-offs as communities consider how surveillance is deployed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MapQuest将新增功能，显示你所在社区周边的Flock摄像头位置，使监控部署变得更透明。此举引发关于隐私和公民自由的讨论，凸显在监控普及与公共安全之间的权衡。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>庆祝 Fortran 的 70 周年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50104726'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fortran marks its 70th anniversary, underscoring its enduring role in scientific computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fortran 已迎来 70 周年，凸显其在科学计算中的持续作用。文中提到语言的演进、社区资源，以及遗留代码在现代仿真中的基础地位。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50127979' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50127979' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://llmspeed.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Llmspeed – feel how fast a local LLM runs before you buy the hardware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Llmspeed——在购买硬件前感受本地 LLM 的运行速度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50127979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50127979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50127979'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Llmspeed lets you quickly estimate how fast a local LLM will run on your hardware, based on model and hardware presets, before you buy gear. It helps avoid over- or under-provisioning by forecasting latency and throughput for your setup.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Llmspeed 允许你在购买硬件前快速估算本地大模型在当前配置上的运行速度，基于模型与硬件的预设参数给出预测延迟与吞吐。帮助避免买错设备、更高效地选型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://llmspeed.dev/og.png' data-full='https://llmspeed.dev/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50060269' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50060269' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/ai-tool-rich-hickey-kjl8e' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;AI&#x27; Is Not a Tool by Rich Hickey</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rich Hickey 的《AI 不是工具》</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50060269'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rich Hickey challenges the common framing of AI as a mere tool, arguing that treating AI as just another utility misses deeper shifts in software design. He advocates new mental models for data, computation, and governance to build reliable, long-lasting AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rich Hickey 质疑将 AI 简化为单纯工具的观点，认为这忽视了软件设计领域的深层变革。他主张在数据、计算与治理层面建立新的思维模型，以构建更可靠、可持续的 AI 系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.licdn.com/scds/common/u/images/email/artdeco/logos/96/linkedin-bug-color.png' data-full='https://static.licdn.com/scds/common/u/images/email/artdeco/logos/96/linkedin-bug-color.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50102247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50102247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50102247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Unfenced lists land parcels that border public land, offering buyers predictable access and potential development or conservation opportunities. The project highlights a niche but practical domain at the intersection of real estate and public land policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：Unfenced 列出紧邻公共土地的待售地块，便于买家获得可预见的通道，潜在的开发或保护机会。该项目揭示了房地产与公共土地政策交汇处的一个小众但实用的领域。</p>
