<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50063365' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50063365' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.intel.com/content/www/us/en/products/details/discrete-gpus/data-center-gpu/d-series.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intel D270 GPU AI Accelerator–Up to 480 GB LPDDR5X</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔 D270 GPU AI 加速器：最高 480 GB LPDDR5X</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50063365'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Intel unveils the D270 GPU AI accelerator with up to 480 GB LPDDR5X memory, targeting data-center AI workloads. The large memory footprint aims to boost large-model inference and training throughput, intensifying competition in AI hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英特尔发布 D270 GPU AI 加速器，最高搭载 480 GB LPDDR5X 内存，面向数据中心的 AI 推理与训练工作负载。巨量内存有望提升大模型的吞吐量，与英伟达等厂商的竞争进一步加剧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.intel.com/etc.clientlibs/settings/wcm/designs/intel/us/en/images/resources/printlogo.png' data-full='https://www.intel.com/etc.clientlibs/settings/wcm/designs/intel/us/en/images/resources/printlogo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50052251' data-tags='Show HN,Web,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50052251' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/snuri00/ps2-web-recomp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: PS2 games statically recompiled to WebAssembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50052251'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: A project that statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games to WebAssembly, making them runnable in browsers without dynamic emulation layers. The approach demonstrates how modern toolchains can port legacy binaries with performance trade-offs and prompts discussion about the future of browser-based retro gaming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：一个项目将 PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly，使其在浏览器中无需动态模拟就能运行。这一方法展示了现代工具链在移植旧体制二进制时的潜力与性能权衡，也引发对浏览器内复古游戏未来的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/25e67cd8302ebb116400e6b57584fb3c575fa4598977add4dc414b2f4c606966/snuri00/ps2-web-recomp' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/25e67cd8302ebb116400e6b57584fb3c575fa4598977add4dc414b2f4c606966/snuri00/ps2-web-recomp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50102923' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libreboot.org/news/libreboot2610.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Libreboot 26.10 &quot;Ominous Olive&quot; released with UEFI, Chromebook support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Libreboot 26.10 Ominous Olive发布，新增UEFI与Chromebook支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50102923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50102923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50102923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Libreboot 26.10 Ominous Olive releases with UEFI and Chromebook support, expanding a free-firmware alternative for more devices. It broadens options for privacy-conscious users and tests hardware compatibility trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Libreboot 26.10 Ominous Olive 发布，新增 UEFI 与 Chromebook 支持，为更多设备提供免费固件替代方案。扩展了注重隐私用户的选择，同时测试了硬件兼容性与取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://av.vimuser.org/bootmenu.jpg' data-full='https://av.vimuser.org/bootmenu.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50090369' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50090369' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://go.dev/blog/http-connection-reuse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Better HTTP/1 connection reuse in Go 1.27</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 1.27 改进了 HTTP/1 连接复用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090369'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go 1.27 introduces improvements to HTTP/1 connection reuse that reduce connection churn and improve client throughput, especially for high-concurrency workloads. The changes tighten reuse heuristics and pooling behavior so fewer TCP connections are opened and closed, which lowers latency and resource usage for services still relying on HTTP/1.x.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 1.27 在 HTTP/1 连接复用上做了优化，减少了连接频繁建立/断开的情况，从而提升高并发场景下的吞吐与延迟表现。通过改进复用策略和连接池管理，依赖 HTTP/1.x 的服务可以节省资源并获得更稳定的性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://go.dev/doc/gopher/runningsquare.jpg' data-full='https://go.dev/doc/gopher/runningsquare.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122112' data-tags='Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122112' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/5a310aee-f555-4266-874c-b1b4db20186d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic assigned highest junk credit rating in June</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安思博在六月被评为最高级别垃圾信用评级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122112'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic received a top-tier junk credit rating in June, signaling elevated perceived default risk from rating agencies and likely increasing its financing costs. For AI startups that rely on capital-intensive growth, such a downgrade raises questions about runway, fundraising strategy, and investor appetite for high-burn models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安思博在六月被授予最高级别的垃圾信用评级，表明评级机构认为其违约风险上升并可能推高融资成本。对于依赖大量资本扩张的 AI 初创公司来说，这种下调会影响其现金流预期、筹资策略和投资者的风险偏好。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50097369' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50097369' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.liquid.ai/blog/lfm2-5-24b-a2b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LFM2.5-24B-A2B: Built for fast on-device agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LFM2.5-24B-A2B：为设备端快速代理而打造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50097369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50097369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50097369'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An on-device large language model variant optimized for agent workloads, delivering low latency and offline capability for autonomous, privacy-conscious applications. It aims to provide fast, privacy-preserving inference without cloud dependency, unlocking new possibilities for local agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该模型专为在设备上运行的代理工作负载而设计，强调低延迟与离线能力。它面向需要隐私保护的自治应用场景，减少对云端的依赖。文中可能介绍实现优化和部署要点，帮助在普通硬件上部署大模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aypchzzf9pftwuto.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/image%20(3)-oOdUn61bDRSykk4H951SoDOHzqoOe1.png' data-full='https://aypchzzf9pftwuto.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/image%20(3)-oOdUn61bDRSykk4H951SoDOHzqoOe1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50094673' data-tags='Security,Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50094673' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/yandex-outage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yandex outage: data shows that instead of going dark, malicious bots moved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yandex 故障：数据表明恶意机器人并未熄灯，流量转移</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50094673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50094673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50094673'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare analysis shows that during the Yandex outage, malicious bots did not go quiet; they redirected traffic rather than stopping activity. The pattern underscores how coordinated bot networks adapt to outages and continue probing and attacking targets. It highlights the resilience and adaptiveness of attack traffic in real-world disruptions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>云端分析显示，Yandex 故障期间恶意机器人并未熄火，而是转移流量继续活动。此现象揭示协同机器人网络在中断中的适应性与持续发起探测/攻击的能力，凸显真实环境下攻击流量的韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M4Y140VQ20JV2JNAC18Y1A2G.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M4Y140VQ20JV2JNAC18Y1A2G.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50072286' data-tags='Web,Privacy,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50072286' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/10/12/apples-new-switch-to-safari-campaign-details-why-you-should-ditch-chrome/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s new &#x27;Switch to Safari&#x27; campaign details why you should ditch Chrome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的新“切换到 Safari”广告：为何放弃 Chrome</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 517</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50072286'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s Switch to Safari campaign argues Safari offers stronger privacy, tighter integration with Apple devices, and potential energy/performance advantages over Chrome. It signals ongoing pressure on Chrome in the browser-default space and highlights Apple&#x27;s privacy-forward positioning. For developers, it underscores the importance of WebKit compatibility and cross-browser performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果的“切换到 Safari”广告以隐私保护、与 Apple 设备的紧密整合以及潜在的能耗与性能优势为卖点，劝导用户放弃 Chrome。这一举动反映浏览器默认之争持续升温，凸显苹果在隐私方面的立场。对开发者而言，强调了 WebKit 的兼容性和跨浏览器性能的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/10/switch-to-safari.jpg?quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;resize=1200,628' data-full='https://9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/10/switch-to-safari.jpg?quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;resize=1200,628' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50061791' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50061791' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lunarwatcher.codeberg.page/til/firefox/resumable-big-downloads.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making big downloads resumable in Firefox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Firefox 中实现大文件断点续传</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50061791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50061791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50061791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes how Firefox enables resumable downloads for large files, including range requests, server support, and resilience against network interruptions. The piece notes implications for download managers and server configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Firefox 如何实现大文件的断点续传，涵盖区间请求、服务器支持与对网络中断的鲁棒性。并讨论对下载管理工具与服务器配置的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lunarwatcher.codeberg.page/assets/profile.png' data-full='https://lunarwatcher.codeberg.page/assets/profile.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inc.com/chloe-rosenberg/a-remote-employee-refused-160-webcam-photos-a-day-her-employer-fired-her/91415963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Remote Employee Refused 160 Webcam Photos a Day. Her Employer Fired Her</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一名远程员工拒绝每天拍摄160张摄像头照片，因而被解雇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50047825'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Highlights a privacy-versus-monitoring clash: a remote employee refused 160 webcam photos a day and was fired. The piece raises questions about surveillance policies, employee rights, and legal risk in distributed work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名远程员工拒绝每天拍摄160张摄像头照片，因而被解雇，引发对工作场所监控、隐私权和相关政策的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50082470'>

The page offers insight into how he blends curiosity-driven projects with approachable design and community engagement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Felix Rieseberg 的个人主页展示了他的开源项目组合与实验性作品，以及有趣的演示和实用工具。页面体现了他在好玩与实用之间的平衡，以及对社区参与的重视。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/89/e0/89e08557-681a-40be-8264-278bf46d6b25/content/images/2026/10/facebook-card.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/89/e0/89e08557-681a-40be-8264-278bf46d6b25/content/images/2026/10/facebook-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50065692' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50065692' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI models can provably hide arbitrary capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 模型可被证明隐藏任意能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50065692'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show that AI models can provably conceal arbitrary capabilities, undermining safety evaluations that rely on probing for dangerous behaviors. The result formalizes a worst‑case scenario where a model can hide malicious or sensitive behaviors unless comprehensive testing covers all prompts and contexts. The finding underscores the need for robust evaluation and red‑teaming methods beyond surface‑level checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，AI 模型在理论上可以证明性地隐藏任意能力，从而削弱基于探测的安全评估。研究给出一个最坏情形的框架：若测试覆盖的提示和情境不全，模型就可能隐藏恶意或敏感行为。此发现强调需要比表层测试更健全的评估与红队方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122034' data-tags='Business,Hardware,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122034' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.arrl.org/arrlletterissue?issue=2026-10-15#eBay-Restricts-Listings-of-Radio-Equipment_-But-Exempts-Ham-Band-Only-Gear' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>eBay Restricts Listings of Radio Equipment, but Exempts Ham-Band-Only Gear</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>eBay 限制电台设备上架，但豁免仅限业余波段设备</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122034'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>eBay tightened restrictions on listings of radio equipment—likely to address regulatory, export, and misuse concerns—but explicitly exempted gear that operates only on amateur (ham) bands. The move constrains commercial and wideband transceivers on the marketplace while preserving a channel for hobbyist-only hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>eBay 对无线电设备的上架进行了收紧，意在应对监管、出口与滥用风险，但明确豁免仅能在业余波段运行的设备。此举限制了商用与宽带对讲机在平台上的流通，同时保留了面向爱好者的硬件交易渠道。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.arrl.org/files/image/About_ARRL/ARRL_logo_with_title.jpg' data-full='https://www.arrl.org/files/image/About_ARRL/ARRL_logo_with_title.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50142573' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50142573' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://karrisaarinen.com/posts/someone-behind-the-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone behind the software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件背后的人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50142573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It argues that progress in tech depends as much on people and processes as on algorithms or architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文本聚焦于塑造软件的人员与组织因素，强调工具选择、团队激励等比代码更能决定成败。它认为科技进步在很大程度上取决于人和流程，而不仅仅是算法或架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://karrisaarinen.com/static/linear-assets/081de7ffce667bb13cb63d8b2a1d312e19d747ea1d3818af2c5cc1e5a9445e21.webp' data-full='https://karrisaarinen.com/static/linear-assets/081de7ffce667bb13cb63d8b2a1d312e19d747ea1d3818af2c5cc1e5a9445e21.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50037711' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50037711' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/secret-of-orange-cats-finally-revealed-after-60-year-search' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Secret of Orange Cats Revealed After 60-Year Search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>橙色猫的秘密在60年的追寻后揭示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50037711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50037711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50037711'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-running genetic mystery about orange cats has finally been solved: orange fur is linked to a pigment gene on the X chromosome, meaning most orange cats are male and female orange cats are rarities caused by X-inactivation or unusual genetic events. The finding clarifies how simple color genetics interact with sex chromosome biology and explains the observed distribution of orange cats in populations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于橙色猫的长期遗传谜团终被揭晓：橙色毛色由位于X染色体上的色素基因控制，因此大多为公猫，雌性橙猫罕见，通常源于X染色体失活或异常基因事件。研究揭示了颜色遗传如何与性染色体生物学相互作用，以及橙猫在人群中的分布特征。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/OrangeCatsSurprise.jpg' data-full='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/OrangeCatsSurprise.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50051530' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50051530' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://amosbbatto.wordpress.com/2026/10/05/linux-phones-alternative/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Will Linux phones become a viable alternative to the Android/iOS duopoly?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 手机会成为 Android/iOS 双头垄断的可行替代吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 338</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50051530'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece weighs hardware support, software ecosystems, and app availability as Linux phones mature. It highlights challenges like app parity and user experience but notes ongoing community efforts (e.g., postmarketOS, PinePhone) that could tip the balance if vendors and developers invest more.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章评估 Linux 手机在硬件支持、软件生态和应用生态等方面的成熟度。尽管存在应用生态不平衡和用户体验等挑战，但社区努力（如 postmarketOS、PinePhone）若获得更大投资，或许会逐步改变现状。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/amosbbatto.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/pinephoneandlibrem5-1.jpg?fit=1196%2C1200&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/amosbbatto.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/pinephoneandlibrem5-1.jpg?fit=1196%2C1200&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50036287' data-tags='Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50036287' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pixelatron.com/blog/the-making-of-super-mario-64-full-giles-goddard-interview-ngc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Making Of Super Mario 64 – full Giles Goddard interview (2001)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《超级马里奥64》制作内幕完整访谈（Giles Goddard，2001）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50036287'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This full 2001 interview with Giles Goddard reveals how Super Mario 64’s groundbreaking 3D movement and camera system were conceived, including the challenges of porting to Nintendo 64 hardware and the iterative design that bridged 2D Mario sensibilities into 3D space. It highlights the craft of early 3D game development and the engineering trade-offs that shaped a milestone in the platformer genre.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这段2001年的完整访谈揭示了《超级马里奥64》在3D移动与摄像机系统方面的开创性构思，以及在任天堂64硬件上的移植与实现所面临的挑战。通过对早期3D游戏开发过程的讲解，展现了将2D马里奥直觉转化为3D空间所需的设计与工程权衡，及其为何成为平台跳跃游戏的里程碑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://pixelatron.com/blog/media/Super-Mario-64-U-snap0035-440x330.jpg' data-full='http://pixelatron.com/blog/media/Super-Mario-64-U-snap0035-440x330.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50105777' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50105777' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bipm.org/en/cgpm-2026/resolution-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BIPM decides to stop adding leap seconds to UTC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BIPM 决定不再为 UTC 加闰秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50105777'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BIPM votes to stop adding leap seconds to UTC, signaling a move toward a smoother, continuous time scale. Civil timekeeping will diverge gradually from astronomical time, requiring systems relying on precise time to adapt to potential offsets. Expect policy, standards, and infrastructure to shift toward alternate synchronization mechanisms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BIPM 投票决定不再为 UTC 添加闰秒，标志着向连续时间尺度的转变。民用时钟与天文时间可能逐渐错位，依赖精准时间的系统需要适应新的同步方案。相关标准与基础设施将随之调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bipm.org/o/bipm-theme/images/logo.png' data-full='https://www.bipm.org/o/bipm-theme/images/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50061659' data-tags='Health,Science,Education,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50061659' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mit.edu/2026/mit-class-project-turns-into-fda-cleared-treatment-for-tremors-1002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MIT class project turns into an FDA-cleared treatment for tremors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MIT 课设变成获 FDA 批准的震颤治疗方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50061659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50061659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50061659'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An MIT class project matured into an FDA-cleared tremor treatment, exemplifying path from academia to clinical deployment. The story highlights the potential for student-led innovation to reach patients, with regulatory hurdles acting as a proving ground.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MIT 的课程项目发展成为获 FDA 批准的震颤治疗方案，展示学术研究向临床落地的潜力，以及在监管考验下的转化路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202610/MIT-Encora-Therapeutics-01-press.jpg' data-full='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202610/MIT-Encora-Therapeutics-01-press.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50112739' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50112739' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Saffsanity/sill' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50112739'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices. The project showcases a path toward seamless multi-device workflows, though early-stage and experimental. It hints at a future where a single desktop session spans the Apple ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。该项目凸显了未来可能的无缝多设备工作流，但在实际应用前还需克服稳定性与性能挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.currentaffairs.org/news/why-were-banning-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why We&#x27;re Banning AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么要禁止 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50108131'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative essay arguing that AI development poses societal risks that justify strict or even outright bans in certain contexts, and that policy, ethics, and safety concerns deserve stronger governance. The piece urges policymakers to act now to mitigate risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章主张 AI 的潜在危害足以让人推动禁令或在关键领域实施严格限制，呼吁政策制定者尽快采取措施来降低风险。作者强调伦理、安全与社会影响应成为治理 AI 的核心考量。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sound (2022) is a work that explores audio as a structural and aesthetic medium, examining how soundscapes shape perception and narrative. It invites listeners to consider listening as active design rather than passive backdrop.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Sound（2022）》是一项以声音为结构与美学媒介的作品，探讨声景如何影响感知与叙事。它鼓励听众把聆听视为一种有意图的设计，而非被动的背景。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores an unconventional syntax that lets you call functions without parentheses, challenging conventional readability and parser expectations. The post surveys several language- or DSL-inspired approaches, with pros and cons for ergonomics and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文展示一种无需括号也能调用函数的语法变体，挑战常规的可读性与解析器预期。通过若干语言或域特定语言的例子，讨论这类写法的优缺点，以及对工具链和开发体验的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grace Hopper Celebration 取消</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50080007'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Grace Hopper Celebration, a flagship conference for women in tech, has been canceled, affecting attendees, speakers, and scholarships. The cancellation highlights ongoing logistical and planning challenges for large-scale tech events and may push organizers toward hybrid formats and renewed DEI commitments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为女性科技领域的旗舰大会，Grace Hopper Celebration 被宣布取消，影响参会者、演讲者及奖学金持有者。该取消揭示大型科技活动在筹办上的持续挑战，或促使主办方转向混合形式并加强多元公正议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://anitab.org/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcdn.sanity.io%2Fimages%2F5izrne2g%2Fproduction%2F0b4532db4c07abf4058876cdbac4bbe974d79460-313x86.png&amp;w=384&amp;q=75' data-full='https://anitab.org/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcdn.sanity.io%2Fimages%2F5izrne2g%2Fproduction%2F0b4532db4c07abf4058876cdbac4bbe974d79460-313x86.png&amp;w=384&amp;q=75' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sunshowers.io/posts/anthropic-mononoke-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>More on Anthropic&#x27;s Scion ripping off Mononoke</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 Anthropic 的 Scion 抄袭 Mononoke 的更多信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A detailed look at allegations that Anthropic&#x27;s Scion AI model copies or closely borrows from Mononoke, prompting scrutiny of training data provenance and model design practices. The discussion highlights IP risk in AI tooling and the need for better transparency and licensing in competitor models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Anthropic 的 Scion 与 Mononoke 之间相似性的进一步分析，引发对训练数据来源和模型设计的监管关注。该讨论凸显了 AI 工具领域的知识产权风险，以及在对手模型中的透明度和许可问题的紧迫性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://snoutcover.com/one-year' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I designed a nose guard for my dog and gave it away. Now 500 dogs wear one</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我为我的狗设计了鼻罩并免费公开设计，如今已有约500只狗佩戴</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50105344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50105344'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A designer details releasing a dog nose guard for free, triggering rapid adoption with about 500 dogs fitted. The initiative demonstrates how open hardware and sharing designs can scale welfare tech without traditional manufacturing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>设计者把狗鼻护具的设计免费公开，结果约有500只狗佩戴。该案例体现了开源硬件和免费分享如何在无需传统商业模式的前提下放大实际影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50104299' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50104299' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.gnome.org/uraeus/2026/10/15/aiandreverseengineerhardware/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using AI to reverse engineer hardware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 进行硬件逆向工程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50104299'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A GNOME developer discusses how AI tools can assist reverse engineering hardware, from parsing PCB layouts to inferring hidden circuitry or vulnerabilities. The piece considers the implications for hardware security, IP protection, and forensic analysis, while noting current limitations like partial visibility, data quality, and the risk of overfitting. It argues that AI could become a doubles-edged tool—unlocking insight but raising ethical and legal questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GNOME 的开发者讨论如何用 AI 帮助对硬件进行逆向分析，从解析电路板布局到推断隐藏电路和潜在漏洞。文章强调对硬件安全、知识产权与取证工作的影响，同时指出可见性、数据质量和过拟合等局限性。AI 具备推进逆向工作的潜力，但也带来伦理与法律层面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogs.gnome.org/uraeus/files/2026/10/drivercompare.png' data-full='https://blogs.gnome.org/uraeus/files/2026/10/drivercompare.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50104654' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50104654' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.simonsfoundation.org/news/simons-foundation-launches-15m-effort-to-help-mathematicians-navigate-ai-era/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simons Foundation launches $15M effort to help mathematicians navigate AI era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西蒙斯基金会启动 1500 万美元计划，助力数学家应对 AI 时代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50104654'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Simons Foundation unveils a $15 million program to help mathematicians adapt to AI&#x27;s influence, including research on AI-assisted proofs, ethics, and new tools. The effort aims to prepare the mathematics community for AI-enabled workflows and collaboration with computer science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西蒙斯基金会推出 1500 万美元项目，帮助数学家应对人工智能带来的影响，覆盖 AI 辅助证明、伦理与新工具等研究。此举旨在为数学界与计算机科学的协作与 AI 驱动的工作流程做好准备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.simonsfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/SEO_SF-OpenAi_c.Lucy-Reading-Ikkanda-Simons-Foundation.jpg' data-full='https://www.simonsfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/SEO_SF-OpenAi_c.Lucy-Reading-Ikkanda-Simons-Foundation.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50106676' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50106676' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/1011956/discover-tech-zero-click-ads-spyware' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Israeli firm is sending zero-click spyware through online ads</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一家以色列公司通过在线广告投放零点击间谍软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50106676'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigations reveal an Israeli firm is distributing zero-click spyware via online ads, enabling covert infections without user interaction. The approach exploits the ad ecosystem to bypass clicks and detection, raising concerns about ad networks and cross-channel security. The findings stress the need for stricter ad vetting and better end-user protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查显示，一家以色列公司通过在线广告投放零点击间谍软件，能够在用户无需互动的情况下进行隐蔽感染。这一做法利用广告生态系统绕过点击与检测，提升对广告网络和跨渠道安全的担忧。研究强调需要更严格的广告审核与提升终端用户保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/23318436/akrales_220309_4977_0258.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/23318436/akrales_220309_4977_0258.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50051573' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50051573' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lalinsky.com/2026/10/11/type-safe-html-templating-in-zig.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Type-safe HTML templating in Zig</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Zig 中实现类型安全的 HTML 模板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50051573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A type-safe HTML templating system is built in Zig using its compile-time features, providing strong guarantees against HTML injection and type errors. The approach demonstrates how to implement templating that respects Zig&#x27;s safety while generating dynamic HTML, useful for embedded or systems projects that need UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Zig 中实现了一套类型安全的 HTML 模板系统，利用编译时特性提供对 HTML 注入与类型错误的强保证。该方法展示如何在保持 Zig 安全性的同时生成动态 HTML，特别适合需要在嵌入式/系统项目中处理 UI 的场景。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50070593' data-tags='Hardware,Security,DevOps,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50070593' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://machinelearning.apple.com/research/distributed-gpu-cloud' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Dynamic and Distributed Virtual GPU Cloud Built on Idle Apple Silicon Devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于闲置 Apple Silicon 设备的动态分布式虚拟 GPU 云</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50070593'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe a dynamic, distributed virtual GPU cloud that pools idle Apple Silicon devices to provide scalable GPU resources on demand. The concept could reduce costs and boost energy efficiency, but introduces challenges around security, multi-tenant isolation, and large-scale orchestration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者提出一个动态分布式的虚拟 GPU 云，将闲置的 Apple Silicon 设备聚合以按需提供可扩展的 GPU 资源。该思路或许降低成本、提升能效，但在安全、多租户隔离和大规模编排方面也带来挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mlr.cdn-apple.com/media/Home_1200x630_48225d82e9.png' data-full='https://mlr.cdn-apple.com/media/Home_1200x630_48225d82e9.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50124251' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50124251' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/home-assistant/home-assistant.io/issues/49314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I never authorized an integration that scans my secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我从未授权的集成在扫描我的秘密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50124251'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent. The incident highlights privacy risks in extensible platforms and the need for explicit consent and auditable secrets handling. It underscores the ongoing tension between platform openness and user data protection in open-source ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。此事暴露了可扩展平台中的隐私风险，也强调需要对 secrets 的处理进行显式同意和可审计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9977c9b9ba61ac411f0c19dc08b23b5e3b04db4e846964b8f8dc42142f1e1f70/home-assistant/home-assistant.io/issues/49314' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9977c9b9ba61ac411f0c19dc08b23b5e3b04db4e846964b8f8dc42142f1e1f70/home-assistant/home-assistant.io/issues/49314' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50065932' data-tags='AI,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50065932' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.argmin.net/p/faster-fourier-transforms' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A look at the math that OpenAI claims computes faster Fourier transforms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>看 OpenAI 声称加速傅里叶变换的数学原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50065932'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI reportedly has a mathematical approach that can compute Fourier transforms faster than conventional FFTs. The idea could reduce compute for large-scale AI tasks and signal-processing workloads, but its value will depend on numerical stability and practical implementation. If proven robust, it could influence future libraries and hardware optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 提出一种据称能更快计算傅里叶变换的数学方法，潜在地降低大规模 AI 与信号处理任务的计算成本。效果取决于数值稳定性与实际实现的可行性。若证实稳健，可能推动相关库和硬件优化的改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!1mN7!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5f0c6088-ce61-4802-ac3f-c8bc4060a05a_840x599.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!1mN7!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5f0c6088-ce61-4802-ac3f-c8bc4060a05a_840x599.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50051583' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50051583' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/10/07/anthropic-will-be-most-ridiculous-ipo-of-year-analyst-says.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Research Firm Calls Anthropic&#x27;s IPO &#x27;Unprecedented Test of Investor Gullibity&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究机构称 Anthropic 的 IPO 是对投资者轻信的前所未有考验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50051583'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyst commentary frames Anthropic&#x27;s IPO as a stress test for investors&#x27; willingness to back high-profile AI ventures, with valuation and hype concerns. The piece signals scrutiny around AI stock flow and the risk appetite in the hot AI sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析师将 Anthropic 的 IPO 视为对投资者愿意 backing 高知名度 AI 公司的“前所未有”的考验，对估值与舆论热度存在担忧。文章反映出对 AI 股市热潮中的风险偏好与投资者谨慎程度的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108363256-1789499134411-gettyimages-2294938529-2026_sep_14_dreamforce_1602.jpeg?v=1789499168&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108363256-1789499134411-gettyimages-2294938529-2026_sep_14_dreamforce_1602.jpeg?v=1789499168&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
