<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50087328' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50087328' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.admin.ch/en/newnsb/NMn5s24l8NjQ' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Switzerland proposes banning profiling-based ads to minors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士拟禁止针对未成年人的基于画像的广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50087328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50087328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50087328'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Switzerland proposes restricting or banning profiling-based ads targeting minors, part of a broader privacy and child-protection push. The move would force advertisers and ad-tech to adapt to stricter data usage rules and could influence regional standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士提议禁止对未成年人进行基于用户画像的定向广告，属于更广泛的隐私与儿童保护举措的一部分。这将迫使广告商和广告技术公司调整数据使用策略，并可能影响区域性标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.admin.ch/images/swiss-logo-flag.svg' data-full='https://www.admin.ch/images/swiss-logo-flag.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50061278' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50061278' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openfatigue.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenFatigue™ – Open is the new closed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenFatigue™——开放才是新封闭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50061278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 239</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50061278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50061278'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenFatigue presents an open-first stance with a collection of tools and policies designed to counteract vendor lock-in. The project frames openness as a pathway to transparency, collaboration, and faster iteration in AI tooling and deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenFatigue 提出以开放为先的做法，提供一套工具和政策以对抗厂商锁定。该项目将开放视为提高透明度、协作与AI 工具迭代速度的途径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openfatigue.ai/favicon.svg' data-full='https://openfatigue.ai/favicon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50137950' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50137950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sphinx-six-theta.vercel.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Sphinx – A hook that quizzes you on your AI&#x27;s code before shipping</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Sphinx—上线前用钩子测试 AI 的代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50137950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50137950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50137950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sphinx provides a hook that quizzes you on your AI&#x27;s code before shipping, acting as a pre-release checkpoint to catch mistakes or unsafe behavior in generated code. This approach pushes validation earlier in the ML lifecycle and reduces post-release surprises. It highlights a practical path for hardening AI-assisted development through early, automated code checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sphinx 提供一个在上线前对 AI 生成代码进行自测的钩子，作为发布前的检查点以捕捉错误或潜在的危险行为。这种做法将验证环节提前到 ML 开发周期，能降低上线后出现意外的概率。展示了通过早期自动化代码检查来提升 AI 辅助开发鲁棒性的一条实际路径。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50120101' data-tags='Politics,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50120101' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ritchietorres.house.gov/posts/rep-torres-announces-power-to-the-players-act-legislation-to-ensure-gamers-own-their-games' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Rep Announces Congressional Legislation to Protect Video Game Ownership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国众议员宣布保护电子游戏所有权的国会法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50120101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 237</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50120101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50120101'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A congressman unveils legislation aimed at securing gamers&#x27; ownership rights for digital games, seeking to curb restrictive DRM terms and ensure players can access or transfer their purchases even as platforms evolve. The proposal signals renewed focus on digital ownership rights and interoperability in gaming, which could affect publishers, stores, and consumer expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该法案由 Torres 议员提出，目标是保护玩家对数字游戏的所有权，限制苛刻的 DRM 条款，并确保玩家在平台变动时仍能访问或转让购买的游戏。此举凸显对数字所有权和跨平台互操作性的关注，可能影响发行商、商店及玩家期望。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d12t4t5x3vyizu.cloudfront.net/ritchietorres.house.gov/uploads/2026/10/image-3-scaled.png' data-full='https://d12t4t5x3vyizu.cloudfront.net/ritchietorres.house.gov/uploads/2026/10/image-3-scaled.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50118688' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118688' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://starskirmish.com/bench/v0-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Gemini 4 Argon plays StarCraft</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Gemini 4 Argon 能玩星际争霸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118688'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 4 Argon demonstrates an AI agent playing StarCraft, showcasing advances in real-time strategy AI benchmarks and agent planning. This project illustrates how language-model-driven or neural agents tackle complex, dynamic environments beyond simple games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 4 Argon 展示了一个能在星际争霸中作战的 AI 代理，凸显了实时策略领域的行动规划进展。该项目证明了语言模型驱动或神经代理在复杂、动态环境中的潜在能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://starskirmish.com/assets/og.png' data-full='https://starskirmish.com/assets/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50074869' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50074869' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.googleblog.com/gemini-4-argon-for-robotics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 4 Argon for Robotics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 4 Argon 专为机器人设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50074869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50074869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50074869'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 4 Argon for Robotics represents Google&#x27;s AI stack tailored for robotics, combining perception, reasoning, and real-time control. It&#x27;s part of a broader push to bring foundation models into physical agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 4 Argon for Robotics 将 AI 能力应用于机器人，融合感知、推理与实时控制，推动基础模型在物理执行体中的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-developer-goog-blog-assets/images/argon__robotics__hero.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x600.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-developer-goog-blog-assets/images/argon__robotics__hero.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x600.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50141909' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50141909' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.apple.com/forums/verify-human/verify.html?next=/forums/thread/850550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>macOS 15.8.2 Breaks OpenGL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 15.8.2 将 OpenGL 支持破坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141909'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS 15.8.2 breaks OpenGL support, causing rendering issues for apps that still rely on legacy GL contexts. Apple has deprecated OpenGL in recent updates, pushing developers toward Metal. The change underscores the risk of relying on deprecated APIs for future OS releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 15.8.2 破坏了 OpenGL 支持，导致仍依赖旧版 GL 上下文的应用出现渲染问题。苹果已在更新中淡化 OpenGL，推崇 Metal，强调了依赖已弃用 API 的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50052638' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50052638' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonkoeck.com/writeups/google-devsite-flask-session-pickle-rce' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Found a $90k Remote Code Execution Vulnerability in Google</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 Google 找到了价值 9 万美元的远程代码执行漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50052638'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researcher details a remote code execution vulnerability in Google&#x27;s DevSite Flask session handling via pickle deserialization, with a notable $90k bug bounty awarded. The writeup highlights how unsafe deserialization can create RCE across web apps, and emphasizes the importance of strict input handling and patching.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究员披露了 Google DevSite 的 Flask 会话处理中通过 pickle 反序列化实现远程代码执行的漏洞，相关奖励达到约9万美元。该分析强调了反序列化漏洞的风险以及在 Web 应用中加强输入处理与及时修补的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://simonkoeck.com/og/google-devsite-flask-session-pickle-rce.png' data-full='https://simonkoeck.com/og/google-devsite-flask-session-pickle-rce.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50100366' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50100366' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/antirez/c64-hw-simulator' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Commodore 64 hardware simulator: break chips and pins, see the results</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Commodore 64 硬件模拟器：解析芯片与引脚，观察结果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50100366'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate. The project illustrates hardware-level behavior and can aid understanding of retro systems, fault tolerance, and emulation accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。该项目有助于理解硬件层面的行为、复古系统的仿真精度，以及对容错设计的研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/772c65beff6b4a2234048fb4874da8de9b43aa12cc1935aa8b02b8f358bd1f38/antirez/c64-hw-simulator' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/772c65beff6b4a2234048fb4874da8de9b43aa12cc1935aa8b02b8f358bd1f38/antirez/c64-hw-simulator' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50112330' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50112330' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ipinfo.io/blog/rpki-protects-70-internet-routes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RPKI now protects 70% of internet routes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RPKI 现已覆盖 70% 互联网路由</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50112330'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RPKI adoption has expanded to cover about 70% of internet routes, a meaningful milestone for BGP route validation and anti-hijack protections. The increase helps reduce misconfigurations and man-in-the-middle risks, though deployment still requires vendor support and operator readiness. Operators should upgrade gear and policies to leverage cryptographic validation across the network.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RPKI 已覆盖约 70% 的互联网路由，为 BGP 路由签名验证与防护提供显著提升，降低路由劫持风险。但全面落地仍依赖厂商支持与运营方准备，需要对设备和策略进行更新，以在全网范围内享受这项 cryptographic validation 的好处。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b1/57/b157bf7f-0fe2-447f-a945-12d7951755b3/content/images/2026/10/ipinfo-rpki-social-share-2400x1256-1.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b1/57/b157bf7f-0fe2-447f-a945-12d7951755b3/content/images/2026/10/ipinfo-rpki-social-share-2400x1256-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50080155' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50080155' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seriouseats.com/biscoff-history-12168161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Delta’s Biscoff cookies were supposed to be temporary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>达美航空的比斯科饼干本应只是临时供应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50080155'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delta Air Lines rolled out Biscoff cookies as a stopgap during supply constraints, only to become a beloved onboard staple with lasting cultural memory. The piece traces how a temporary amenity can become a lasting icon and a case study in product longevity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>达美航空在供给紧张时将比斯科饼干作为临时零食推出，结果却成为航班上的长久标配，成为旅客记忆中的经典。本文追踪这一临时福利如何演变成品牌符号与长期存在的案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.seriouseats.com/thmb/wUzxTxKnB_QUPQjtGkycR-W5TEY=/1500x0/filters:no_upscale():max_bytes(150000):strip_icc()/lotus_corporate_1-1LOTUSBAKERY-0047e2226d1a4c00af5d9fbe1e26fc07.jpg' data-full='https://www.seriouseats.com/thmb/wUzxTxKnB_QUPQjtGkycR-W5TEY=/1500x0/filters:no_upscale():max_bytes(150000):strip_icc()/lotus_corporate_1-1LOTUSBAKERY-0047e2226d1a4c00af5d9fbe1e26fc07.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50049872' data-tags='Open Source,Design,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50049872' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/shaongitbd/doory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Doory – Open-Source Canva</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Doory：开源的 Canva 替代工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50049872'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options. It represents a privacy-friendly, self-hostable alternative for teams and individuals who want control over their design pipeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。它为注重隐私、希望自托管设计流程的团队与个人提供了一个可控的替代选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4af21805adbf722ba00bcb0dcac5d2499d909c3235b76858d0d3a4d8bb86cca5/shaongitbd/doory' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4af21805adbf722ba00bcb0dcac5d2499d909c3235b76858d0d3a4d8bb86cca5/shaongitbd/doory' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50127586' data-tags='Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50127586' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/valimised/ivxv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Source code of the Estonian online voting system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统源代码公开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50127586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50127586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50127586'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research. This openness can improve transparency and help identify vulnerabilities, though online voting remains a debated topic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。这一举措有望提升透明度并帮助发现潜在漏洞，但在线投票的安全性与隐私性仍是讨论焦点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/38ef9ee1d48baa0b4f1b86fbd22d43b8f8e2963db37144330f08c08302b08311/valimised/ivxv' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/38ef9ee1d48baa0b4f1b86fbd22d43b8f8e2963db37144330f08c08302b08311/valimised/ivxv' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50053216' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50053216' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cendyne.dev/posts/2026-10-11-fable-took-my-jorb.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fable Took My Job</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fable 抢走了我的工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50053216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50053216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50053216'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay about how Fable affected the author&#x27;s job, illustrating AI&#x27;s impact on creative work and the need to adapt.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者讲述 Fable 如何影响自己的工作，揭示 AI 工具对创意工作岗位的冲击以及需要通过再培训来适应新角色。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cendyne.dev/c/JY6wB9Y9' data-full='https://cendyne.dev/c/JY6wB9Y9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50051532' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://radar.cloudflare.com/bots/as13238?dateRange=7d#bot-traffic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yandex bot traffic down 99% after data center attack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yandex 爬虫流量在数据中心攻击后下降 99%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50051532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50051532'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data center attack cut Yandex bot traffic by about 99%, affecting automated indexing and monitoring. The incident shows how infrastructure-level disruptions ripple into web services and analytics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据中心遭攻击后，Yandex 的爬虫流量下降约 99%，影响自动索引与监控服务。此事件显示基础设施层面的中断如何波及到网页服务和分析工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50063717' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50063717' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/prabanta-dev/janas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Janas: Run LLMs larger than RAM by streaming experts from SSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Janas：通过从 SSD 流式加载专家参数，在 RAM 之外运行超大规模 LLM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50063717'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed. By using a mixture-of-experts design, it fetches parts of the model only when required, reducing memory pressure without needing massive hardware upgrades. The approach could lower the bar for experimenting with large models on commodity machines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。它利用专家模型的分布式架构，只有在需要时才获取相应参数，降低对内存的压力，无需巨额硬件升级。该思路若规模化，将降低在普通硬件上尝试大模型的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2b8709e4e5b18677acb52688a2e08eedee46dc111fd625837cd421d857b1a052/prabanta-dev/janas' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2b8709e4e5b18677acb52688a2e08eedee46dc111fd625837cd421d857b1a052/prabanta-dev/janas' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50139067' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50139067' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://t3.at/blog/2026-10-17-parser-combinators/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parser Combinators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解析器组合子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50139067'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parser combinators are a functional-programming technique for building parsers by composing small parsing functions. The piece surveys core ideas, common combinators, and practical patterns, offering examples across languages and showing how to get readable, maintainable parsers without writing a monolithic parser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解析器组合子是通过将小型解析函数组合来构建解析器的函数式编程技术。文中概述了核心思想、常用组合子与实践模式，并给出跨语言示例，展示如何用模块化方式实现可维护的解析器。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://t3.at/assets/series/parser-combinators.jpg' data-full='https://t3.at/assets/series/parser-combinators.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://petal.live/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Petal, open-source CoScreen/ScreenHero finally free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Petal 开源协作工具，类似 CoScreen/ScreenHero 终于免费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50092470'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Petal provides an open-source alternative to CoScreen/ScreenHero for live collaboration and screen sharing, now freely available for self-hosted deployments. It lowers the bar for teams to build collaborative workflows without vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Petal 提供了一个开源的实时协作与屏幕共享替代方案，类似 CoScreen/ScreenHero，现已免费对外提供自托管部署。让团队在无厂商锁定的前提下实现协作工作流，门槛更低。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50139692' data-tags='Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50139692' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/legal-experts-warn-metas-new-terms-still-grant-broad-scraping-permissions-despite-language-changes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Legal experts warn Meta&#x27;s new terms still grant broad scraping permissions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法律专家警告：Meta新条款仍授予广泛抓取权限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50139692'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Legal scholars warn that Meta&#x27;s updated terms continue to grant broad rights to scrape data, despite language changes. The stance could enable large-scale data harvesting, raising concerns for researchers, startups and privacy advocates about how the platform&#x27;s data can be used.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法律专家警告，尽管措辞有所改动，Meta更新条款仍授权广泛抓取数据。这可能允许大规模数据收集，引发研究者、初创公司和隐私倡导者对平台数据使用的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/AdobeStock_1108807119_Editorial_Use_Only-700x440.jpeg' data-full='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/AdobeStock_1108807119_Editorial_Use_Only-700x440.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50119991' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/databases/2026/10/16/postgres-19-delayed-after-ai-reviewers-find-more-bugs-than-humans-can-fix/5303471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres 19 delayed after AI reviewers find more bugs than humans can fix</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres 19 因 AI 审核发现的问题过多而延迟发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50119991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50119991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50119991'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Postgres 19 release delayed after AI-assisted reviewers flagged more bugs than humans could fix. The episode illustrates both the promise and limits of AI in critical software QA, and may force changes in release workflows and bug triage for open-source projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 19 推迟发布，原因是在 AI 审核阶段发现的问题数量超过人力可修复的能力。此事件显示 AI 辅助代码审查在关键基础设施软件上的前景与局限，或将改变开源项目的发布与缺陷处理流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/228745.jpg?imageId=228745&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/228745.jpg?imageId=228745&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50114342' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danieltemkin.com/esolangs/folders' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Folders Programming Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Folders 编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50114342'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes an esoteric language where folders drive structure and execution, offering a playful exploration of nontraditional programming models. The design questions how data organization primitives influence syntax, semantics, and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一种以文件夹为核心概念的极简/实验性编程语言，展示把数据组织结构直接映射到执行模型的可能性。通过此设计反思编程语言的语法、语义及开发工具链的边界。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lab.specr.net/rigged-draw/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: How random are you?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：你有多随机？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50074909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50074909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50074909'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>How Random Are You? is an interactive exploration of personal randomness, testing whether our choices resemble true randomness. The project touches on probability, bias, and how AI systems model randomness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个关于个人随机性的互动测试，揭示人们在判断随机性时的偏差，以及 AI 如何建模与评估随机性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.sky.com/story/breaking-bad-star-rj-mitte-dies-aged-34-13598332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RJ Mitte has died</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 212</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50063831'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RJ Mitte, the actor known for Breaking Bad, has died at 34. His portrayal of Walter White Jr. helped humanize a central character, and his advocacy for autism awareness left an impact beyond the screen.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《绝命毒师》演员 RJ Mitte 去世，享年34岁。他在剧中饰演的沃特·怀特 Jr. 让人物家庭一角更具人性，他还积极倡导自闭症意识，对影视界和公众的认知产生了影响。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bfl.ai/blog/flux-3-image-klein' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FLUX 3 Image Klein</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flux 3 Image Klein：开源权重，56 亿参数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50123580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50123580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50123580'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flux 3 Image Klein is a 5.6B-parameter vision model released with open weights, intended for image tasks and local experimentation. Open access to the weights makes it useful for research, fine-tuning, and deployment trade-offs where mid-sized models balance performance and efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flux 3 Image Klein 是一款拥有 56 亿参数、公开权重的视觉模型，适用于图像任务和本地试验。公开权重利于研究、微调和在性能与效率间做出折衷的部署场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bfl.ai/api/og/blog/flux-3-image-klein' data-full='https://bfl.ai/api/og/blog/flux-3-image-klein' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50057668' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50057668' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://taylor.town/kk-ai-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kevin Kelly&#x27;s 2016 AI Predictions, 10 Years Later</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>凯文·凯利 2016 年 AI 预测 十年回顾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50057668'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective evaluation of Kevin Kelly&#x27;s 2016 AI predictions, noting which forecasts proved prescient and which fell short as AI matured.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾凯文·凯利2016年的AI预测，评估哪些预言准确、哪些落空，以及人工智能发展步伐的真实速度。文中强调自动化、人机协作以及快速落地等长期主题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.lede.me?title=Kevin+Kelly%27s+2016+AI+Predictions%2C+10+Years+Later&amp;lede=Kelly+predicted+AI+heavyweights+while+advocating+for+a+future+of+open+weights&amp;author=taylor.town&amp;date=2026.10.12' data-full='https://i.lede.me?title=Kevin+Kelly%27s+2016+AI+Predictions%2C+10+Years+Later&amp;lede=Kelly+predicted+AI+heavyweights+while+advocating+for+a+future+of+open+weights&amp;author=taylor.town&amp;date=2026.10.12' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50086165' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50086165' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/timescale/pg_vectorsearch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pg_vectorsearch: An index that is faster than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pg_vectorsearch：比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快的索引</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50086165'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database. Embedding an optimized index into Postgres can simplify architecture by avoiding external vector stores while improving search speed for vector-heavy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。把高效索引嵌入 Postgres 可以减少对外部向量存储的依赖，同时为向量密集型负载提升检索速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9bc9d4b1fab981e8198ab2804551c72703cedd61e328587dd2d405bd73fc2dc0/timescale/pg_vectorsearch' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9bc9d4b1fab981e8198ab2804551c72703cedd61e328587dd2d405bd73fc2dc0/timescale/pg_vectorsearch' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50120664' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50120664' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.gallup.com/poll/708614/desire-migrate-drops-new-low.aspx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Desire to Move Permanently to U.S. at New Low</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>希望永久移居美国的意愿降至新低</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50120664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50120664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50120664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global interest in permanently relocating to the United States has fallen to a new low in Gallup polling, reflecting softer immigration sentiment or shifting preferences. The trend could influence tech hiring and policy debates as companies and governments adapt to talent flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球对永久移居美国的意愿降至新低，反映出移民热度回落或偏好变化。这一趋势可能影响科技人才引进和相关政策的讨论与安排。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imagekit.gallup.com/fusion/tr:n-emd_soc_meta_og/POLL/ea2080b7-ab7b-4413-bc85-2559747a97ba.jpg' data-full='https://imagekit.gallup.com/fusion/tr:n-emd_soc_meta_og/POLL/ea2080b7-ab7b-4413-bc85-2559747a97ba.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50109707' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50109707' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/10/15/why-are-we-even-doing-all-this/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are we even doing all this?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们到底在干什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50109707'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terence Tao questions the meaning and goals behind ongoing high-effort mathematical research and big projects, urging clarity about value and societal impact. He argues for more thoughtful alignment between goals, resources, and real-world benefits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩质疑持续高强度的数学研究及由大科技驱动的项目的意义与目标，呼吁明确价值、优先级与社会影响。并指出在实际应用回报与资源投入之间应有更清晰的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1792167555' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1792167555' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50114758' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50114758' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://leodemoura.github.io/blog/2026-10-15-lean-now-ships-with-verified-kernels/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lean now ships with verified kernels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lean 现已随附经过验证的内核</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114758' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114758' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50114758'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lean now ships with a formally verified kernel, boosting trust in the core runtime and ensuring foundational safety properties. This marks a step toward certifiable software and reduces risk of unsound computations in critical workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lean 现已随附经过形式化验证的内核，提升核心运行时的可信度并确保基础安全属性。这标志着向可证明的软件迈进，降低关键工作流中计算不一致的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leodemoura.github.io/static/images/leo.jpg' data-full='https://leodemoura.github.io/static/images/leo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50064673' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50064673' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://red-green-blue.com/kid-pix-the-early-years' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Origin Story of Kid Pix</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kid Pix 的起源故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50064673'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The origin story traces how Kid Pix evolved from a playful drawing tool into a defining kids&#x27; software, driven by interface experiments, user feedback, and clever affordances for low-friction creativity. It highlights design choices—big buttons, joyful feedback, and forgiving tools—that made it a durable educational classic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>起源故事讲述了 Kid Pix 如何从一个有趣的绘图工具发展为儿童软件的代表，靠的是界面实验、用户反馈和降低创作门槛的巧妙设计。文章重点介绍了让它成为教育经典的设计要点：大按钮、愉悦反馈与宽容的工具机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/517c0a2be4b0d5eb59dbd468/1371748446133-W2X1056NBXFZ451BX97Z/Unknown.jpeg' data-full='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/517c0a2be4b0d5eb59dbd468/1371748446133-W2X1056NBXFZ451BX97Z/Unknown.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50131440' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50131440' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://finance.yahoo.com/economy/policy/article/the-us-federal-budget-deficit-for-2026-just-came-in-its-1992-trillion-191917669.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US federal budget deficit jumps 12% in one year to nearly $2 Trillion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国联邦预算赤字一年内跃升12%，接近2万亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50131440'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US federal budget deficit rose about 12% year over year to just under $2 trillion, driven by persistent spending and higher interest costs. The result underscores ongoing fiscal strain and will feed arguments about debt sustainability, budget priorities, and the trajectory of deficits under current policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国联邦预算赤字同比增长约12%，接近2万亿美元，反映持续的支出压力和更高的利息成本。此现象凸显财政压力，影响债务可持续性讨论、财政优先级及在现行政策下赤字走向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/5b1c027cab1879390d41eb8d5fdd929bc6bf950aa0b6a86131e21877519e4097/lightyear_networkapi/resizefill_w1200%3Bquality_80%3Bformat_webp/https%3A%2F%2Fd29szjachogqwa.cloudfront.net%2Fimages%2F2026-10%2Fce5f3393-d53f-48ea-91b1-aac1887c024f' data-full='https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/5b1c027cab1879390d41eb8d5fdd929bc6bf950aa0b6a86131e21877519e4097/lightyear_networkapi/resizefill_w1200%3Bquality_80%3Bformat_webp/https%3A%2F%2Fd29szjachogqwa.cloudfront.net%2Fimages%2F2026-10%2Fce5f3393-d53f-48ea-91b1-aac1887c024f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50118977' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118977' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://calif.io/research/big-fish' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Big Fish: Exploiting a 37-Year-Old Kernel Bug in OpenBSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Big Fish：在 OpenBSD 上利用一项37年历史的内核漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118977'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate exploitation of a 37-year-old kernel bug in OpenBSD, highlighting historic vulnerabilities that persisted in modern systems. The finding underscores the importance of memory safety, thorough patching, and ongoing code review in operating systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在 OpenBSD 上利用一项37年前的内核漏洞，揭示了长期存在于现代系统中的历史性弱点。此发现强调了内存安全、及时打补丁与持续的代码审查对于操作系统健康的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://calif.io/og/big-fish.jpg' data-full='https://calif.io/og/big-fish.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50059446' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50059446' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/feds-got-warrants-to-use-fake-cell-towers-to-hunt-down-don-lemons-producer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Feds got warrants to use fake cell towers to hunt down Don Lemon&#x27;s producer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦获准使用假冒基站追踪 Don Lemon 的制片人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50059446'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Federal warrants authorized the use of fake cell towers to locate a suspect connected to a case involving a television producer. The report underscores how law enforcement uses telecom deception tools to pinpoint targets, raising privacy concerns and the need for transparency and oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦机构获准动用假冒基站等追踪相关目标，试图定位与知名节目制作人相关的嫌疑人。这暴露了执法部门在定位隐私方面使用电信欺骗工具的做法，凸显对透明度与监督的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ac94a7a4676236d922d7944/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Feds%20Got%20Warrants%20to%20Use%20Fake%20Cell%20Towers%20to%20Hunt%20Down%20Don%20Lemon%E2%80%99s%20Producer_v1.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ac94a7a4676236d922d7944/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Feds%20Got%20Warrants%20to%20Use%20Fake%20Cell%20Towers%20to%20Hunt%20Down%20Don%20Lemon%E2%80%99s%20Producer_v1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50091875' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50091875' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/america-didn-t-lose-its-rare-earth-industry.-it-sold-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America didn&#x27;t lose its rare earth industry, it sold it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国并非失去稀土产业，而是把它卖掉了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50091875'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>America didn’t lose its rare-earth industry; it sold it, outsourcing processing and refining abroad while maintaining some mining. The piece reframes the risk around reliance on foreign supply chains and argues for reviving domestic processing to strengthen resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国并非失去稀土产业，而是将加工与精炼等环节外包到国外，同时保留部分开采能力。文章将风险聚焦于对外部供应链的依赖，并主张重建国内加工能力以提升供应链韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lawfare-assets-new.azureedge.net/assets/images/default-source/article-images/rare-earth-2.jpg?sfvrsn=e020d640_5' data-full='https://lawfare-assets-new.azureedge.net/assets/images/default-source/article-images/rare-earth-2.jpg?sfvrsn=e020d640_5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50108229' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50108229' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eink.com/news/detail/EInk-Launches-Latest-Version-of-Color-ePaper-Platform-E-Ink-Kaleido-4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>E Ink Kaleido 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>E Ink Kaleido 4 彩色电子纸</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mikroverk/esp32sim' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Esp32sim – Run ESP32 firmware in the browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Esp32sim—在浏览器中运行 ESP32 固件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware. It leverages browser-based emulation to emulate the ESP32 environment, reducing setup friction for developers and students. A practical tool for hands-on learning and quick debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。它通过浏览器端的仿真实现 ESP32 环境的模拟，减少开发者和学生的搭建成本。是一个面向动手学习与快速调试的实用工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openculture.com/2026/10/a-900-page-pre-pantone-guide-to-color-from-1692.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>900-Page Pre-Pantone Guide to Color from 1692 (With Complete High-Res Scan)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1692年的 Pantone 之前的900页色彩指南（含完整高分辨率扫描）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50053487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50053487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50053487'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 900-page color guide from 1692 predates Pantone and has been fully scanned in high resolution. It reveals early methods of cataloging colors, names, and pigments, offering historical insight into design and printing practices. The scan makes rare color references accessible for researchers and designers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这部1692年的900页色彩指南在 Pantone 出现之前成书，现已完成高分辨率完整扫描。它记录了早期的颜色分类、命名和颜料信息，提供了设计与印刷实践的历史视角。高分辨率的扫描让研究者和设计师能获取珍贵的色彩参考。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.universityofcalifornia.edu/news/new-study-shows-popular-glp-1-weight-loss-drug-may-slow-biological-aging' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLP-1 may slow biological aging</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLP-1药物或可减缓生物学衰老</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50132328'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study suggests GLP-1-based therapies, known for weight loss, might slow biological aging markers, hinting at extended healthspan. While intriguing, experts caution that results are early and require broader trials to verify real-world aging benefits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项新研究显示，用于减重的GLP-1药物可能减缓生物学衰老的标志，暗示潜在的健康寿命延长。但结果尚处于早期，需要更多临床研究来验证在现实世界的实际效用。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090084'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amsterdam police confiscated a server belonging to an Italian left-wing ISP at an American request, illustrating cross-border law-enforcement cooperation. The case raises questions about jurisdiction, political content, and how foreign authorities influence digital spaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在美国请求下，阿姆斯特丹警方扣押了意大利左翼互联网服务提供商A/I的服务器。这一行动凸显跨境执法合作的现实性，同时引发关于管辖权、政治内容及外部当局对数字空间影响的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50064536' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://doi.org/10.1111/acel.70751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blood cells from 80-year-old donor become stem cells with molecular age under 20</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>80岁捐献者血细胞经分子年龄逆转至20岁以下后成为干细胞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50064536'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reprogram blood cells from an 80-year-old donor into stem cells with molecular age under 20, suggesting markers of cellular rejuvenation. The finding hints at future age-related therapies, while underlining the challenge of translating molecular aging metrics into functional improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员把一名80岁捐献者的血细胞经分子年龄逆转至20岁以下后成为干细胞，显示了在细胞层面逆转衰老标志的可能性。该成果为未来的抗街老疗法提供新线索，同时也凸显了将分子年龄指标转化为实际功能的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50084622' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://defence24.com/geopolitics/starlink-limit-hits-ukraines-drones-the-battlefield-depends-on-spacex' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Starlink limit hits Ukraine&#x27;s drones</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50084622'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starlink bandwidth limits are constraining Ukraine’s drone operations on the battlefield. The bottleneck underscores how satellite internet can become a critical chokepoint in modern warfare and pushes for redundancy and alternative comms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>星链带宽限制正在影响乌克兰的无人机行动。该瓶颈凸显卫星互联网在现代战争中可能成为关键瓶颈，并推动对冗余通讯和替代通信手段的需求。</p>
