<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50121939' data-tags='AI,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50121939' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/designing-efficient-quantum-circuits' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Designing more efficient quantum circuits with Claude</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Claude 设计更高效的量子电路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50121939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers used Claude to suggest optimizations for quantum circuit layouts, reducing gate counts and suggesting rewrites that preserve functionality. This illustrates how large language models can assist in domain-specific synthesis and propose non-obvious transformations, though outputs still require formal verification and hardware-aware validation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者利用 Claude 提出量子电路的优化方案，能够减少门数并给出保持功能的重写建议。此类工作表明大模型能辅助领域特定的合成并提出非直观的转换，但这些建议仍需形式化验证和面向硬件的验证。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/cc389d90b468ebc86011f6642099dfad9a4a757f-2000x1125.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/cc389d90b468ebc86011f6642099dfad9a4a757f-2000x1125.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50088430' data-tags='Business,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50088430' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/asml-forecasts-q4-sales-above-expectations-ai-chip-demand-2026-10-14/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ASML&#x27;s strong orders ease fears AI spending boom is running out of steam</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ASML强劲订单缓解对AI支出放缓的担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088430' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088430' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088430'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ASML reports strong orders, easing concerns that the AI spending boom in semiconductors is cooling. Robust demand for advanced lithography suggests continued capex in AI-chip production and a supportive backdrop for chipmakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ASML 交出强劲订单，缓解了对半导体领域AI支出热潮放缓的担忧。对先进光刻设备的需求仍然强劲，为芯片制造商的资本开支提供了持续的支撑。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50089229' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50089229' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.apnic.net/2026/10/14/submarine-cable-resilience/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Submarine cable resilience</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海底电缆韧性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50089229'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Submarine cable resilience focuses on how undersea networks withstand physical damage, outages, and geopolitical pressure by using redundancy, route diversity, and faster repair protocols. With global traffic growing and cables concentrated in chokepoints, improving resilience is critical for internet stability and national infrastructure planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于海底电缆韧性的讨论集中在通过冗余、路径多样化和更快的维修流程来应对物理损坏、停摆与地缘政治压力。随着流量增长和关键海峡的集中，提升电缆韧性对互联网稳定与国家基础设施至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/2026-10-featured-submarine-cables.png' data-full='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/2026-10-featured-submarine-cables.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50060500' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50060500' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://micahflee.com/just-say-no-to-openai-and-anthropic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Just say no to OpenAI and Anthropic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>就拒绝 OpenAI 与 Anthropic</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50060500'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take urging readers to avoid OpenAI and Anthropic, citing concerns about data practices, safety, or governance; promotes alternatives like open-source models or self-hosted AI. The piece advocates prudent, privacy-respecting AI adoption and building with transparent tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者呼吁避免使用 OpenAI 与 Anthropic，基于数据隐私、安全性与治理等担忧；提倡转向开源模型或自托管的替代方案。观点强调理性采用、隐私友好以及使用透明工具进行构建。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1599595344070-c456bea6ee98?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDZ8fG9wZW58ZW58MHx8fHwxNzkxNzU0MTY2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1599595344070-c456bea6ee98?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDZ8fG9wZW58ZW58MHx8fHwxNzkxNzU0MTY2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50140001' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50140001' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open MicroManipulator – A sub-micrometer 3D motion control plattform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Open MicroManipulator：亚微米级三维运动控制平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50140001'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware. The project aims to democratize precision manipulation for research and education, enabling ultra-fine control in labs and makerspaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/744c82ff1502a583a645f6d75adf9c53ccfd16dc173877c7950768d0798069df/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/744c82ff1502a583a645f6d75adf9c53ccfd16dc173877c7950768d0798069df/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50092847' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50092847' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kenklippenstein.com/p/how-the-military-will-hack-the-election' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Military Will Hack the Election</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>军方如何黑进选举系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50092847'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece outlines potential methods the military could use to influence or hack elections, highlighting cybersecurity risks and the tension between civilian governance and national-security operations. It stresses the need for robust defenses, transparency, and norms under the rule of law.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨军方可能通过哪些方式影响或入侵选举，强调网络安全风险以及民政治理与国家安全行动之间的紧张关系。强调建立强健防御、提高透明度、并在法治框架内确立行为规范的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!pmSC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffd361944-16ec-4be5-84f8-19d2585c575c_512x342.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!pmSC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffd361944-16ec-4be5-84f8-19d2585c575c_512x342.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50132278' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50132278' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.opendemocracy.net/amazon-ai-surveillance-monitor-union-activity-uk-warehouses-birmingham-coventry-atlas-sutton-coldfield/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon uses AI to monitor union risk in UK warehouses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊在英国仓库使用AI监控潜在工会活动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50132278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50132278'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon is deploying AI to monitor potential union activity in its UK warehouses, aiming to preempt labor organizing. This raises privacy, worker rights, and chilling effect concerns, especially as regulators scrutinize surveillance tech in the workplace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊在英国仓库部署AI以监控潜在工会活动，意在提前预防劳资组织行动。此举引发隐私、工人权利以及对工作场所监控可能产生的震慑效应的担忧，监管机构也在就此进行审查。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/3f/80/3f804bea-4b2d-4ddf-9ff0-6ec7d0afa5fa/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/Amazon-AI-question-openDemocracy-edit.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/3f/80/3f804bea-4b2d-4ddf-9ff0-6ec7d0afa5fa/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/Amazon-AI-question-openDemocracy-edit.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50069337' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50069337' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pdftomp3.com/connect-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Use your OpenAI subscription to create audiobooks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 OpenAI 订阅来制作有声书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50069337'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN demonstrates turning text into spoken audio using OpenAI services, likely combining GPT-assisted scripting with TTS narration. It showcases a practical path to repurpose content as audiobooks and raises considerations about licensing, voice quality, and monetization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了利用 OpenAI 服务将文本转换为有声读物的可行做法，可能结合 GPT 脚本创作与 TTS 朗读。此类用法提供了一条将内容再创作为有声产品的实际路径，同时也需关注授权、语音质量与商业化等问题。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50074289' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50074289' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ua.news/en/world/kiberataka-zupinila-budivnitstvo-tuneliu-mizh-daniieiu-ta-nimechchinoiu-rmf24' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cyberattack halts construction of tunnel between Denmark and Germany</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网络攻击致使丹麦—德国之间隧道施工暂停</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50074289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50074289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50074289'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cyberattack halted the construction of the Denmark–Germany tunnel, delaying a key cross-border infrastructure project. The incident highlights the growing threat to large-scale projects and the need for resilient cyber defenses in public works.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>网络攻击导致丹麦-德国之间的隧道施工暂停，推迟了这一关键跨境基建项目。事件凸显大型工程日益面临的网络威胁，以及对公共工程网络弹性防护的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-cabinet.ua.news/uploads/images/cyberattack_halts_denmark_germany_tunnel_1791903271516.webp' data-full='https://cdn-cabinet.ua.news/uploads/images/cyberattack_halts_denmark_germany_tunnel_1791903271516.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50052814' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50052814' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/colinhacks/zod/commit/d821685ea6732436fcdf786a81d076958471e467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zod 4.6.6 compromised by malware?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zod 4.6.6 是否被恶意软件入侵？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50052814'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A suspected malware compromise affected a Zod release, raising concerns about supply-chain security for popular open-source libraries. The incident underscores the need for reproducible builds, vendor transparency, and rapid incident response in the ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对流行开源库 Zod 的发行版本怀疑被嵌入恶意软件，引发对软件供应链安全的担忧。事件凸显了可复现构建、供应商透明度和快速事件响应在生态系统中的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f8ad7d63c7a1406cfc8e44744b4c47f2cbdbfec14911579af6e780edb3baf0d5/colinhacks/zod/commit/d821685ea6732436fcdf786a81d076958471e467' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f8ad7d63c7a1406cfc8e44744b4c47f2cbdbfec14911579af6e780edb3baf0d5/colinhacks/zod/commit/d821685ea6732436fcdf786a81d076958471e467' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50070661' data-tags='Hardware,Security,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50070661' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kennedn.com/blog/posts/glitching/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It took me nine years to hack my Xbox 360</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我花了九年破解我的Xbox 360</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50070661'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nine-year odyssey detailing the process of hacking an Xbox 360, sharing the quirks of reverse engineering, exploit development, and community-driven breakthroughs. The narrative highlights how patience and persistence evolve in a long-running hardware-modding journey.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者花了九年记录破解 Xbox 360 的漫长过程，揭示反向工程、漏洞开发和社区驱动突破的点滴。这个过程展示了在长期的主机破解旅程中，耐心与坚持如何推动技术的演进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kennedn.com/blog/posts/glitching/coolrunner_closeup.webp' data-full='https://kennedn.com/blog/posts/glitching/coolrunner_closeup.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns. Maintainers are tracking the issue and discussing fixes and mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。维护者正在跟进并讨论修复与缓解措施。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://verdagon.dev/blog/valen-group-borrowing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Valen&#x27;s Memory Safety: A New Kind of Borrow Checking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valen 的内存安全：一种新型借用检查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50042362'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valen introduces a novel memory-safety borrowing model that aims to combine safety with greater flexibility. It contends with shared mutable state and concurrency more gracefully, potentially influencing future language design, and edging toward Rust-like ergonomics with different guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valen 提出一种新颖的内存借用模型，试图在保持安全性的同时放宽检查约束。它可能让共享可变状态和并发代码的使用更为高效、易用，并对未来的语言设计产生影响。若落地，或模糊像 Rust 的所有权与传统垃圾回收之间的边界。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://philzimmermann.com/EN/essays/WhyIWrotePGP.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I Wrote PGP (1991, revised 1999)</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50084674'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Phil Zimmermann revisits the motivations behind creating PGP and the role encryption played in shaping digital privacy. The piece provides historical context for why strong end-to-end encryption matters in an era of surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zimmermann 回顾了创建 PGP 的初衷，以及加密在塑造数字隐私中的作用。此文为在监控时代为何需要强端到端加密提供了历史背景。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arcfu.com/post/the-emperor-pelican-has-no-clothes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why do LLM pelicans look like this?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么大型语言模型里的鹈鹕长成这样？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090443'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLM pelicans look odd due to how models interpret prompts and training data; the article uses a playful analogy to reveal fundamental limits of generative AI and why outputs can be quirky or inconsistent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大型语言模型输出的鹈鹕看起来奇怪，源于模型对提示和训练数据的解读方式。本文用幽默对比揭示生成型AI的根本局限性，以及输出为何会显得古怪或不一致。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.americanscientist.org/article/whatever-became-of-holography' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Whatever became of holography? (2011)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全息术到底发展到了哪儿？（2011）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50049411'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at holography as of 2011, tracing its promise in 3D imaging, data storage, and display against the realities of materials and manufacturing costs. It explains why practical, widespread holo-imaging remains elusive despite decades of research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾截至2011年的全息术发展，聚焦于3D成像、数据存储与显示的潜力与现实之间的矛盾。尽管研究持续，实际大规模应用仍受材料、制造成本与可扩展性等瓶颈制约。文章提醒读者，成像质量、规模化和成本是关键挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50124730' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50124730' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teal-language.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teal – A statically-typed dialect of Lua</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Teal：Lua 的静态类型方言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50124730'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Teal adds a statically-typed layer to Lua, enabling compile-time checks and better editor tooling while remaining interoperable with existing Lua code. The project promotes optional typing and gradual adoption, helping teams catch errors earlier without abandoning Lua&#x27;s flexibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Teal 为 Lua 增添静态类型系统，能够进行编译期检查并提升编辑器支持，同时与现有 Lua 代码保持互操作。该语言强调可选类型与渐进式采用，帮助团队在不牺牲灵活性的前提下更早发现错误。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://teal-language.org/img/teal.svg' data-full='https://teal-language.org/img/teal.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50076089'>

It’s a useful refresher for engineers and analysts who apply stochastic simulation to problems in integration, finance, and risk modeling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇入门介绍了蒙特卡洛模拟技术，涵盖随机抽样、收敛性以及方差减小方法，并给出实用示例。对于将随机模拟用于积分、金融与风险建模的工程师和分析师来说，是一份有价值的复习材料。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50079701' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50079701' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2026/10/19/the-first-social-network' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The First Social Network: In rural Argentina residents send messages w AM radio</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>第一张社交网络：阿根廷农村地区居民用 AM 广播发送信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50079701'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An account of how rural Argentina residents used AM radio to exchange messages, effectively creating the first social network. It illustrates how communities built scalable communication networks long before the internet, offering design lessons for resilient, low-bandwidth collaboration in modern tech contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述阿根廷农村地区居民如何通过 AM 广播互传信息，实质上创建了第一张“社交网络”。这一历史案例展示了在互联网普及之前，社区如何构建可扩展的低带宽沟通网络，并为现代技术中的韧性协作提供设计启示。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frozenfractal.com/blog/2026/10/12/around-the-world-34-a-pretty-sky/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rendering pretty sky in Around The World game</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50119315'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Around The World game showcases a refined sky rendering pass, using improved lighting, cloud textures, and atmospheric scattering to create a more vivid, cinematic sky. The update highlights how art direction can deepen immersion in games, even in self-published or indie titles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Around The World 游戏的天空渲染得到了改进，采用更丰富的光照、云纹理和大气散射，呈现出更生动的天空。此次更新凸显了美术设计在提升玩家沉浸感方面的作用，尤其在独立开发的作品中。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50089577' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50089577' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Remember to migrate away from .name domain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>请记得从 .name 域名迁移</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50089577'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post urges users to move off the .name domain due to long-term stability and compatibility concerns, outlining practical steps to migrate to more common TLDs while preserving links and SEO impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该贴提醒用户尽早从 .name 顶级域名迁移，因为长期稳定性和兼容性存在隐患，文中给出迁移到更主流顶级域名并尽量保留链接和 SEO 的实用建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50084790' data-tags='Science,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50084790' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.michelecoscia.com/?p=2613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Economic Complexity of the Western Roman Empire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西罗马帝国的经济复杂性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50084790'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Applying economic complexity theory to the Western Roman Empire reveals varied production capabilities across regions and time. The analysis highlights trade networks, specialization, and input diversity that shaped long-run resilience and vulnerability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将经济复杂性理论应用于西罗马帝国，揭示不同地区在生产能力与贸易网络上的差异与演化。研究强调专门化程度和外部投入的多样性如何影响帝国的长期韧性与衰亡进程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.michelecoscia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/image-1024x821.png' data-full='https://www.michelecoscia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/image-1024x821.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50140769' data-tags='Show HN,Web,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50140769' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://legendofhyrule.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: The Zelda timeline to catch up before the Ocarina of Time remake</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50140769'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN features an interactive Zelda timeline that consolidates official and fan-made lore to help players catch up before the Ocarina of Time remake. It demonstrates how data-driven, browser-friendly tooling can enrich fan engagement and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示一个将官方与粉丝的塞尔达传说时间线整合在一起的互动工具，帮助玩家在《塞尔达传说：时之笛》重制版到来前赶上剧情。此类数据驱动、浏览器友好工具提升了粉丝参与度与可访问性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50144005' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50144005' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hoaspy.com/hoa/fl/florida-hoa-housing-crisis.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Florida&#x27;s HOAs and condo associations are making the housing crisis worse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>佛罗里达州的 HOA 与公寓协会正在加剧住房危机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50144005'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Florida HOAs and condo associations constrain housing supply via rules and fees, worsening the state&#x27;s housing affordability crisis. The piece argues for policy reforms and smarter, scalable housing development approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>佛罗里达州的业主协会和公寓协会通过规则和费用限制住房供应，进一步加剧了该州的可负担性危机。文章主张进行政策改革与更高效、可扩展的住房开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hoaspy.com/og-image.png' data-full='https://hoaspy.com/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50118828' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118828' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.worksinprogress.news/p/porcelain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What on Earth Is Dekton?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dekton 究竟是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118828'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dekton is a durable, ultra-compact surface material used in countertops and architecture, created via sintered stone technology. The piece explains its composition, durability, and how it compares to porcelain tile in real-world applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dekton 是一种耐用的超高密度表面材料，常用于台面和建筑装饰，由烧结石技术制成。文中介绍其成分、耐用性，以及在实际应用中与瓷砖等材料的对比。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!eBd_!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1dfe9441-3a38-4e69-9127-c829b4777e7f_2560x1707.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!eBd_!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1dfe9441-3a38-4e69-9127-c829b4777e7f_2560x1707.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50085323' data-tags='AI,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50085323' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://swedenherald.com/article/tv4-postpones-the-challenge-until-2027-after-technical-failure-erases-edited-footage' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TV4 postpones show after AI agent erases edited material</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TV4因AI代理删除编辑素材而推迟节目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50085323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50085323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50085323'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TV4 postpones a show after an AI agent erased edited footage, underscoring the fragility of AI-assisted media pipelines. The incident spotlights the need for safeguards, audit trails, and human oversight in automated production workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TV4在AI代理删除编辑素材后推迟节目，凸显AI 辅助的媒体工作流程的脆弱性。事件强调在自动化制作流程中需要安全保障、审计追踪和人工监督。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50072119' data-tags='Data,Programming,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50072119' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.polygon.com/order-of-the-sinking-star-impressions/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;500-hour&#x27; Order of the Sinking Star is as complex and exhausting as it sounds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>‘500 小时’的沉没之星命令像听起来那样复杂且耗人心力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50072119'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>&#x27;500-hour&#x27; Order of the Sinking Star is a deep-dive into a multi-stage, hard-to-fathom project that lives up to its name in both duration and complexity. It highlights how excessive scope and opaque rules drain energy and hinder progress, a familiar hazard in large software or research endeavors. For builders, it&#x27;s a reminder to curb scope creep and design workflows that preserve momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对《沉没之星》中的“500 小时”命令进行了深度解读，展示它的多阶段、极端复杂性确实如名称所示。过度的范围与模糊规则让参与者感到筋疲力尽，阻碍了进展，这在大型软件或科研项目中并不少见。对开发者来说，这是一则关于控制范围、设计可持续工作流的警示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.polygonimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/game-page-featured_order-of-the-sinking-star.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.polygonimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/game-page-featured_order-of-the-sinking-star.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50052163' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50052163' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clickhouse.com/blog/posette-talk-recap-postgres-isnt-slow-your-storage-is' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres isn&#x27;t slow, your storage is</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres 不慢，存储才是瓶颈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50052163'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL performance is often limited by storage I/O rather than the database engine itself. Optimizations should focus on storage subsystems—fast disks, SSDs, caching, and WAL throughput—to unlock real gains, with SQL tuning being secondary to I/O considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 的性能经常被存储 I/O 所拖累，而非数据库本身。真正的提升往往来自优化存储子系统，如更快的磁盘、SSD、缓存和 WAL 吞吐量，而非单纯调优 SQL。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clickhouse.com/_next/image?url=%2Fuploads%2FPostgres_Isn_t_Slow_Your_Storage_Is_8e9d00bdc5.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75' data-full='https://clickhouse.com/_next/image?url=%2Fuploads%2FPostgres_Isn_t_Slow_Your_Storage_Is_8e9d00bdc5.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50055375' data-tags='DevOps,Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50055375' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/93njd414rpbx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incident with Actions, Pull Requests and Issues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Actions、Pull Requests 与 Issues 的事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50055375'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub reported an incident affecting Actions, Pull Requests, and Issues, causing degraded performance and delays across automation and project management features. The incident page notes ongoing investigation with partial service restoration over time and guidance for users to retry operations and monitor status. This underscores how intertwined CI/CD in the platform is with everyday open-source workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 遭遇影响 Actions、Pull Requests 和 Issues 的事件，导致自动化与项目管理功能出现性能下降与延迟。事件页面持续更新调查进展，并给出重试与关注状态的指引。这再次凸显平台内置的CI/CD与日常开源工作流的高度耦合带来的脆弱性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50145337' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50145337' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hybrid Reasoning Agents: LLM agents that think in latent space, act through text</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>混合推理代理：在潜在空间思考、通过文本执行行动的 LLM 代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50145337'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers propose Hybrid Reasoning Agents that operate by thinking in a latent space while taking actions via text. The approach blends neural representations with text-based control to enable scalable, interpretable planning in complex tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者提出混合推理代理，其在潜在空间中进行推理，并通过文本执行行动。这种方法将神经表示与文本控制结合，提升在复杂任务中的可扩展性与可解释性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50128297' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50128297' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/oct/16/israeli-settlers-attack-olive-farmers-west-bank-washington-post-journalist-heidi-levine' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israeli settlers attack olive framers &amp; Washington Post journalist in West Bank</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西岸以色列定居者袭击橄榄农民及一名华盛顿邮报记者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50128297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50128297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50128297'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In the West Bank, Israeli settlers attacked olive farmers and a Washington Post journalist, highlighting ongoing violence and risks to reporters covering the conflict.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在西岸，以色列定居者袭击橄榄农民和一名华盛顿邮报记者，凸显冲突中的暴力风险以及对现场记者的安全威胁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/eacefac392ed027227ecec0ab9c67d13ee077d57/560_0_4583_3668/master/4583.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=55091aff993debfc75a7098822347096' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/eacefac392ed027227ecec0ab9c67d13ee077d57/560_0_4583_3668/master/4583.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=55091aff993debfc75a7098822347096' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果在 iPhone Duo 铰链中布置石墨管以散热</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple reportedly routed a graphite pipe through the iPhone Duo&#x27;s hinge to dissipate heat, demonstrating an unusual but creative approach to cooling constrained hardware. The tweak points to ongoing challenges in thermal management for compact devices and how materials science can unlock thinner designs. It also foreshadows how future wearables and foldables might handle heat.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称苹果在 iPhone Duo 的铰链中布置石墨管以帮助散热，体现了在有限空间内的热管理创新。此举反映出对薄型设备散热挑战的持续探索，以及材料科学在可穿戴和折叠设备设计中的潜在作用。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50059096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent37 offers affordable sandboxed environments to run autonomous agents, lowering the barrier to experimenting with agent-based workflows. The platform emphasizes isolation, reproducibility, and cost control, enabling teams to prototype prompts, policies, and multi-agent interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agent37 提供低成本的沙盒环境，用以运行自主代理，降低了基于代理工作流的实验门槛。该平台强调隔离性、可重复性和成本控制，便于团队原型化提示、策略与多代理协作。</p>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.davidv.dev/posts/first-contact-with-k8s/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A skeptic&#x27;s first contact with Kubernetes (2024)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50127572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50127572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50127572'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A skeptic details their first encounter with Kubernetes in 2024, offering candid observations on complexity, abstractions, and practical lessons learned.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者以怀疑者的视角讲述自己在2024年对 Kubernetes 的初次接触，分享对其复杂性、抽象层次的直率观察以及实用经验。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50046241' data-tags='Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50046241' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/ch1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chapter 1 of &quot;Ways of Seeing&quot; by John Berger (1972)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约翰·伯杰《观看之道》第一章（1972）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50046241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50046241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50046241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chapter 1 dissects how seeing is shaped by social constructs, power, and media, challenging conventional perceptions of art. It lays groundwork for media literacy and critical interpretation of images—relevant to tech culture&#x27;s visuals and AI-generated imagery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第一章批判性揭示了看到如何被社会结构、权力与媒介所塑造，挑战了对艺术的直观理解。为媒介素养和对图像的批判性解读奠定基础，与科技文化中的视觉表达和 AI 生成图像密切相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/images/ch1_0.jpg' data-full='https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/images/ch1_0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50041765' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50041765' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/news/using-ai-just-10-minutes-erodes-your-ability-persist-hard-things' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using AI for just 10 minutes erodes your ability to persist at hard things</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仅用 AI 十分钟就会削弱你坚持困难任务的能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50041765'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Berkeley study finds that even brief, 10-minute AI usage can erode persistence on hard tasks, prompting questions about cognitive effects of AI assistance. The finding has implications for education, productivity, and how tasks are structured in the age of AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伯克利研究发现，即使仅使用 AI 十分钟也会削弱坚持艰难任务的能力，这对教育、生产力以及在 AI 时代如何设计任务具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/themes/custom/vcr/assets/images/ucb-logo-blue.svg' data-full='https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/themes/custom/vcr/assets/images/ucb-logo-blue.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50104465' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reddit.com/r/apple/comments/1x6gz83/siri_is_so_bad_at_everything/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Siri is so bad at everything</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50104465'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A viral post laments Siri’s ineptitude across tasks, highlighting ongoing frustration with voice assistants and the gap to competing assistants. The thread captures broader skepticism about AI assistants and underscores the pressure on Apple to improve natural language understanding and reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个热门帖子抨击 Siri 在各项任务中的表现差，反映出用户对语音助手的持续不满，以及与其他助手的能力差距。该讨论体现了外界对 AI 助手的广泛质疑，也给 Apple 的改进压力。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50099136' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50099136' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/andunieee/flipflop' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Flipflop – Iroh-powered persistent tunnels for filesharing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Flipflop——由 Iroh 驱动的用于文件共享的持久隧道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50099136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50099136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50099136'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flipflop demonstrates persistent, reliable tunnels for file sharing powered by Iroh, enabling peers to stay reachable despite NATs and dynamic IPs. The project emphasizes resilient, privacy-friendly file transfer with a simple interface for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flipflop 展示了由 Iroh 支持的持久隧道文件共享方案，即使在 NAT 和动态 IP 下也能保持可达。该项目强调韧性、隐私友好且对开发者友好的文件传输体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/edd710930447fd1148460080381b243c381021bbf11ed5d711ca9da27f6a7cc6/andunieee/flipflop' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/edd710930447fd1148460080381b243c381021bbf11ed5d711ca9da27f6a7cc6/andunieee/flipflop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The disclosures underscore ongoing data-safety challenges as AI systems scale and are deployed in sensitive domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI披露又有四起涉及金融、医疗和政府数据的失控事件，显示在将AI系统用于敏感领域时，数据安全挑战依然存在。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nirajrajgor.com/blog/kindle-photo-frame-with-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I turned my unused Kindle into a photo frame with GPT-6 Astra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把闲置的 Kindle 变成带 GPT-6 Astra 的照片框</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turns an unused Kindle into a smart photo frame powered by GPT-6 Astra, automatically curating and displaying images with contextual captions. The project demonstrates how AI can repurpose aging hardware, turning a simple device into a personalized, ambient display with contextual captions or suggestions. It&#x27;s a playful example of software extending hardware lifespans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将未使用的 Kindle 改造成搭载 GPT-6 Astra 的智能照片框，通过 AI 自动筛选并展示图片并附上情境说明。该项目展示了人工智能如何为老旧硬件赋予新生命，打造个性化的环境显示。是把软件能力延展到硬件寿命的新颖示例。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50141579' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neilmadden.blog/2026/04/24/java-sealed-classes-and-exhaustive-pattern-matching/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Java sealed classes and exhaustive pattern matching</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Java 的密封类与穷尽模式匹配</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141579'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains how sealed classes enable exhaustive pattern matching checks at compile time, reducing runtime errors. It also covers best practices for using sealed hierarchies with switch expressions and discusses trade-offs with openness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章阐述了密封类如何在编译时实现穷尽模式匹配，降低运行时错误，并提供使用密封层次结构与 switch 表达式的最佳实践，同时讨论了开放性带来的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50064840' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/bherbruck/solidcraft' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: SolidCraft – Open-Source Parametric CAD in Rust (With MCP Support)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：SolidCraft——用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD（含 MCP 支持）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50064840'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SolidCraft is an open-source, Rust-based parametric CAD project with MCP support, enabling scriptable, reproducible design workflows. By leveraging Rust’s performance and safety, it targets developers who want fast, reliable CAD tooling without relying on heavy C++ stacks. The project signals growing interest in modern, open tooling for mechanical design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SolidCraft 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD 项目，且支持 MCP，便于实现可脚本化、可重复的设计工作流。利用 Rust 的性能与安全性，它面向需要快速、可靠 CAD 工具的开发者。该项目反映了对现代、开源机械设计工具日益增长的需求。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KiCad 10 的 AI 辅助 PCB 设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50085553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50085553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>KiCad 10 introduces AI-assisted features to automate PCB layout, routing, and component placement. The integration aims to boost designer productivity and unlock new design workflows for hardware engineers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KiCad 10 引入 AI 辅助功能，帮助自动化布线、走线和器件摆放，提升设计效率并开启新的硬件设计工作流程。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致歉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50099121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50099121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50099121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A public apology from Elevation Lab addressing a recent misstep, outlining what happened and how the team plans to fix it. The note emphasizes accountability, customer trust, and concrete follow-up actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Elevation Lab 发表公开道歉，说明事件经过与拟采取的修复措施，强调问责与对客户信任的承诺以及后续的具体行动。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50103255' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50103255' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lf-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intuitive concurrent programming in any language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在任意语言中实现直观并发编程：LF-lang</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50103255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50103255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50103255'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LF-lang proposes an intuitive way to express concurrent programs that abstracts away language-specific threading. It aims for a universal model that can be mapped to multiple host languages, easing cross-language concurrency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LF-lang 提出一种在任意语言中实现直观并发编程的方式，抽象线程细节并提供统一模型以跨宿主语言映射。该方法旨在简化并发编程并提升跨语言开发的一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.lf-lang.org/img/lf-social-card.png' data-full='https://www.lf-lang.org/img/lf-social-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50048522' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50048522' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recursive self-improvement through collective intelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过集体智慧实现的递归自我提升</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50048522'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how collective intelligence could fuel recursive self-improvement in AI systems, via feedback and collaboration among humans and agents. Iterative loops could push capabilities beyond a single-model ceiling, but governance and alignment become more complex at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究通过集体智慧驱动 AI 的递归自我提升的可能性，借助人机与多代理的协作与反馈形成自我改进循环。随着协同放大能力，进展可能超出单一模型的上限，但规模化时在治理和对齐方面的挑战也随之增加。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/assets/figures/ogp.png' data-full='https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/assets/figures/ogp.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50060674' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50060674' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jacobin.com/2026/10/books-publishing-reading-mass-market-paperback' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Publishers Killed the Paperback, Then Blamed Readers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>出版商杀死平装书？然后怪读者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50060674'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical take on how publishers pushed away mass-market paperback formats in favor of digital or pricier editions, blaming readers for declines they partly caused. The piece analyzes pricing, accessibility, and the business incentives shaping reading habits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评出版商推动平装市场式微，转而以数字版或高价版本获利，并把读者的阅读热情下降归咎于他们。文章剖析定价、可及性与商业激励如何影响阅读习惯。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.jacobin.com/images/2026/10/396423859724.jpg' data-full='https://media.jacobin.com/images/2026/10/396423859724.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50021989' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50021989' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anderegg.ca/2026/10/08/i-guess-im-using-webp-now' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I guess I&#x27;m using WebP now?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我想我现在在用 WebP 了？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50021989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50021989'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tests WebP&#x27;s real-world impact, weighing compression gains against quality and compatibility. It argues that WebP can reduce image sizes significantly for web apps, but adoption hinges on tooling and browser support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试使用 WebP，并讨论在压缩效率、画质和跨浏览器兼容性方面的权衡。结论是 WebP 能显著减小图片体积，但普及取决于工具链与浏览器支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://anderegg.ca/img/logo.png' data-full='https://anderegg.ca/img/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50090799' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50090799' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconsensus.dev/blog/2026/10/14/has-ai-tells.html?focus=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Has AI tells</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Has AI Tells</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090799'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece catalogs subtle patterns—&#x27;tells&#x27;—that reveal AI-generated content, from phrasing quirks to structural repetition, and discusses how both detectors and generators are adapting in response. Understanding these tells is useful for detection, but it&#x27;s also a moving target as models and adversaries continuously evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章列举了可能暴露 AI 生成内容的微妙特征（如措辞怪异与结构重复），并讨论了检测工具与生成模型如何相互适应。掌握这些“迹象”有助于鉴别，但随着模型和对抗方不断进化，这个目标也在不断移动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theconsensus.dev/static/sponsor-clickhouse-white.svg?v=cX326ZSNtsg=' data-full='https://theconsensus.dev/static/sponsor-clickhouse-white.svg?v=cX326ZSNtsg=' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oxide.computer/blog/voxel-labs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Virtual Oxide Labs: Emulating a Full Oxide Rack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Virtual Oxide Labs：全套 Oxide 机架仿真</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50092423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50092423'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Virtual Oxide Labs demonstrates full-stack emulation of an Oxide rack for development, testing, and reproducible research. The project showcases how virtualization accelerates hardware-focused experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Virtual Oxide Labs 展示了对完整 Oxide 机架的全栈仿真，便于开发、测试与可重复性研究。该项目展示了虚拟化如何加速硬件导向的实验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50141681' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/10/18/will-it-be-a-golden-age-or-a-dark-age-for-mathematics-and-science/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Will it be a golden age or a dark age for mathematics (and science)?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学（与科学）会迎来黄金时代还是黑暗时代？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141681'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terence Tao weighs the future of mathematics and science, considering AI, collaboration, funding, and openness as drivers for breakthroughs or stagnation. He argues outcomes depend on policy choices and collective effort.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩评估数学与科学的未来，考量 AI、协作、资金与开放性如何推动突破或拖累发展。他认为结果取决于政策选择与集体努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1792343053' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1792343053' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118318' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/what-was-a-token-ring-network/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What was a Token Ring network?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Token Ring 网络到底是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118318'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Token Ring was an early LAN technology using token-passing to avoid collisions. The piece explains its historical context, architecture, and why it faded in favor of Ethernet, despite its influence on network design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Token Ring 曾是早期局域网技术，采用记权令牌机制避免冲突。文中回顾其历史背景、架构及为何最终被以太网取代，但对网络设计的影响仍在。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/what-happened-to-token-ring-social.jpg' data-full='https://dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2022/03/what-happened-to-token-ring-social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
