<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50079280' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/a-new-super-ice-is-10-times-stronger-bringing-it-close-to-the-strength-of-concrete' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New &#x27;super ice&#x27; 10 times stronger, bringing it close to the strength of concrete</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型“超级冰”强度提升至原来十倍，接近混凝土强度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50079280'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists unveil a &#x27;super ice&#x27; that is 10 times stronger and approaching the strength of concrete. The material advances could enable new industrial applications for cold-region construction and energy-efficient targets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家推出一种“超级冰”，强度提高十倍，接近混凝土强度。该材料有望在冷区建筑与工程应用中带来新机会。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/IceBlock-1200x675.jpg' data-full='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/IceBlock-1200x675.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50049508' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50049508' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tedhaley.ca/pensieve/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local Indexing to reduce tool calling and token usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地索引以减少工具调用和令牌消耗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50049508'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces local indexing to cut down external tool invocations and token usage in AI workflows, improving speed and reducing costs. The approach relies on caching or embedding domain-specific knowledge locally so agents can answer without round-trips.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出通过本地索引来减少外部工具调用和 tokens 使用，在 AI 工作流中提升速度并降低成本。这种方法借助本地缓存或领域知识的嵌入，使代理在本地就能给出答案，减少远程请求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tedhaley.ca/pensieve/og.png' data-full='https://tedhaley.ca/pensieve/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50044272' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50044272' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://epestr.com/blog/reverse-engineering-a-commercial-spatial-audio-effect/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I reverse engineered a commercial spatial audio effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何逆向工程一个商业空间音效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50044272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50044272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50044272'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author reverse engineers a commercial spatial audio effect, outlining DSP techniques and signal-flow insights to reproduce similar results. The write-up demonstrates practical approaches to dissecting professional audio pipelines and the limits of IP protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过动手分析，一步步揭示了一个商业空间音效是如何实现的，包括数字信号处理技术、信号流程与复现思路。此文展示了知识产权保护的边界，以及公开的 DSP 知识如何帮助人们理解专业音频处理的工作原理。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50072485' data-tags='Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50072485' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://orlp.net/blog/two-stack-sliding-window-aggregation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two-Stack Sliding-Window Aggregation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两栈滑动窗口聚合</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50072485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50072485'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a two-stack approach to maintaining sliding-window aggregates with improved performance characteristics. The data-structure technique offers amortized constant-time updates and is particularly relevant for real-time analytics and streaming workloads. It compares favorably to traditional deque-based solutions in certain workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出双栈结构的滑动窗口聚合算法，实现对滑动窗内聚合值的高效维护，更新成本摊销为常数级。该方法在实时分析与流式处理场景中具有优势，并与传统的双端队列实现进行对比。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50054465' data-tags='Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50054465' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phloem.stemdeck.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Phloem: Git history grown like a slime mold</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Phloem：Git 历史像黏菌一样生长</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50054465'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Phloem visualizes Git history as growth patterns reminiscent of slime mold, offering a fresh lens on repository evolution and activity. It helps engineers spot trends in branching and contributor dynamics at a glance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Phloem 将 Git 历史以类似黏菌生长的模式可视化，提供观察代码库演变的新视角，帮助开发者一眼看出分支与贡献者动态的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/aws/announcing-aws-lambda-web-functions-for-fast-consistent-web-app-performance-anywhere/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS Lambda Web Functions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS Lambda Web Functions 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50069318'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AWS announces Lambda Web Functions, enabling fast, consistent web app performance from anywhere by running web workloads inside Lambda. This could simplify serverless web architectures and broaden edge-ready deployment options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AWS 宣布推出 Lambda Web Functions，使网页工作负载在 Lambda 中运行，从而在任何地点实现快速且一致的网页应用性能。这可能简化无服务器网页架构并扩展就绪边缘部署的选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2908q01vomqb2.cloudfront.net/da4b9237bacccdf19c0760cab7aec4a8359010b0/2026/10/09/01979_AWS_Lambda_Web_Functions-horizontal-1-1120x630.png' data-full='https://d2908q01vomqb2.cloudfront.net/da4b9237bacccdf19c0760cab7aec4a8359010b0/2026/10/09/01979_AWS_Lambda_Web_Functions-horizontal-1-1120x630.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50097843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50097843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50097843'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into the hinge, hydraulics, and control systems that let articulated buses bend safely around corners. The piece explains how articulation joints balance flexibility with stability, enabling high-capacity transit in urban cores.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入解读让铰接式公交车在转弯时仍能安全弯曲的铰接、液压和控制系统。文章阐释铰接点如何在灵活性与稳定性之间取得平衡，使大容量交通在城市核心得以运行。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/SaarthurR/atticus-mac' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Atticus – Hold-to-talk voice assistant for macOS that runs on Claude -p</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Atticus——在 macOS 上运行的按住说话的语音助手，基于 Claude-p</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Atticus is an open-source macOS voice assistant that uses Claude with a &#x27;hold-to-talk&#x27; interface, enabling convenient, hands-free interaction. It demonstrates modular, privacy-friendly AI tooling on the desktop with a popular LLM backend.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Atticus 是一个开源的 macOS 语音助手，采用 Claude-p 提供后台服务并使用“按住说话”的交互模式，便捷直观。展示了在桌面端使用主流大语言模型后端的模块化、注重隐私的 AI 工具链。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50134617' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://doctor-seo.net/en/blog/news/chrome-edge-removing-xslt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome and Edge are removing XSLT: what breaks and what does not</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome 和 Edge 将移除 XSLT：哪些会受影响，哪些不会</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50134617'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome and Edge are dropping XSLT support, which will break client-side XML transformations many sites rely on. Expect breakage in legacy workflows, but server-side transforms or alternative pipelines can mitigate; developers should audit dependencies and plan migrations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 与 Edge 将移除 XSLT 支援，将使依赖于客户端对 XML 进行转换的站点受到影响。遗留工作流可能中断，但服务器端转换或替代方案可以缓解；开发者应审视依赖并规划迁移。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>聪明人不会认为别人很笨（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50086313'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This essay argues that smart people should not assume others are stupid, underscoring the value of epistemic humility and respectful discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章主张聪明人不应以为他人愚蠢，强调知识论上的谦逊和尊重性话语的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50083673' data-tags='Health,Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50083673' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hub.jhu.edu/2026/10/12/magnetic-millirobots-cardiac-treatment/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wireless magnetic millirobots that deliver medicine inside the beating heart</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无线磁控微型机器人在跳动的心脏内递药</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50083673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50083673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50083673'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have demonstrated wireless, magnetically actuated millirobots capable of navigating the beating heart to deliver drugs with targeted precision. By operating without wires or implants, these micro-robots aim to minimize invasive procedures for cardiac therapies. Real-world challenges remain in steering through dynamic blood flow, ensuring biocompatibility, dosing accuracy, and regulatory safety before human use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究团队展示了通过磁控无线微型机器人在跳动的心脏内导航并定点递药的能力。此类机器人无需导线或体内植入即可实现药物递送，潜在地降低心血管治疗的侵入性。要在人体应用落地，仍需解决随动的血流导航、材料生物相容性、给药精度与安全性等挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50136150'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>3Blue1Brown argues that this is an ideal moment to study math, highlighting accessible resources, visual intuition, and the excitement of discovery that modern tools unlock. The video frames math as a living, creative pursuit powered by these tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>3Blue1Brown 认为现在学习数学是最好的时机，强调可获取的资源、直观的可视化和新工具在现代环境中带来的发现乐趣。视频将数学呈现为由这些工具驱动的充满创造性的现实探索。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://indigene.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Indigene – Native plants for where you stand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Indigene——就地本地植物指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50064470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50064470'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Indigene is a Show HN project that maps native plants to your location, helping you discover regional flora and plan local gardens. By tying plant data to geolocation, it spotlights the value of localized biodiversity tools and community knowledge in sustainable living.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Indigene 是一个 Show HN 项目，将本地植物按你的位置映射，帮助你发现区域植物并规划本地花园。通过地理定位将植物数据联动，凸显了本地化生物多样性工具在可持续生活中的价值与社区知识的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50069942' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50069942' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/1009802/character-ai-files-for-bankruptcy-disney' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Character.ai Files for Bankruptcy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Character.ai 申请破产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50069942'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Character.ai has filed for bankruptcy, signaling a formal restructuring and potential shifts in product strategy, staffing, or asset disposition as the company navigates financial challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Character.ai 已提交破产申请，标志着正式的重组与可能的产品策略、人员调整或资产处置等变化，公司正应对财政挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/10/STK278_character_ai_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/10/STK278_character_ai_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50141429' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/artificial-intelligence/anthropics-proposed-ai-safety-bill-only-kicks-in-if-50-deaths-or-usd1-billion-in-damages-are-on-the-line-proposal-would-effectively-make-the-commerce-department-the-only-agency-capable-of-stopping-an-ai-model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s proposed AI safety bill only kicks in if 50 deaths or $1B in damages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 提议的 AI 安全法案：仅在50例死亡或1亿美元损失时才生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141429'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The proposed AI safety bill by Anthropic triggers only when fatalities exceed 50 or damages reach $1B, raising questions about risk-based governance and the role of regulatory agencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 提出 的 AI 安全法案仅在死亡人数达到50人或损失超过1亿美元时生效，引发关于风险分级治理与监管机构职责的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50135379' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openjdk.org/projects/jdk/28/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JDK 28</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JDK 28 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50135379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50135379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50135379'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenJDK&#x27;s JDK 28 continues the project’s rapid release cadence, delivering under-the-hood JVM and library enhancements. Expect performance tweaks, improved diagnostics, and evolving language features that influence enterprise Java development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenJDK 的 JDK 28 延续六个月的发布节奏，带来 JVM 与标准库的改进。预计在性能、诊断与语言特性方面有更新，对企业级 Java 开发影响显著。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openjdk.org/images/openjdk2.svg' data-full='https://openjdk.org/images/openjdk2.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50125545' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50125545' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theatlantic.com/science/2026/10/giant-african-snails-florida-invasion/688850/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nine-Inch Snails</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>九英寸蜗牛</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50125545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50125545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50125545'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Giant African land snails are spreading in Florida, posing ecological and agricultural risks given their large size and slow-dispersal habits. The piece looks at how authorities monitor, manage, and study this invasive species, and what their presence reveals about containment challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>非洲巨型蝸牛正在佛州扩散，对生态与农作物造成风险，因其体型大且繁殖力强。文章探讨当局如何监测、遏制以及研究这种入侵物种，以及它们对有效控制的启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122736' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122736' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sandordargo.com/blog/2026/09/30/cpp26-debugging' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C++26: The New Debugging Header</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C++26：新的调试头文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122736'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C++26 introduces a standardized debugging header intended to centralize diagnostics, better runtime checks, and developer-facing utilities like stack traces and improved assertions. Standardizing these tools should make cross-platform debugging more consistent and reduce ad-hoc, platform-specific debugging code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C++26 引入了一个标准化的调试头文件，旨在集中诊断功能、改进运行时检查，并提供堆栈跟踪与更完善的断言等开发者工具。标准化后将使跨平台调试更一致，减少零散的特定平台调试代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sandordargo.com/assets/img/SANDOR_DARGO_ROUND.JPG' data-full='https://www.sandordargo.com/assets/img/SANDOR_DARGO_ROUND.JPG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50080246' data-tags='Show HN,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50080246' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wonderhop.io/en/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Wonderhop – A daily game: guess what most people say, or what none do</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wonderhop：每日竞猜大作战——猜多数人会说什么，还是没有人会说什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50080246'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wonderhop is a daily game where players guess what the majority will say or what no one would say. This format encourages quick, social-psychology oriented responses and can shed light on collective perception biases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wonderhop 是一款每日游戏，玩家猜测多数人会说什么，或者没人会说什么。这个玩法强调快速的社交心理反应，能揭示集体认知偏差。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wonderhop.io/og/en.png' data-full='https://wonderhop.io/og/en.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50096110' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50096110' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.iwm.org.uk/history/iwm/cwr/telephone' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SIGSALY: Churchill&#x27;s top secret hotline to the president</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SIGSALY：丘吉尔与总统之间的绝密热线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50096110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50096110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50096110'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SIGSALY was a massive wartime cryptographic voice system that provided secure communications between Allied leaders. It represents an early, fully digital approach to encrypted telephony and marked a milestone in cryptography and secure data transmission.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SIGSALY 是二战时期的巨型数字化加密语音系统，为盟军领导人提供安全通信，标志着对话音 cryptography 的重要里程碑，也是早期数字化密钥传输的里程碑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.iwm.org.uk/sites/default/files/2022-11/churchill_and_guy_on_phone.jpg' data-full='https://www.iwm.org.uk/sites/default/files/2022-11/churchill_and_guy_on_phone.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50090932' data-tags='Show HN,Privacy,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50090932' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clova.email/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I wrote an email app that accepts only one sender per inbox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: 我写了一个每个收件箱只接受一个发件人的邮件应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090932'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This email app enforces a single-allowed-sender-per-inbox model to create ultra-focused, low-noise communication channels—useful for newsletters, one-to-one project inboxes, or transactional messages. The constraint trades flexibility for simplicity, aiming to eliminate spam and cognitive overhead by design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个邮件应用对每个收件箱只允许一个发件人，打造高度聚焦、低干扰的沟通通道，适合新闻简报、项目专用邮箱或事务性通知。它以设计上的限制换取简洁性，旨在通过规则化消除垃圾邮件和认知负担。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clova.email/og-image.png' data-full='https://clova.email/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50065572' data-tags='Programming,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50065572' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frankforce.com/littlejs-is-2d-and-3d-now/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LittleJS is 2D and 3D now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LittleJS 现在支持 2D 和 3D</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50065572'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LittleJS, a lightweight JavaScript game engine, has added 3D capabilities while retaining its minimalist 2D API and small footprint. That expansion lets developers prototype simple 3D scenes and games without moving to heavier engines, keeping fast load times and easy deployment for web-based projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>轻量级 JavaScript 游戏引擎 LittleJS 在保持简洁 2D API 和小体积的前提下加入了 3D 功能。开发者因此能在不迁移到复杂引擎的情况下快速搭建简单的 3D 场景和游戏，维持网页项目的快速加载与便捷部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://frankforce.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/site-train-sunset.png' data-full='https://frankforce.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/site-train-sunset.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50060547' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50060547' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-08-sticky-alpine-darwin-year-carnivorous.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sticky alpine flower confirms Darwin&#x27;s 150-year-old carnivorous plant prediction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>粘性高山花证实达尔文关于食虫植物的150年预测</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50060547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A alpine plant with a sticky trapping mechanism provides evidence for Darwin&#x27;s long-standing prediction about carnivory in alpine environments. The result informs evolutionary biology about how nutrient-poor habitats drive novel adaptations and may broaden the range of carnivory in plants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现某高山植物具粘性捕虫机制，验证了达尔文关于高山环境中食虫现象的长期预测。这一发现帮助理解在贫瘠环境中如何推动新颖适应，以及食虫性在植物中的潜在扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/carnivorous-plants-con.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/carnivorous-plants-con.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50081151' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50081151' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blumify.io/people' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I built a tool to track what AI leaders say and how their views change</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我打造了一个工具，用于跟踪 AI 领袖在视频中的发言及其观点的变化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50081151'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tool collects AI leaders&#x27; statements from video sources and analyzes how their positions shift over time, offering a data-driven lens on industry trends and potential biases. It reveals how discourse among influential AI figures evolves as technologies and policies evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个工具从视频中抓取 AI 领袖的陈述，并分析其观点随时间的变化，为行业趋势与潜在偏见提供数据化视角。该工作揭示了在技术与政策演进中，知名人物的话语如何发生演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blumify.io/opengraph-image?5887fe79cbe072ac' data-full='https://blumify.io/opengraph-image?5887fe79cbe072ac' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50118267' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118267' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anuragbhatia.com/post/2026/06/rfc8950-announce-ipv4-with-ipv6-next-hop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFC8950: Announce IPv4 with an IPv6 next-hop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RFC8950：使用 IPv6 下一跳宣布 IPv4</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118267'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RFC 8950 proposes a method to announce IPv4 reachability via an IPv6 next-hop, facilitating coexistence during IPv4/IPv6 transitions. The approach can simplify routing in dual-stack networks and content delivery paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RFC 8950 提出通过 IPv6 下一跳宣布 IPv4 的可达性，以简化在双栈网络中的路由与内容分发路径，帮助平滑过渡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.anuragbhatia.com/web/post/2026/06/rfc8950-announce-ipv4-with-ipv6-next-hop/topology.png' data-full='https://cdn.anuragbhatia.com/web/post/2026/06/rfc8950-announce-ipv4-with-ipv6-next-hop/topology.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50138087' data-tags='Business,Web,Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50138087' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://webmasters.stackexchange.com/questions/88659/how-can-cloudflare-offer-a-free-cdn-with-unlimited-bandwidth/88685#88685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Matthew Prince on why Cloudflare offers a free version and always will (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Matthew Prince 解释为何 Cloudflare 将继续提供免费版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50138087'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare maintains a free tier as a core part of its growth and web-wide resilience strategy. The model relies on paid upsells and enterprise features for revenue, while free users help widen the network effects and trust in the platform. This stance has broad implications for developers relying on free CDN/security features and for the economics of internet infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 将免费版视为增长与网络韧性策略的核心组成，依靠付费升级和企业级功能来盈利。免费用户扩大了网络效应与平台信任度，这一做法对依赖免费 CDN/安全功能的开发者，以及互联网基础设施的经济生态具有广泛影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50080275' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pulseos.finance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Pulse Local, a Mac budgeting app whose AI models run on your machine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Pulse Local——在本机运行 AI 模型的 Mac 预算应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50080275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50080275'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pulse Local is a Mac budgeting app whose AI models run on your machine, not in the cloud. This on-device AI approach improves privacy and reduces reliance on network connections, but may constrain model size or update cadence. It signals a broader shift toward privacy-preserving local AI for consumer apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pulse Local 是一款在 Mac 上运行其 AI 模型的记账应用，全部在本地完成推理，不依赖云端。这种本地化 AI 提升了隐私保护和离线能力，但也可能受限于模型规模和更新频率。它体现了在消费类应用中向本地化 AI 演进的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(287) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.getlazarus.org/dynamic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Free Pascal compiler now supports dynamic packages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Free Pascal 编译器现已支持动态包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50144066'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Free Pascal compiler now supports dynamic packages, enabling runtime-loading of modular components and plugins. This makes it easier to distribute extensible Pascal apps and reduces binary bloat by loading code on demand, aligning with Lazarus/LF project workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Free Pascal 编译器现已支持动态包，允许在运行时加载模块和插件，提升可扩展性并降低初始二进制体积。此举与 Lazarus、跨平台 Pascal 应用的生态更紧密结合。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/dogs-seem-to-agree-with-humans-that-bouba-is-round-and-kiki-is-spiky' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dogs seem to agree with humans that &#x27;bouba&#x27; is round and &#x27;kiki&#x27; is spiky</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>狗似乎认同人类的圆形-尖刺联想：Bouba/Kiki 实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50136979'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dogs align with humans on Bouba/Kiki shape-label mappings, suggesting shared perceptual categories across species. The result supports the idea that some cross-species associations are rooted in perceptual priors rather than language.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>犬类在 Bouba/Kiki 的形状标签映射上与人类保持一致，表明不同物种可能共享感知类别。研究结果支持某些跨物种联想源自感知先验，而非语言。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/GettyImages-597067015-2560x1440-1.jpg' data-full='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/GettyImages-597067015-2560x1440-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sebsite.pw/w/20261012-notstdc.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your C is not standard C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的 C 不是标准 C</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50071546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Your C is not standard C argues that much real-world C code relies on non-standard extensions, risking portability and surprising compiler behavior. The piece advocates writing clearer, standard-conforming C and provides examples of common pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出日常写的 C 往往偏离标准 C，非标准扩展带来的可移植性与编译器行为的意外。提出书写更清晰、符合标准的 C 的建议，并给出常见陷阱的示例。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article outlines a rising trend of websites blocking AI agents from crawling or accessing content, and its implications for AI training data, monetization, and the future of automated browsing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章概述越来越多的网站阻止 AI 代理访问其内容的趋势，以及这对 AI 训练数据、商业模式与自动化浏览未来的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50069439' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50069439' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opennet.ru/base/maillist/169.txt.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Major ICQ security hole (1998)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1998年ICQ重大安全漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50069439'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Old but instructive: a major security hole in ICQ from 1998 exposed users to potential remote injection or information disclosure due to weak cryptography and flawed authentication. The incident is revisited to illustrate the evolution of instant-messaging security models and why modern protocols hardened against such flaws.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾1998年的ICQ重大安全漏洞，该问题暴露出用户可能被远程利用或信息泄露，原因在于早期加密与认证设计的薄弱。此文通过这次事件阐释即时通讯领域早期的风险模型，以及这些漏洞如何推动后续的安全改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opennet.ru/opennet2.gif' data-full='https://opennet.ru/opennet2.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heise.de/en/news/Crypto-thieves-sell-hardware-wallets-with-secret-eSIM-11485188.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Crypto thieves sell hardware wallets with secret eSIM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加密盗贼售卖带有秘密 eSIM 的硬件钱包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50068508'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Criminals are reportedly selling hardware wallets with a secret eSIM, enabling covert connectivity and control. The development highlights evolving attack vectors in crypto security and the need for trusted supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，犯罪分子在市场上售卖内置秘密 eSIM 的硬件钱包，可能实现隐蔽连网与控制。这一趋势暴露了加密安全领域攻击手段的演变以及对供应链信任的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thunk.blog/posts/types-of-types-haskell-kind-system/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Types of types: Haskell&#x27;s Kind system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Haskell 的 Kind 系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090927'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delves into the Haskell kind system, explaining how kinds classify type constructors and support higher-kinded types. It clarifies the role of kinds in enabling advanced type-level programming and ensuring kind-correctness in generic code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍 Haskell 的 Kind（类型的类型）系统，解释如何用来分类类型构造器并支撑高阶类型。通过示例，阐明 Kind 在实现高级类型级编程和保证通用代码的类型正确性中的作用。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50092722'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ESPN producer quit in protest over the sports-betting ecosystem and its impact on coverage. She says she cannot promote gambling that can cause addiction and would be complicit in harm. This underscores the ethical tension media faces when betting sponsors are embedded in sports.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名 ESPN 制作人因体育博彩生态对报道的影响而辞职，表示自己不能宣传可能导致成瘾的博彩。此事凸显了当博彩赞助深度渗透体育媒体时，记者在职业伦理与公众利益之间的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful visualizer renders regex engines as a 3D marble run, helping users see how patterns process input and what paths matches take. The project offers an intuitive way to understand nondeterminism, backtracking, and automata concepts behind regex.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的可视化项目把正则表达式的执行过程以3D大理石轨道来展示，帮助用户直观理解模式匹配时的输入处理、回溯与自动机背后的机制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50142449' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50142449' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/10/8087-addition-subtraction-microcode.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Intel 8087 chip: floating-point addition and subtraction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔 8087 浮点加减运算的微码揭秘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50142449'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article dives into the 8087 microcode path for floating-point addition and subtraction, revealing early FP unit design tricks that made real-time math feasible on x86. It connects these traces to the evolution of modern FP performance and architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入分析 8087 浮点加法/减法的微码路径，揭示早期浮点单元设计中的巧妙之处，使得 x86 上的实时浮点运算成为可能，并将这些细节与现代浮点性能及架构的演进联系起来。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50112887' data-tags='Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50112887' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1094721/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Comparing Chromium Development at Google and Igalia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 Google 与 Igalia 在 Chromium 开发上的对比分析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50112887'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative look at how Chromium development happens at Google versus Igalia, highlighting differences in governance, patch review speed, and community involvement. The piece sheds light on how corporate-backed projects balance openness with product timelines, and what open-source contributors can learn from both models. For developers, it matters for how you contribute to large, browser-level projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Google 与 Igalia 在 Chromium 开发上的分工与流程进行了对比分析，涉及治理、补丁评审速度和社区参与度等差异。其洞察有助于理解企业驱动的开源项目如何平衡商业目标与开放协作，以及开发者应如何在大型浏览器项目中贡献。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50104310' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50104310' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.signalscorps.com/blog/prompt-injection-in-http-headers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prompt injection in HTTP headers: what 217M web responses show</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HTTP 头部中的提示注入：217M 次网络响应揭示的含义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50104310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A large-scale look at prompt injection via HTTP headers shows how header-based prompts can influence results and potentially leak or manipulate responses across many sites. The findings point to practical mitigations and safer integration practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对数亿网页响应的分析，揭示了通过 HTTP 头部进行提示注入的风险及其广泛暴露，可能影响代理等获取的 AI 行为，并提出应对与防护策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.signalscorps.com/assets/social/blog-prompt-injection-in-http-headers.png' data-full='https://www.signalscorps.com/assets/social/blog-prompt-injection-in-http-headers.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50045273' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50045273' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50045273'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/15502383/55cb68bc-8b51-4f67-8fce-9b32dcd2b140' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/15502383/55cb68bc-8b51-4f67-8fce-9b32dcd2b140' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on AI&#x27;s progress but growing realization of systemic limitations. It argues for cautious deployment and awareness of failure modes as models scale and real-world use expands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 发展进程的挑衅性观察，指出其日益显现的系统性局限。主张在模型规模化和实际应用扩展的同时，保持谨慎部署并关注潜在的失效模式。</p>

The piece touches on construction choices, material palette, and what this kind of high-concept housing signals for prefab and sustainable architecture. It showcases how bold architectural experiments translate into real-world homes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>位于加州Idyllwild的Donaldson Futuro House以未来派的模块化设计为特征，强调室内外的无缝连接。文章讨论了施工选择、材料风格，以及此类高概念住宅对预制建筑和可持续居住的启示。它展示了大胆的建筑实验如何落地为现实住宅。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50088643' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50088643' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.qawolf.com/blog/how-we-solved-windows-desktop-automation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running Windows desktop tests from Linux with disposable VMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Linux 上使用一次性虚拟机运行 Windows 桌面测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088643'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The team implemented disposable Windows VMs orchestrated from Linux to run desktop automation and tests, avoiding a persistent Windows test farm. Ephemeral VMs reduce flaky state, simplify cleanup, and make CI more reproducible while letting Linux-based pipelines control GUI-heavy Windows test scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>团队通过从 Linux 控制的一次性 Windows 虚拟机来执行桌面自动化测试，避免维护长期存在的 Windows 测试集群。短暂 VM 可以减少不稳定状态、简化清理并提升 CI 的可复现性，同时让基于 Linux 的流水线管理需要 GUI 的 Windows 测试场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.qawolf.com/blog/how-we-solved-windows-desktop-automation/share-image.png' data-full='https://www.qawolf.com/blog/how-we-solved-windows-desktop-automation/share-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mac 的 Dock：从 OS 10 到 macOS 27 的演变</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50065713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of how the Mac Dock has evolved from OS X through macOS 27, tracing changes in iconography, spacing, and interaction. The post notes how design priorities shifted toward clarity, theming, and performance, and what that means for developers and workflows today. It also shows how small UI tweaks can ripple across the platform&#x27;s ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾从 OS X 到 macOS 27，Mac Dock 在图标、间距和交互等方面的演变。作者指出设计重点从美观转向清晰度、主题感与性能等方面的取舍，以及这些变化对开发者和工作流的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌全球数据中心分布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50048768'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google operates a vast, world-spanning data-center network to support search, cloud services, and AI workloads, with attention to latency, redundancy, and energy sourcing. The data-centre footprint underpins modern internet services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌运营着覆盖全球的数据中心网络，以支撑搜索、云服务和 AI 工作负载，强调低时延、冗余与能源采购。文章点出规模、区域性考量及可持续承诺和监管影响，强调数据中心是现代互联网服务的基础设施。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50028264' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/10/a-cray-1-supercomputer-replica-from-30-obsolete-mac-minis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Cray-1 supercomputer replica from 30 &quot;obsolete&quot; Mac Minis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 30 台废弃 Mac mini 复刻 Cray-1 超级计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50028264'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A maker builds a Cray-1 replica using 30 obsolete Mac Minis, turning a historic vector supercomputer into a tangible, low-cost homage. The project highlights challenges of scale, cooling, and software emulation but illustrates the charm of hardware archaeology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位发烧友用 30 台废弃的 Mac mini 复刻 Cray-1 的架构，成为一个有趣的复古项目。此举暴露出规模、散热与软件兼容等挑战，同时也凸显了硬件考古的魅力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Cray-1_Vertical_Chassis-1-810x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Cray-1_Vertical_Chassis-1-810x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aaron Ardiri has released PalmOS Lemmings, a faithful PalmOS port of the classic puzzle game Lemmings. It highlights retro game preservation and the ongoing hobbyist effort to run vintage software on aging mobile platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aaron Ardiri 发布了 PalmOS Lemmings，这是对经典解谜游戏 Lemmings 的 PalmOS 版本移植，展示了对复古游戏的保护与在老旧移动平台上运行早期软件的热情。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50111752' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50111752' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(332) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eF6bMSjPepM' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is Liquid Metal Killing Our PCs? Scientific Analysis and a 2-Year Test [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>液态金属会杀死我们的电脑吗？科学分析与两年测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50111752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50111752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50111752'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A scientific analysis and two-year test investigate the effects of liquid metal as a thermal interface material in PCs. The results highlight corrosion risks with certain metals, reliability considerations, and guidelines for safe use in AMD/Intel CPUs and GPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对液态金属作为散热界面材料的科学分析与两年测试揭示了某些金属的腐蚀风险及可靠性问题。给出在不同CPU/GPU组合中的使用建议，帮助选手正确评估是否采用液态金属散热。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/LargeModGames/spotatui' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Spotatui, a Spotify TUI/GUI without web API rate limits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Spotatui——无需 API 速率限制的 Spotify 客户端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50070010'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spotatui offers a Spotify client with no web API rate limits, using a local or alternate access path, enabling smoother playback experiences. The project raises questions about API policy, terms of service, and future reliance on official endpoints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Spotatui 提供一个无需网页 API 速率限制的 Spotify 客户端，提升播放体验并可能走近本地化访问路径，同时引发对服务条款和未来 API 变动的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sebsite.pw/w/20261009-strtod.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No portable way to detect string-to-float conversion errors in standard C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>标准 C 中没有可移植的字符串转浮点数错误检测方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50034001'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Standard C offers no portable, uniform signal when strtod fails or overflows, making robust error handling difficult across platforms. The post likely outlines practical patterns or workarounds to reliably detect conversion errors, suggesting safer parsing strategies for C libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>标准 C 未提供可移植、统一的字符串转浮点数错误信号，跨平台的错误处理变得困难。文中给出若干可行的模式或变通方案，以更可靠地检测转换错误，并提出更安全的解析策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thedrive.com/news/tesla-cannot-guarantee-roadster-reservation-holders-will-ever-get-a-car' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla &#x27;Cannot Guarantee&#x27; Roadster Reservation Holders Will Ever Get a Car</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉：不能保证 Roadster 预订者最终会提车</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141295'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla is cautious about Roadster reservations, signaling uncertain production timelines and the possibility that some buyers may never receive a vehicle. The stance reflects ongoing supply chain and manufacturing challenges for the high-end model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉对 Roadster 预订者表示谨慎，暗示产期不确定，部分买家可能永远买不到车。此举反映出高端车型在供应链与制造方面仍存在挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(336) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cell.com/matter-light/fulltext/S3117-5848(26)00119-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Programmable pigments: Photochromic nanoparticle inks for rewritable tattoos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可编程色素：用于可重写纹身的光致变色纳米颗粒墨水</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50041252'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces photochromic inks that change color under light and can be rewritten, enabling temporary or reusable tattoos. The work blends materials science with practical design ideas, hinting at durable, addressable graphics on skin.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在光照下可变色、可重写的光致变色纳米粒子墨水，可实现可重复使用的纹身。该研究将材料科学与设计应用结合，展现皮肤上可控、可重复的图形潜力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50121601'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GifCities is an archive that rescues and showcases animated GIFs and small artifacts from the Geocities era, enabling browsing and download of early web visual culture. It’s a practical reminder that much of the web’s heritage is lightweight images and page fragments that benefit from curation and preservation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GifCities 是一个档案站点，保存并展示来自 Geocities 时代的动画 GIF 和小型网页艺术品，支持浏览与下载早期网络的视觉文化。它提醒我们，网络遗产中大量价值位于轻量级图像与页面片段，值得被整理与保存。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50042370' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50042370' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(338) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2024-02-scandinavia-farmers-slaughtered-hunter-population.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scandinavia&#x27;s first farmers slaughtered hunter-gatherer population, DNA suggests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DNA 显示斯堪的纳维亚首批农民屠杀猎人-采集者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50042370'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ancient DNA suggests early farming populations replaced or violently displaced hunter-gatherers in Scandinavia, shedding light on population dynamics during Europe&#x27;s transition to agriculture. The finding informs debates on migration, culture, and conflict in prehistory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古代DNA研究显示，斯堪的纳维亚早期农民群体在向农耕社会过渡时，替代或暴力驱逐了猎人-采集者群体，为欧洲史前期人口动态的讨论提供新证据，涉及迁徙、文化与冲突。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2024/scandinavias-first-far.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2024/scandinavias-first-far.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50086363' data-tags='Hardware,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50086363' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/nvidias-first-gpu-geforce-256/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia&#x27;s first GPU: GeForce 256</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达的第一款 GPU：GeForce 256</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50086363'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on GeForce 256 examines how Nvidia&#x27;s first GPU helped define the consumer graphics market with hardware T&amp;L and a focus on gaming performance. The piece traces the architecture&#x27;s influence on subsequent GPU design and the industry&#x27;s shift toward programmable pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 GeForce 256 的回顾介绍了该卡如何通过硬件 T&amp;L 和对游戏性能的关注来塑造消费级显卡市场。文章梳理了这代架构对后续 GPU 设计的影响以及行业向可编程渲染流水线的转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/first-nvidia-gpu-social.jpg' data-full='https://dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/first-nvidia-gpu-social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 72</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50068437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DishwasherBench examines whether frontier AI models can interact with and control a real-world appliance like a dishwasher. The project highlights the practical hurdles of getting agents to bridge software planning with hardware actuation.</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RimWorld 1.7 shifts from Unity to Ludeon&#x27;s in-house Waylith engine, signaling a major engine migration for the indie studio. The change promises tighter control, potential performance gains, and easier future updates, though it may affect mod compatibility. Further patch notes will reveal how existing saves and mods are affected.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RimWorld 1.7 将引擎从 Unity 转移到公司自研的 Waylith 引擎，标志着这家独立工作室在技术栈上的重大变动。此举有望带来性能提升与更新的可控性，但可能影响模组兼容性。后续补丁说明将揭示现有存档和模组的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50082637' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(345) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sfgate.com/national-parks/article/hawaii-volcanoes-park-great-crack-22358849.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 8-mile hole in a Hawaii national park that no one can explain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>夏威夷国家公园内8英里长的裂缝尚无解释</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50082637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50082637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50082637'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists identified an 8-mile-long fracture in Hawaii&#x27;s national park that has puzzled researchers. Early observations hint at a tectonic fault or lava-driven rift, but no consensus exists yet. The mystery underscores how dynamic volcanic landscapes can evolve rapidly and why continuous monitoring matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在夏威夷国家公园发现一条长达8英里的断裂，尚无明确解释。初步观察指向地壳构造断层或与熔岩相关的裂缝，但尚无定论。此事凸显了活跃火山景观的快速演变，以及持续监测的重要性。</p>
