<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50077619' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50077619' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://parsimony.lasu.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Parsimony – an open-source benchmark for how much code AI models add</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Parsimony——一个衡量 AI 模型添加代码量的开源基准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50077619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50077619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50077619'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parsimony provides a benchmark to quantify how much code AI models generate beyond user prompts, helping assess code efficiency and potential bloat. By codifying a parsimonious metric, it enables comparisons of models on code minimalism, maintainability, and security implications of generated code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Parsimony 提供一个用于量化 AI 模型在代码生成中额外添加代码量的开源基准，帮助评估代码的高效性与膨胀风险。通过将“简约”指标系统化，它便于对比模型在代码最小化、可维护性和生成代码带来安全隐患方面的表现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://parsimony.lasu.dev/og.png' data-full='https://parsimony.lasu.dev/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://app.getsupers.com/auto-research' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Auto Research: Integer multiplication below n log n</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自动研究：低于 n log n 的整数乘法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50047664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An automated research project probes whether integer multiplication can beat the conventional n log n barrier in practice. It demonstrates how tooling can search the algorithmic space with minimal human input and surfaces claims that push the discussion on asymptotic limits. The takeaway is that automation may uncover novel ideas long ignored by intuition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个自动化研究项目在探讨整数乘法是否可能在实际情形中优于经典的 n log n 上界。它展示了通过自动化工具在最少的人为干预下探索算法空间，并引发关于渐近极限的新讨论。核心观点是自动化可能揭示被直觉忽视的创新思路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50115354' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50115354' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://softpanorama.org/OFM/Paradigm/Ch03/norton_commander.shtml' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The history of development of Norton Commander (2011)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Norton Commander 的发展史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50115354' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50115354' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50115354'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Norton Commander was a pioneer in DOS-era file management, popularizing a dual-pane interface that influenced later GUIs. The piece traces its evolution, design choices, and cultural impact on software UX in the 1980s and beyond.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Norton Commander 是 DOS 时代的文件管理先驱，以双窗格界面影响了后来的图形用户界面。文章回顾其发展历程、设计取舍及对软件用户体验的影响，展现早期PC软件的创新与风格。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://softpanorama.org/Images/splogo.gif' data-full='https://softpanorama.org/Images/splogo.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50139998'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Brain Sandwich presents a provocative reflection on cognition and AI, using a brain-as-a-sandwich metaphor to challenge common assumptions about thinking and computation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《大脑三明治》对认知与 AI 进行挑衅性反思，用“大脑如同三明治”的比喻挑战人们对思维与计算的常见假设。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50098274' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.demofox.org/2026/10/03/the-double-headed-stack-allocator/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Double Headed Stack Allocator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>双头栈分配器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50098274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50098274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50098274'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Double Headed Stack Allocator introduces a two-headed approach to stack allocation, aiming to speed up short-lived allocations and reduce fragmentation. It analyzes how splitting responsibilities between heads can optimize lifetime management for stack-based data. Practical implications for high-performance C/C++ code and potential drawbacks are discussed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>双头栈分配器提出一种双头的栈分配方式，旨在提升短期分配的速度并减少碎片。文章分析将生命周期管理职责分成两个头部来优化栈数据的分配与释放。讨论了对高性能 C/C++ 代码的现实影响及潜在的权衡与缺点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://demofox2.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/grid10x1_allocated.png?w=842' data-full='https://demofox2.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/grid10x1_allocated.png?w=842' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/johnrash/bebox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BeBox is a Haiku application that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BeBox：在 Haiku 上模拟 BeInc 的 BeBox</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50111742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50111742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50111742'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BeBox is a Haiku app that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox, allowing retro BeOS-era software to run on modern Haiku OS. It&#x27;s a niche project that showcases emulation fidelity and the continued appeal of vintage systems. For enthusiasts, it&#x27;s a glimpse into how hobbyists keep old ecosystems alive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BeBox 是一个在 Haiku 操作系统上运行的 BeBox 模拟应用，允许复古 BeOS 时代的软件在新环境中运行。该项目展示了仿真与系统级再现的潜力，也反映出爱好者对旧系统的持续热情。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0ac9af792a18a96b44e7c69e8503182cbd75658ef43dcdde1481b067499fa70e/johnrash/bebox' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0ac9af792a18a96b44e7c69e8503182cbd75658ef43dcdde1481b067499fa70e/johnrash/bebox' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50034950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://softwaredoug.com/blog/2026/10/08/bag-of-decisions.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bag of Decisions Reranker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decision 叠加重排序器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50034950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50034950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bag of Decisions reranker presents a framework to improve decision-making in models by ranking candidate decisions using a diverse feature set, aiming to outperform single-step generation. The post outlines how to combine multiple signals to select better outcomes, with practical guidance for implementing rerankers in ML pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>‘Bag of Decisions’ 重排序器提出一个通过多特征对候选决策排序的框架，旨在优于单步生成。文章阐述了如何将多种信号结合以选出更优结果，并给出在机器学习管线中实现重排序器的实用指南。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://softwaredoug.com//assets/media/2026/bag-of-decisions/image2.png' data-full='https://softwaredoug.com//assets/media/2026/bag-of-decisions/image2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50055632' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50055632' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://robotsinplainenglish.com/e/2024-01-21-gold-bug.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Gold-Bug: Edgar Allan Poe&#x27;s story that taught me about cryptography (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>金甲虫：爱伦·坡的故事教会我的密码学（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055632' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055632' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50055632'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Gold-Bug is used as a gateway to teach classical cryptography—substitution ciphers, frequency analysis, and the detective work behind codebreaking. Revisiting the story helps illustrate the historical roots of modern crypto thinking and why human patterns and context still matter in interpreting encrypted messages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《金甲虫》被用来讲解传统密码学的基础，如替换密码、频率分析以及破译背后的推理过程。重读这篇故事有助于展示现代加密思想的历史根源，并说明在解密信息时人类行为模式与语境的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://robotsinplainenglish.com/images/robots-logo.png' data-full='https://robotsinplainenglish.com/images/robots-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@timventura/akhetonics-photonic-computing-1-000x-faster-cpus-b9e91c470079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Akhetonics Photonic Computing: 1,000x Faster CPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Akhetonics 光子计算：CPU 提升一千倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50008566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50008566'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Akhetonics claims photonic computing could deliver up to 1,000x speedups for CPUs, driven by light-based processing. While the prospect is tantalizing for AI workloads and energy efficiency, real-world practicality faces material, fabrication, and integration hurdles before it becomes practical.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称 Akhetonics 的光子计算有望让 CPU 达到高达 1000x 的速度提升，依赖光信号处理。对 AI 工作负载和能效潜在影响重大，但在材料、制造与整合方面仍存挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://courk.cc/rp2350-challenge-laser' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Laser Fault Injection on a Budget: RP2350 Edition (2025)</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50101649'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates laser fault injection on a budget targeting RP2350-based hardware, outlining setup, experiments, and mitigations to protect against such attacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了针对 RP2350 硬件的低成本激光故障注入方法，并给出搭建、实验与防护措施，提醒对硬件安全的重视。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50110081' data-tags='Health,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50110081' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nltimes.nl/2026/10/15/world-first-southern-ground-hornbill-gets-3d-printed-beak-prostheses-artis-zoo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Southern ground hornbill gets 3D printed beak prostheses at Artis Zoo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艺术家动物园的南方地冠犀鸟获3D打印喙假体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50110081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50110081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50110081'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>At Artis Zoo, a Southern ground hornbill received a 3D-printed beak prosthesis, a world-first. The custom prosthesis aids feeding and daily functioning, showcasing how 3D printing enables tailored wildlife rehabilitation. This milestone could spur broader use of additive manufacturing in veterinary care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰 Artis 动物园为一只南方地冠犀鸟装上了3D打印的喙假体，创下全球首例。定制假体改善了其进食和日常功能，展示了3D 打印在野生动物康复中的潜力。此举或推动兽医领域对增材制造的更广泛应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nltimes.nl/sites/nltimes.nl/files/styles/news_article_full_desktop_2x/public/2026-10/Zuidelijke-hoornraaf-met-3D-snavel.-Foto-ARTIS-Ronald-van-Weeren_.jpg?h=e5aec6c8' data-full='https://nltimes.nl/sites/nltimes.nl/files/styles/news_article_full_desktop_2x/public/2026-10/Zuidelijke-hoornraaf-met-3D-snavel.-Foto-ARTIS-Ronald-van-Weeren_.jpg?h=e5aec6c8' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50090842' data-tags='AI,Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50090842' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1100449/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linus Torvalds&#x27;s first LLM-assisted patch series</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linus Torvalds 的首个 LLM 辅助补丁系列</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090842'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linus Torvalds submitted his first patch series that used a large language model to assist with code edits, marking a notable mainstream kernel maintainer experiment with AI tooling. The patches signal growing acceptance of LLMs as editing assistants, but they also invite scrutiny about correctness, provenance, and the need for rigorous review in critical codebases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linus Torvalds 提交了他首个使用大型语言模型辅助的补丁系列，表明核心维护者开始实验将 AI 工具用于代码编辑。此举反映出 LLM 作为辅助编辑器的日益被接纳，但也带来了关于正确性、来源以及在关键代码库中保持严格审查的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50081930' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50081930' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/is-attention-the-essence-of-intelligence-20261012/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is &#x27;Attention&#x27; the Essence of Intelligence?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>注意力是智能的本质吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50081930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quanta Magazine questions whether attention mechanisms underpin intelligence, weighing evidence from neuroscience and AI research, and explaining how attention might enable efficient processing and generalization in learning systems. The piece clarifies ongoing debates about the role of attention in artificial versus natural intelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Quanta Magazine 质疑注意力机制是否构成智能的核心，结合神经科学与 AI 研究的证据，解释注意力如何实现高效处理与学习系统的泛化能力。文章澄清了人工智能与自然智能中关于注意力作用的持续辩论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Qualia-Attention-cr.Lisett-Ledon-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Qualia-Attention-cr.Lisett-Ledon-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://store.steampowered.com/app/5426130/Tiny_Brutalism/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tiny Brutalism – Thanks to HN it&#x27;s on Steam</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tiny Brutalism 因为 HN 的热议登上 Steam</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50144144'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tiny Brutalism is on Steam thanks to Hacker News. The indie title embraces minimalist brutalist aesthetics and concise gameplay that found an audience online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立游戏《Tiny Brutalism》因 Hacker News 的热议而登上 Steam，成为人气话题。游戏以极简的 Brutalist 风格和简短节奏获得关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shared.fastly.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/5426130/6c4fbde42081a7cbeea3c423c6d80d1ea6c66b69/capsule_616x353.jpg?t=1792177083' data-full='https://shared.fastly.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/5426130/6c4fbde42081a7cbeea3c423c6d80d1ea6c66b69/capsule_616x353.jpg?t=1792177083' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50091794' data-tags='DevOps,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50091794' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/bealesh/neckbeard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neckbeard – A DevOps/platform/SRE plugin for your agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Neckbeard——为你的代理提供的 DevOps/平台/SRE 插件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50091794'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation. It aims to make agents more useful and safer in operational contexts by providing ready-made connectors and operational primitives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。它通过提供现成的连接器和操作原语，使代理在运行时管理场景中更实用且更安全。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ca21c6ab6ee9b1258c9eafbc0d8f14c8b45ccc3c871ea516343cd91dd3b536e7/bealesh/neckbeard' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ca21c6ab6ee9b1258c9eafbc0d8f14c8b45ccc3c871ea516343cd91dd3b536e7/bealesh/neckbeard' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://marcpaul.io/logs/same-job-two-engines-innodb-and-iceberg-internals/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Same Job, Two Engines: InnoDB and Iceberg Internals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>同一工作，两个引擎：InnoDB 与 Iceberg 的内部原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50071466'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical comparison of InnoDB and Iceberg internals when performing the same data processing job, highlighting architectural differences, transactional guarantees, and ideal use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对同一工作负载，InnoDB 与 Iceberg 的内部实现进行深入对比，揭示架构差异、事务保证与各自的适用场景。面向数据库管理员和数据工程师给出选型洞察。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50121562' data-tags='Programming,Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50121562' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic Console overcharged my card 100x due to internationalization input bug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 控制台因国际化输入错误将我的卡多收了 100 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50121562'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports being charged 100x by Anthropic&#x27;s console due to an internationalization input parsing bug that misinterpreted currency or decimal separators. The incident highlights risks in billing code and the importance of robust input validation, especially for global platforms handling diverse locale formats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位用户报告称因 Anthropic 控制台的国际化输入解析错误导致被多收 100 倍费用，问题出在对货币或小数分隔符的误解析。该事件凸显了计费代码的风险以及在面向全球用户的平台上对多样化本地化格式进行严格输入校验的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(374) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://onlineservices.epicgames.com/news/introducing-easy-anti-cheat-linux-v2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gaming on Linux without cheating</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 版 Easy Anti-Cheat v2：在 Linux 上实现无作弊的游戏体验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088166'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Epic Games rolls out Easy Anti-Cheat Linux v2, enabling anti-cheat support on Linux to ensure fair online play. The update marks a milestone for Linux gaming and addresses long-standing anti-cheat challenges on the platform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Epic Games 推出 Easy Anti-Cheat Linux v2，为 Linux 平台提供反作弊支持，确保在线游戏的公平性。此举标志着 Linux 游戏生态的里程碑，也缓解了长期存在的反作弊难题。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50067570' data-tags='Education,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50067570' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(375) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lemonde.fr/en/international/article/2026/10/10/denmark-set-to-allow-the-return-of-physical-force-to-maintain-order-in-schools_6758443_4.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Denmark set to allow return of &#x27;physical force&#x27; in school</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹麦拟允许在学校使用“体罚”以维持秩序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50067570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50067570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50067570'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Denmark is set to reintroduce the option of physical discipline in schools to maintain order. The move prompts debate over student rights and teacher authority, with potential implications for safety, well-being, and educational outcomes. The decision highlights how cultural norms around discipline intersect with modern education policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丹麦 将在学校重新允许使用体罚以维持秩序，引发关于学生权利与教师权威的辩论，关系到安全、福祉与教育成果。此举也暴露出不同文化对纪律的认知差异及其对教育政策的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.lemonde.fr/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' data-full='https://www.lemonde.fr/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50057030' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50057030' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://animationobsessive.substack.com/p/an-unmade-film-shaped-hayao-miyazaki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Unmade Film Shaped Hayao Miyazaki</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未完成的电影如何塑造宫崎骏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50057030'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An unfinished project from early in Miyazaki&#x27;s career shaped his later storytelling and world-building, revealing how unrealized ideas can influence a director&#x27;s signature themes. The piece connects concept art, world-building, and creative philosophy that guided Studio Ghibli&#x27;s aesthetic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一部早期未完成的电影草案影响了宫崎骏后来的讲故事方式与世界观。文章揭示未成形的设想如何在概念艺术、世界观构建与创作哲学中孕育出工作室吉卜力独有的美学基因。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7p5LxFdc-Qg' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla makes cars that fly? [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉在做飞行汽车吗？[视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50114077'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video hints that Tesla may be exploring flying car concepts, fueling discussion about feasibility and regulatory hurdles of aerial mobility. While speculative, the footage underscores ongoing interest in multi-modal mobility from major carmakers. Watch for real-world milestones before treating this as imminent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段视频暗示特斯拉在探索飞行汽车相关方向，引发关于可行性及监管障碍的热议。虽然消息多为炒作，但也凸显了汽车厂商在实现多模态出行方面的持续兴趣。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50033601' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50033601' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shnatsel.github.io/safe-simd-in-rust-even-on-the-inside/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Safe SIMD in Rust, even on the inside</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 中的安全 SIMD，即便在底层也安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50033601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50033601'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how to use SIMD in Rust with safety guarantees, addressing unsafe blocks and presenting safer abstractions or crates that expose SIMD benefits without compromising memory safety. Highlights performance wins for numeric workloads and implications for libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在 Rust 中实现带有安全保障的 SIMD，解决不安全代码的问题，并介绍更安全的抽象或库以在不牺牲内存安全的情况下获得 SIMD 性能。强调对数值工作负载的性能收益以及对库的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50135762' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50135762' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.picheta.me/post/killswitch-lang/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a programming language that LLMs can&#x27;t use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我设计了一个连 LLM 都用不了的编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50135762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50135762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50135762'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I built a programming language that LLMs can&#x27;t use, exploring design choices that frustrate model-assisted code generation. The result, Killswitch Lang, highlights challenges in tooling, language features, and safeguards for human-centric programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我设计了一个让大型语言模型无法使用的编程语言，探讨了阻碍模型辅助代码生成的设计选择。其成果强调了对编程工具、语言特性以及人为驱动开发的安全性挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.picheta.me/_astro/killswitch-banner.0YTJ74BT.png' data-full='https://blog.picheta.me/_astro/killswitch-banner.0YTJ74BT.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QH_LPimUJMs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beyond Tetris: The other games of Alexey Pajitnov [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超越俄罗斯方块：帕吉特诺夫的其他游戏 [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50136017'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Beyond Tetris highlights Pajitnov’s other puzzle games, exploring his broader design influence beyond the iconic title. The video offers context on his design philosophy and legacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《超越俄罗斯方块：帕吉特诺夫的其他游戏》聚焦他除《Tetris》之外的谜题游戏，探讨他在更广领域的设计影响。视频提供对其设计理念与遗产的背景解析。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stario.dev/apps/life' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Game of Life – But Multiplayer, Hypermedia and Python</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生命游戏：多人模式、超媒体与 Python 的结合</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50085171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50085171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50085171'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reimagined Conway&#x27;s Game of Life that supports multiplayer, hypermedia interfaces, and is implemented in Python, creating an interactive, extensible take on a classic cellular automaton.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将康威生命游戏扩展为多人模式、超媒体界面，核心实现采用 Python，打造一个互动且可扩展的经典细胞自动机版本。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50087616' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50087616' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sklar.rocks/non-determinism-doesnt-matter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Non-Determinism Doesn&#x27;t Matter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>非确定性并不重要</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50087616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50087616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50087616'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that for many modern software systems, non-determinism isn&#x27;t a critical obstacle because of robust design and fault-tolerant patterns. The piece emphasizes that managing uncertainty effectively matters more than forcing determinism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《非确定性并不重要》一文认为，在现代软件系统中，非确定性并非关键障碍，得益于鲁棒设计和容错模式。文章强调对不确定性的管理比盲目追求确定性更关键。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50129274' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50129274' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nos.nl/video/2635315-wildcamera-s-tonen-hoe-zwijnen-en-herten-zich-aanpassen-aan-de-wolf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;Tame&quot; boar and deer are instinctively adapting to the wolf in the Netherlands</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰野猪与鹿在狼的存在下本能地调整行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50129274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50129274'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wild boar and deer instinctively alter their behavior as wolves return to the Dutch landscape, shifting movement patterns and habitat use to avoid predation. Wildlife cameras capture these rapid adaptations, illustrating how predators reshape prey behavior even before formal conservation policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着狼在荷兰回归，野猪和鹿本能地改变活动范围与出没路径以躲避捕食者。野生动物监控镜头记录了这些快速的适应，显示捕食者在未制定具体管理措施前就已影响猎物行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.cdn.nos.nl/8/D/r/i/A/6/kQCcBd57XxHrCbV5XHmAcMEM73p9GiE5Twz91hH/3x2x1344x756-1024x576.webp' data-full='https://images.cdn.nos.nl/8/D/r/i/A/6/kQCcBd57XxHrCbV5XHmAcMEM73p9GiE5Twz91hH/3x2x1344x756-1024x576.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50115144' data-tags='Finance,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50115144' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coached.com/essays/the-insider-trading-youre-allowed-to-do/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The insider trading you&#x27;re allowed to do</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你被允许做的内幕交易</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50115144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50115144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50115144'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues there are legally permitted forms of trading around confidential information under current rules, exposing a gray area in finance and markets. The piece challenges readers to rethink what is considered &#x27;insider trading&#x27; and why some practices slip through the cracks. It also reflects on the incentives created by loopholes and the need for clearer regulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭示在现行法规下仍存在被视为内幕交易的合法边界，指出规则漏洞与监管盲点。作者呼吁对相关规定进行改革，以减少市场滥用的空间。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50133830'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft and Carnegie Mellon University have teamed up to train early-career software engineers, offering mentorship, practical skills, and industry-ready programs to bridge academia and the workforce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软与卡内基梅隆大学联合推出针对初级软件工程师的培训计划，提供实战技能、导师制以及对接产业的职业发展机会，缩小学术与行业的差距。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.avclub.com/epic-games-disney-ai-delay' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Epic Games tells employees to use AI to get Disney game done</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50046022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50046022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50046022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Arena allocators in C explain a memory allocation strategy that allocates in large zones, improving speed and locality at the cost of fragmentation and lifetime management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C 语言中的 Arena 分配器介绍了一种按大区域分配内存的策略，提升分配速度与局部性，但需权衡碎片化与生命周期管理。</p>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As market selloffs and rising rates pressure financing, the AI data center deal boom faces potential headwinds, affecting valuations, capex, and deployment timelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>股市抛售与利率上升对 AI 数据中心交易热潮施压，可能影响估值、资本支出和部署时间表。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.gentoo.org/mgorny/2026/10/12/a-plea-not-to-use-uv-build/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A plea not to use uv-build</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>请勿使用 uv-build</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50076048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50076048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50076048'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative critique urges developers to avoid uv-build, citing concerns over reliability or maintainability. The post argues established build pipelines are preferable for reproducible results and long-term security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 uv-build 的强烈抵制呼吁认为其可靠性与可维护性不足，强调使用成熟的构建流程能带来更好的可重复性与长期安全。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pi.website/blog/api-beta' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Physical Intelligence API Beta: Frontier Models for Every Robot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Physical Intelligence API Beta：为每个机器人提供的前沿模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50079437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50079437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Beta introduces a Physical Intelligence API aimed at enabling embodied AI for robotics, with frontier models designed to handle perception, planning, and actuation. It promises a standardized interface to accelerate robotics development and edge deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该 Beta 版本推出了 Physical Intelligence API，旨在让机器人具备具身智能，结合前沿模型处理感知、规划与执行任务。通过标准化接口，它有望加速机器人开发并支持边缘部署。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vacuula/vacuula' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Custom server to revive neato bots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自定义服务器让 Neato 机器人重新工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50097257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50097257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50097257'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source project provides a custom server to revive Neato robots by replacing or augmenting the official backend, enabling continued control and firmware updates. This demonstrates how community-driven hardware hacking can extend the life of consumer IoT devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源项目提供自定义服务器，通过替代或增强官方后端，让 Neato 机器人继续被控制并获得固件更新，展现了社区驱动的硬件改造如何延长消费物联网设备的寿命。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50118662' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50118662' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/epdf/10.1002/aaai.70113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to make System 1 LLMs show System 2 competencies?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何让系统1 的 LLM 展现系统2 的能力？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50118662'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers propose methods to elicit System 2-style reasoning from System 1 language models, combining prompting, architecture tweaks, or training to improve deliberative accuracy. The work explores improving reliability and long-horizon planning in LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者提出通过提示设计、架构调整或再训练等方式，让系统1 的语言模型呈现更具系统2 的推理能力，提高其可靠性和长时序规划的表现。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50075392' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50075392' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nhm.ac.uk/discover/news/2021/december/worlds-largest-eagle-hunted-unlike-any-other-bird-of-prey.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The largest eagle hunted unlike any other bird of prey (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>世界上最大的鹰以独特猎食方式猎捕猎物（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50075392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50075392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50075392'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece discusses the hunting behavior of the world&#x27;s largest eagle, likely Haast&#x27;s eagle, which hunted large prey such as moa in a distinctive way, offering paleoecological insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文探讨世界上体型最大的鹰（很可能是豪斯特鹰）以独特方式猎捕如 moa 等大型猎物的行为，提供对古生态与捕食者—猎物关系的见解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nhm.ac.uk/content/dam/nhm-www/discover/haasts-eagle/haasts-eagle-reconstruction-moa-skeleton-full-width-kenny-and-nhm.jpg' data-full='https://www.nhm.ac.uk/content/dam/nhm-www/discover/haasts-eagle/haasts-eagle-reconstruction-moa-skeleton-full-width-kenny-and-nhm.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50087337' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50087337' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/teaching-birds-lost-songs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists are teaching birds to sing lost songs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家教鸟儿重唱失落的歌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50087337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50087337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50087337'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers are teaching birds to sing dialects or song variants that have disappeared from local populations by using playback, tutoring, and social cues, aiming to restore cultural song traditions. This work has implications for conservation biology and reintroduction programs because learned vocalizations affect mate choice and territorial behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家通过回放、辅导与社会提示，教会鸟类那些在本地种群中已消失的方言或歌声变体，旨在恢复文化性歌唱传统。由于声学学习会影响配偶选择与领域行为，这项工作对保护生物学与物种再引入计划具有实际意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Rufous-Collared-Sparrow_Sharp.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1791980521&amp;s=8004f631ec61a6b1491b17de4771e115' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Rufous-Collared-Sparrow_Sharp.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1791980521&amp;s=8004f631ec61a6b1491b17de4771e115' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50081133' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50081133' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/10/honey-bee-stingers-are-double-piston-venom-pumps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>High-speed video reveals how honey bee stingers pump venom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高速视频揭示蜜蜂刺针如何泵送毒液</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50081133'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A high-speed video study uncovers the mechanics of how honey bee stingers deliver venom, revealing a two-piston pumping action that rapidly injects venom. The finding has implications for biomechanics, insect physiology, and could inspire bio-inspired design in sensors or microfluidics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高速成像研究揭示蜜蜂刺针释放毒液的力学过程，显示出双活塞式泵送的快速注 venom 机制。这一发现对生物力学、昆虫生理学具有启示，也可能激发生物仿生设计在传感器或微流控领域的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/bee-TOP-1152x648-1791731084.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/bee-TOP-1152x648-1791731084.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50057119' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.plover.com/prog/git/history-proposal.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maybe a better way to track the release history of a Git repo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>跟踪 Git 仓库发行历史的或更好的方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50057119'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal for improving how release history is tracked in Git repositories, potentially by introducing structured release records, conventional commits, or automated changelogs. The idea aims to make it easier to audit, reference, and revert changes across projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种改进 Git 仓库发行历史追踪的方法，可能包括结构化发行记录、约定式提交或自动变更日志等做法，旨在提升审计、引用和跨项目回溯的便利性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pic.blog.plover.com/prog/git/history-proposal/current-practice.svg' data-full='https://pic.blog.plover.com/prog/git/history-proposal/current-practice.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50082358' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.historytoday.com/archive/history-matters/tudor-life-time-plague' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Tudor life in the time of plague (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瘟疫时期的都铎生活</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50082358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50082358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50082358'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of everyday life in Tudor England during plague outbreaks. It covers how towns responded with quarantines and public health measures, and how disease shaped work, family life, and the economy. The piece connects historical hardships to present-day lessons on resilience and public health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文聚焦都铎时代英国在瘟疫流行时的日常生活，介绍城镇如何实施隔离与公共卫生措施，以及疾病对家庭、就业和经济的影响。它展示了瘟疫如何改变社会结构与日常实践，并与当代公共卫生经验形成对照。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50086838' data-tags='Hardware,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50086838' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://va3ndf.com/blog/hacker-badges/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making 1,750 programmable PCB badges for Canada&#x27;s biggest hackathon</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50086838'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A massive production run produced 1,750 programmable PCB badges for Canada&#x27;s largest hackathon. The effort highlights hardware accessibility for educators and students, and the challenges of scaling hardware distribution for large events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为加拿大最大规模的黑客松制作了1,750枚可编程 PCB 徽章，凸显了在教育和学生群体中普及硬件的努力以及大规模活动的分发挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50082113' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50082113' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/10/13/us/nyc-horse-carriage-ban' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New York City&#x27;s iconic horse-drawn carriages expected to take their last trot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市标志性马车或将进入最后一趟巡游</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50082113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50082113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50082113'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports NYC&#x27;s horse-drawn carriages facing an end due to regulatory changes or bans, signaling a shift in urban policy and tourism aesthetics. The story weighs heritage preservation against animal welfare, noise, and modern transit needs, and notes the operators&#x27; adaptation plans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市拟取消马车运营，标志性的马车行业可能迎来末日巡游。文章权衡了传统文化遗产与动物福利、城市交通优化的矛盾，并报道运营者的转型计划。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50078691' data-tags='Open Source,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50078691' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.zdnet.com/business/dutch-tax-authorities-cancel-microsoft-365-cloud-rollout/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dutch tax authorities cancel Microsoft 365 cloud rollout and turn to open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰税务机关取消 Microsoft 365 云端部署，转向开源解决方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50078691'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dutch tax authorities scrapped a planned Microsoft 365 cloud rollout and pivoted to open-source alternatives. The decision signals growing scrutiny of vendor lock-in, data sovereignty, and total cost of ownership in government IT. It could spur more government uptake of open-source tools and influence cloud strategy in other jurisdictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰税务机关取消计划中的 Microsoft 365 云端部署，改用开源替代方案。此举反映了对供应商锁定、数据主权与总拥有成本的日益关注，影响政府 IT 策略。此案例可能促使其他政府机构在云策略上更多考虑开源工具的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.zdnet.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/GettyImages-1222986158.jpg' data-full='https://www.zdnet.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/GettyImages-1222986158.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50088767' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50088767' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jedrzej.maczan.pl/2025_11_21_dp_knapsack_sliding_hirschberg' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>0/1 Knapsack Problem in PyTorch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 PyTorch 中实现的 0/1 背包问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088767'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This PyTorch-focused post demonstrates a dynamic programming solution to the 0/1 knapsack problem using tensor operations, and discusses memory-efficient approaches like a sliding window and Hirschberg optimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 PyTorch 的上下文中，演示了使用张量运算实现 0/1 背包问题的动态规划，并探讨了滑动窗口等内存高效的优化方法，甚至涉及 Hirschberg 优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jedrzej.maczan.pl/knapsack.png' data-full='https://jedrzej.maczan.pl/knapsack.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50100440' data-tags='Security,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50100440' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://humanpro.co/articles/we-still-use-paper/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We Still Use Paper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们仍在使用纸张</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50100440'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite digital tools, paper remains stubbornly present in many workflows due to concerns about security, auditability, or cultural familiarity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管数字工具普及，纸张在许多工作流程中仍屡屡出现，原因包括安全性、可审计性以及文化习惯等。文章探讨了纸张在某些场景中的优势，以及向数字系统过渡时面临的障碍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://humanpro.co/media/images/We_Still_Use_Paper.jpg' data-full='https://humanpro.co/media/images/We_Still_Use_Paper.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50088937' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50088937' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sfchronicle.com/california/article/yosemite-national-park-highway-140-22463847.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California is building a $400M shield for Yosemite&#x27;s most dangerous highway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州为优胜美地最危险公路投资4亿美元建防护工程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088937'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California is funding a $400M project to reinforce Highway 140 into Yosemite, incorporating rockfall barriers, improved drainage, and roadway widening. The upgrade aims to reduce closures and accidents at a park gateway that handles heavy seasonal traffic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州正在为通往优胜美地国家公园的公路140段投入4亿美元的加固工程，包含防坠石屏障、排水改进和路面扩建。该升级旨在减少因事故和自然灾害导致的封闭与事故，改善公园入口处的交通安全。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sfchronicle.com/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' data-full='https://www.sfchronicle.com/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ClickHouse/clickhouse-c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ClickHouse C – Talk to ClickHouse using its native binary protocol</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ClickHouse C：用原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 通信</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50049003'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Announces a C library that talks to ClickHouse using the database&#x27;s native binary protocol, reducing overhead and simplifying integration from C applications. This streamlines high-performance data ingestion and querying for C ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>推出一个用于 C 的 ClickHouse 客户端库，通过原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 交互，降低开销并简化在 C 应用中的集成，提升数据写入与查询效率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tommyb3ll.github.io/FlowCE/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FlowCE: Calculus on your TI-84 Plus CE</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50033486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FlowCE reimagines TI-84 Plus CE as a calculus playground, delivering symbolic and numeric computation on a classic calculator. The project highlights how enduring educational hardware can be extended with modern software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FlowCE 让 TI-84 Plus CE 成为一个微积分平台，支持符号计算与绘图等功能，体现教育硬件的持续创新与再利用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(413) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aaronsonoracle.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Aaronson Oracle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿伦森神谕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50086484'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Aaronson Oracle is a playful project inspired by Scott Aaronson’s work that answers complexity- and theory-themed questions, blending humor with serious theoretical insight. It serves as both an educational toy and a pointer to deeper discussions in computational complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“阿伦森神谕”是一个受 Scott Aaronson 启发的趣味项目，回答与复杂性理论相关的问题，既有幽默成分也包含严肃的理论见解。它既是个教学性质的玩具，也能引导人进入更深入的计算复杂性讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aaronsonoracle.com/og-card.png' data-full='https://aaronsonoracle.com/og-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50123356' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50123356' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.themarshallproject.org/2026/10/16/911-book-policing-mental-health' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Fatal Flaw of 911</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>911 的致命缺陷</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50123356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50123356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50123356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The current 911 system funnels many behavioral-health and crisis calls toward police rather than specialized responders, producing harm through criminalization and inappropriate responses. Fixing this requires dispatch triage, alternatives to armed responders, and investment in community-based mental-health crisis teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现行的 911 系统把大量行为健康和危机求助转给警察，导致刑事化和不当应对带来伤害。要解决问题需要改进调度分流、提供非武装应对选项，并投入社区级的心理健康危机处置团队。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bark is a toll-free BBS for 1990s teens, offering a nostalgic look at pre-internet-era online communities and early social networking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bark 是专为90年代青少年设计的免话费 BBS，展现了拨号上网时代的在线社区与早期社交网络的风貌及其文化影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了使用 Unsiloed 与 TypeSafe Jev 的文档审阅工作流，强调结构化数据与类型安全处理如何提升准确性与可重复性。此组合体现了提升文档处理效率的实用 AI 工具链。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50095034' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/uk-news/2026/oct/14/sally-rooney-says-she-can-no-longer-sell-books-in-the-uk-because-of-palestine-action-support' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sally Rooney can no longer sell books in UK due to Palestine Action support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨莉·鲁尼因巴勒斯坦行动支持在英国无法出售书籍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50095034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50095034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50095034'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sally Rooney says she can no longer sell books in the UK due to Palestine Action support, illustrating how activist actions can affect publishing and distribution. The stance prompts broader questions about free expression, marketplace access, and the pressures authors face amid political campaigning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>萨莉·鲁尼表示因巴勒斯坦行动的支持，她在英国无法出售书籍。这一立场凸显了政治行动对出版与发行的影响以及作者在政治运动环境下面对的压力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50082021' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://strafe.chat/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Strafe: A new open-source, E2E Encrypted, and federated alternative to Discord</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Strafe：一个开源、端对端加密、联邦化的 Discord 替代方案</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软 AI 的 Frontier 预训练计划标志着在多样数据上的大规模预训练以及提升模型能力与安全性的技术路径。该举措凸显行业向更具基础性、可复用的模型构建块迈进。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/graphicle-ai/graphicle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Graphicle, a Rust port of Graphify with memory for coding agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Graphicle——Graphify 的 Rust 移植，带有编码代理记忆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088713'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Graphicle is a Rust port of Graphify that adds a memory model tailored for coding agents, enabling them to retain context and past decisions during multi-step programming tasks. Implementing this in Rust emphasizes performance and safety, making it attractive for systems that need reliable, low-latency agent-assisted coding workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Graphicle 是 Graphify 的 Rust 移植版，增加了为编码代理设计的记忆模型，使代理在多步骤编程任务中能保留上下文与历史决策。用 Rust 实现强调性能与安全，适合需要可靠、低延迟代理辅助编码的系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c6523df5ec58df2111834dc961032c6aef9dcb157f9ea915f43421947be94ddc/Graphicle-AI/graphicle' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c6523df5ec58df2111834dc961032c6aef9dcb157f9ea915f43421947be94ddc/Graphicle-AI/graphicle' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marshall_Islands_stick_chart' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Marshall Islands Stick Chart</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马绍尔群岛的棒状图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50078757'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stick charts are an ancient navigational tool used by Marshall Islanders to memorize sea routes. The post explains how they worked and what they reveal about traditional seafaring knowledge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>棒状图是马绍尔群岛人用于记忆海上航线的古老导航工具。本文介绍它们的工作原理，以及对传统航海知识的启示。</p>
