<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122920' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122920' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://words.filippo.io/seeds-solved/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The NIST Elliptic Curves Seeds Bounty Has Been Claimed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NIST 椭圆曲线种子赏金被认领</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122920'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The bounty to reveal the seeds used in NIST elliptic-curve parameter generation has been claimed, resolving long-standing opacity about how those curves were chosen. Confirming the seeds reduces one major source of suspicion about possible backdoors in standardized curves and improves transparency for cryptographic trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于揭示 NIST 椭圆曲线参数生成时所用种子的赏金已被认领，解决了长期存在的参数选择不透明问题。确认这些种子消除了标准曲线可能存在后门的一个重要疑点，有助于提升密码学信任的透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/fc811646-65b3-4c41-9a2c-ff68584f75b5.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/fc811646-65b3-4c41-9a2c-ff68584f75b5.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brookslybrand.com/posts/do-frameworks-matter-anymore/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do Frameworks Matter Anymore?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>框架还重要吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50081092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50081092'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflection on the current landscape of software development: frameworks still matter for productivity, abstraction, and maintainability, but developers increasingly use modular libraries and tooling, opting for lighter, more flexible stacks. The piece weighs tradeoffs between opinionated frameworks and bespoke toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对现代软件开发格局的思考：框架仍然提高生产力、抽象能力与可维护性，但开发者越来越偏好模块化库和工具，选择更轻量、灵活的技术栈。文章权衡了框架的方便性与定制工具链之间的取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50133736' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50133736' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(428) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.scottstevenson.net/p/pattern-matching-beats-narrative' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pattern matching beats narrative reasoning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>模式匹配胜过叙事推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50133736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50133736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50133736'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pattern matching can outperform narrative reasoning in certain AI tasks by offering clearer structure, fewer brittle inferences, and easier verification. The post argues that focusing on pattern-driven approaches may improve reliability and explainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在某些 AI 任务中，模式匹配因结构更清晰、推理不易出错且更易验证而胜过叙事推理。文中主张，转向模式驱动的方法可能提升系统的可靠性和可解释性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hM0_!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9551e2e7-d576-4bd2-a781-dbaf96f8dbc0_2400x1350.heic' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hM0_!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9551e2e7-d576-4bd2-a781-dbaf96f8dbc0_2400x1350.heic' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50104783' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50104783' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(429) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.openrewrite.org/reference/latest-versions-of-every-openrewrite-module' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recipes Are Moving from Maven Central to the Code Genome Project</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>配方从 Maven Central 迁移到 Code Genome Project</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50104783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50104783'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRewrite&#x27;s automated code transformation recipes are moving from Maven Central to the Code Genome Project, centralizing distribution and versioning. The shift could improve auditability, security, and discoverability for teams relying on automated refactors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRewrite 的代码转换配方正从 Maven Central 迁移至 Code Genome Project，集中管理与版本控制。此举有望提升审计、安全性与可发现性，特别对依赖自动化重构的团队有帮助。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://docs.openrewrite.org/img/moderne-poster-logo.svg' data-full='https://docs.openrewrite.org/img/moderne-poster-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50088447' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50088447' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brooker.co.za:443/blog/2026/10/04/encoders.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Strands Decider: Why Not an Encoder?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Strands 决策器：为什么不用编码器？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50088447'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues for decoder-style architectures in the &#x27;Strands Decider&#x27; design, explaining tradeoffs that make decoders better suited for certain sequential decision problems than encoders. The post breaks down performance, flexibility, and training considerations that motivated choosing one architecture over the other.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在“Strands 决策器”中论证了为何采用解码器式架构，阐明在某些序列决策问题中解码器相较于编码器的权衡优势。文章解析了性能、灵活性和训练方面的考量，这些因素促成了架构选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(442) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thing.net/~rdom/ecd/EDTECD.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Electronic Disturbance Theater and Electronic Civil Disobedience (1998)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys the Electronic Disturbance Theater and its use of networked art to provoke digital civil disobedience, exploring legal, cultural, and rights-based implications for online protest.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章回顾电子干扰剧场及其通过网络艺术进行数字公民抗命的行动，讨论此类在线抗议在法律、文化与数字权利方面的影响与边界。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(444) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patreon.com/vihart/posts/vi-harts-big-171939430' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vi Hart&#x27;s Big Navier-Stokes Post</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vi Hart 的 Navier–Stokes 大作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50067098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50067098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50067098'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vi Hart publishes a long form exploration of the Navier–Stokes equations, offering intuitive explanations of fluid dynamics and turbulence. The piece combines visuals and math to make PDEs accessible, illustrating why this area remains central to both science and computation. It&#x27;s a resource for educators and curious technologists alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vi Hart 发布了关于 Navier–Stokes 方程的长文，提供对流体动力学与湍流的直观解释。文章结合可视化与数学推导，让偏微分方程的核心思想更易理解，凸显此领域在科学与计算中的重要性。对教育工作者和技术爱好者都具有参考价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50108491' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(446) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hamishcampbell.com/we-are-entering-a-period-of-overlapping-crises/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We are entering a period of overlapping crises</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们正进入一个叠加危机的时期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108491' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108491' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50108491'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that multiple global crises—climate, energy, geopolitics—are converging, stressing the need for resilient technology, cross-border collaboration, and adaptable policy. It frames tech as a lever for managing complexity rather than a standalone solution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出气候、能源、地缘政治等多重危机会同時叠加，强调需要韧性技术、跨境协作和灵活的政策来应对。将科技视为应对复杂性的重要杠杆，而非单独的解决方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hamishcampbell.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/smash-the-state-764x1024.jpeg' data-full='https://hamishcampbell.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/smash-the-state-764x1024.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article revisits vintage travel clocks, exploring their mechanical charm, portability, and the rituals around packing timepieces for trips. It offers design reflections relevant to portable electronics and the enduring appeal of tactile, mechanical time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文重新审视复古旅行钟，探索其机械魅力、便携性与出行时的使用仪式。讨论围绕可携式电子产品的设计启示，以及触觉、机械时计的恒久魅力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50124642' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neuralwatt.com/news/why-harnesses-matter-for-energy-efficiency' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why harnesses matter for energy (and token) efficiency</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何高效的能量管理策略对能源与令牌效率重要</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50124642'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Efficient harnesses, meaning energy management strategies and workflow optimizations, matter for both energy and token efficiency in AI workloads. The piece outlines approaches like dynamic voltage/frequency scaling, hardware-software co-design, and efficient tokenization to reduce energy per operation and the cost of processing tokens.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，提升能源与令牌效率取决于有效的能量管理策略和工作流优化。通过动态电压/频率调整、软硬件协同设计以及高效的令牌化方案等方法，可以显著降低单位运算能耗与 AI 工作负载的成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69451759a4952bcf865fbb2d/6acec5062fef8892df4256e2_6acec50428ae0c788db4d04a_harness-energy-v4-hero-harness-energy.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69451759a4952bcf865fbb2d/6acec5062fef8892df4256e2_6acec50428ae0c788db4d04a_harness-energy-v4-hero-harness-energy.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50081124'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50102480' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50102480' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(452) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://math.pku.edu.cn/teachers/jiangm/courses/CG/Lectures/spline-history.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A History of Curves and Surfaces in Computer Aided Geometric Design [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计算机辅助几何设计中的曲线与曲线史（PDF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50102480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50102480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50102480'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical survey of curves and surfaces in CAD/CAM, tracing the development from Bezier curves to B-splines and NURBS, with implications for modern design pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 CAD/CAM 中曲线与曲面的历史进行梳理，从 Bezier 曲线到 B 样条再到 NURBS，揭示对当代设计流程的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50108850' data-tags='Science,Health,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50108850' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/10/microplastics-in-water-arent-inert-and-make-damaging-chemicals-on-their-own/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microplastics in water aren&#x27;t inert and make damaging chemicals on their own</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>水中的微塑料并非惰性，会自行生成有害化学物质</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50108850'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show microplastics in water can react to form harmful chemicals, challenging the assumption that they are inert carriers for pollutants. The result has implications for water treatment, environmental policy, and public health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究表明水中的微塑料并非惰性本身，它们还能自行生成有害化学物质，这改变了“微塑料只是载体”的认知。对水处理、环境政策和公共健康都具有重大影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-1922961716-2560x1440.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-1922961716-2560x1440.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50060923' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50060923' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.itsnicethat.com/features/david-rudnick-johnnie-campbell-arsenal-rocastle-typeface-graphic-design-spotlight-121026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>David Rudnick on &quot;borderline impossible&quot; process of designing Arsenal typeface</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>David Rudnick 谈 Arsenal 字体设计的几乎不可能过程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50060923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article delves into the arduous design process behind Arsenal, a typeface pushed to its limits by ligatures, multi-script support, and aesthetic consistency. Rudnick reveals the almost impossibly strict constraints and iterative challenges that typographers face when producing a production-ready font.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入探讨 Arsenal 字体设计的艰苦过程——在连字、多语言支持与审美一致性之间不断推动极限。Rudnick 展示了在制作可投入生产的字体时所遇到的几乎不可能的严格约束与迭代挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://m.itsnicethat.com/original_images/Arsenal_Rocastle_16x9_001.png?class=w1440' data-full='https://m.itsnicethat.com/original_images/Arsenal_Rocastle_16x9_001.png?class=w1440' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50117935' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50117935' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/superoptimizing-an-fpu-emulator/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Superoptimizing an FPU Emulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>极致优化 FPU 仿真器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50117935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50117935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50117935'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post dives into aggressive optimizations for a floating-point unit emulator, including low-level bit tricks, caching strategies, and algorithmic rewrites to maximize speed without sacrificing accuracy. A vivid case study in emulator performance tuning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入探讨对 FPU 仿真器进行极致优化的技巧，包括底层位运算、缓存策略和算法改写，在不牺牲准确性的前提下尽可能提升速度，成为仿真性能调优的生动案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/superoptimizing-an-fpu-emulator/og.png' data-full='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/superoptimizing-an-fpu-emulator/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50090484' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50090484' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openstation.blog/2026/10/13/i-deleted-168000-lines-of-comments-my-ai-agent-didnt-notice/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Deleted 168,000 Lines of Comments. My AI Agent Didn&#x27;t Notice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我删除了 168,000 行注释。我的 AI 代理没有注意到</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50090484'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author deleted 168k lines of comments from a codebase and observed that their AI agent didn&#x27;t flag the change, exposing a blind spot: agents often prioritize executable behavior over non-executable documentation. The incident warns that relying solely on agents can miss critical context, and that comments and documentation remain important signals for human understanding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者从代码库中删除了 168,000 行注释，发现其 AI 代理并未注意到这一变动，暴露了代理重视可执行行为而忽略非可执行文档的盲点。此事提醒我们单靠代理可能会错过重要上下文，注释与文档仍然是人类理解的重要信号。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/openstation.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/openstation-og-share.png?fit=1200%2C630&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/openstation.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/openstation-og-share.png?fit=1200%2C630&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/public-benefit-fund' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investing in the Public Benefit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>投入公共利益基金</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50143755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50143755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50143755'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic launches a Public Benefit Fund to support initiatives that advance public welfare and AI safety beyond core commercial products. The fund invites researchers and nonprofits to collaborate, signaling a shift toward responsible AI stewardship and long-term societal impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 推出公共利益基金，资助提升公共福祉与人工智能安全的项目，超越核心商业产品的范畴。基金面向研究机构和非营利组织开放合作，标志着在负责任的 AI 治理与长期社会影响方面的努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/85648c93880ac27347f8e6a4008650a65c12ede8-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/85648c93880ac27347f8e6a4008650a65c12ede8-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sparcopen.org/news/2026/sparc-releases-cost-of-knowledge-accounting-analysis-profitability-report/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Public Pays to Produce Research. Shareholders Collect the Return</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>公众为研究付费，股东却收回报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50139636'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SPARC&#x27;s analysis argues that publicly funded research often generates profits that accrue to shareholders, calling for policy changes to better align public investment with broad social benefit.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SPARC 的分析指出 publicly funded 的研究常产生股东受益的利润，呼吁通过政策调整让公共投资更广泛地惠及社会。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eli.thegreenplace.net/2026/notes-on-the-complex-roots-of-unity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Notes on the Complex Roots of Unity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单位根的复数笔记</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50138002'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Notes on complex roots of unity discuss geometric placement on the unit circle, basic algebraic properties, and connections to Fourier analysis and cyclotomic polynomials. The piece clarifies how symmetry and roots underpin many discrete-transform techniques.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾单位根的几何分布与基本代数性质，并揭示它们与傅里叶分析、循环多项式等的联系。通过直观讲解，阐明对称性在离散变换中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.ibiblio.org/eldritch/nhb/paperc/intro.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Voyage of the Paper Canoe: 2500 Miles from Quebec to the Gulf of Mexico, 1874-5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纸船之旅：1874-75 从魁北克到墨西哥湾的2500英里航行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50108165'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical account of a 2,500-mile paper canoe expedition across North America in 1874-75, highlighting ingenuity and endurance of explorers in lightweight craft. The journey sheds light on historical mobility and exploration tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述1874–75 年间一艘纸质独木舟横跨北美的2500英里航行，突出探险家在轻量化船体上的智慧与毅力。此行为了解历史上的移动性与探险技术提供了新视角。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.ibiblio.org/eldritch/nhb/paperc/images/previous.gif' data-full='http://www.ibiblio.org/eldritch/nhb/paperc/images/previous.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50109642' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50109642' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.independent.co.uk/news/uk/politics/bbc-licence-fee-charter-youtube-tax-netflix-disney-b3067500.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BBC licence fee to be extended with &#x27;YouTube tax&#x27; to force more to pay</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BBC 许可费将通过“YouTube 税”扩大覆盖，以迫使更多人缴费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50109642'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The proposal would expand BBC funding by applying a streaming platform levy, effectively charging more households for content regardless of whether they watch live TV. It stirs debate over public broadcasting funding, digital equity, and enforcement across platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提案通过对流媒体平台征收“在线视频税”，扩大 BBC 财政覆盖，使更多家庭为内容付费，而不仅限于传统电视观看。此举引发对公共广播资金、数字公平及跨平台征收的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.independent.co.uk/2026/09/23/11/1365172587..?trim=0,0,0,1&amp;width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' data-full='https://static.independent.co.uk/2026/09/23/11/1365172587..?trim=0,0,0,1&amp;width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cbirds：在你的终端里飞翔的一群鸟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50036023'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cbirds is a playful terminal project that renders a flock of birds in the terminal, likely using Unicode/ASCII art or simple graphics. It adds a whimsical visual element to the command line experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cbirds 是一个有趣的终端项目，在命令行中呈现一群飞翔的鸟子，通常使用 Unicode/ASCII 或简单图形。为日常的命令行体验增添一抹趣味。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50062575' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50062575' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elizagen.org/genealogy.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Genealogy of Eliza</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Eliza 的家谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50062575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50062575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50062575'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The genealogy maps the lineage of Eliza and its descendants, showing how early conversational agents spawned generations of chatbots and influenced design patterns in dialog systems. It highlights which ideas persisted, which evolved, and how historical artifacts still shape modern conversational AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该家谱描绘了 Eliza 及其后续系统的谱系，展示早期对话代理如何催生多代聊天机器人并影响对话系统的设计范式。文中指出哪些理念被保留、哪些发生了演进，以及历史遗产如何继续影响现代会话 AI。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://elizagen.org/genealogy/paper/ELIZAGen/eliza_tree.png' data-full='https://elizagen.org/genealogy/paper/ELIZAGen/eliza_tree.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122387' data-tags='Politics,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50122387' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(464) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/chicago-area-cops-helped-hsi-spy-on-minnesota-anti-ice-activists/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chicago-Area Cops Helped HSI Spy on Minnesota Anti-ICE Activists</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>芝加哥地区警察协助 HSI 监视明尼苏达反 ICE 活动者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122387'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chicago-area police provided assistance that helped Homeland Security Investigations surveil anti-ICE activists in Minnesota, revealing cross-jurisdictional cooperation on domestic political surveillance. The episode raises civil-liberties concerns about information sharing, scope creep in investigations, and transparency around policing political activity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>芝加哥地区警方协助使国土安全调查局得以监视明尼苏达的反 ICE 活动者，曝光了跨辖区合作用于国内政治监控的情况。此事引发对公民自由的担忧，涉及信息共享的范围扩大、调查职能的蔓延以及对政治活动警务透明度的质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6acf9d6f3ad68bc4076ddf49/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/GettyImages-2296548226.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6acf9d6f3ad68bc4076ddf49/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/GettyImages-2296548226.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50144175' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50144175' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.peterp.me/articles/personal-software-factories-are-a-waste-of-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Personal software factories are a waste of time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>个人软件工厂是浪费时间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50144175'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative essay argues that building personal software factories—automating personal tooling and code—is inefficient; instead, embrace outsourcing or platform-level tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章批评打造个人软件工厂的想法，认为为个人工作流投入时间与资源收益有限。作者建议转向可复用的工具、平台和开源生态以提升长期生产力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.peterp.me/og/personal-software-factories-are-a-waste-of-time.png' data-full='https://www.peterp.me/og/personal-software-factories-are-a-waste-of-time.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(466) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/15/chirp-pong/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chirp Pong – uses microphones and audio for networked game play</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chirp Pong：用麦克风与声音进行网络对战</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50114061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50114061'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chirp Pong demonstrates a Pong game using audio channels (microphones) as the network medium, illustrating low-bandwidth, novelty networking and potential privacy considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chirp Pong 用音频通道（麦克风）作为网络媒介来实现对战，展示了低带宽的新颖网络方式及潜在的隐私考量。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50098485' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50098485' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brooker.co.za/blog/2026/07/29/lorenz-and-little.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lorenz and Little: How Much Does Your Tail Cost?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>洛伦茨与 Little：尾部成本到底有多高？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50098485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50098485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50098485'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of tail-risk costs in probabilistic models, using Lorenz curves and Little&#x27;s law to illustrate how rare events dominate total costs, with implications for risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过洛伦茨曲线和 Little 定律，分析尾部风险在概率模型中的成本如何主导总成本，并讨论对风险管理的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于好奇心与发现乐趣的反思，讨论词典如何塑造语言学习与思维方式，并将寻找新词带来的瞬间洞察与工程实践联系起来。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50100387' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50100387' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(469) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/awslabs/aidlc-workflows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Aidlc: Adaptive workflow steering rules for AI coding agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Aidlc：用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50100387'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aidlc introduces adaptive workflow steering rules to guide AI coding agents, aiming to improve safety, reliability, and efficiency in code generation. The framework uses dynamic policies to adapt to tasks and context, reducing riskier operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aidlc 提出用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则，旨在提升代码生成的安全性、稳健性与效率。通过动态策略调整任务与上下文，降低风险操作的发生。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/30080308329dc1e6c26966fd45ec174c188d263606c117ec0f95ea7925fc9ba4/awslabs/aidlc-workflows' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/30080308329dc1e6c26966fd45ec174c188d263606c117ec0f95ea7925fc9ba4/awslabs/aidlc-workflows' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50042936' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50042936' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/hibanaworks/hibana' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Choreographic Programming in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 的编舞式编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50042936'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Choreographic programming applies global specifications to coordinate distributed components. The Rust project demonstrates how to express choreography as code, enabling safer composition of interacting modules. It offers a path to modeling concurrent systems with formal constraints while leveraging Rust&#x27;s type system.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>舞蹈式编程通过全局规范来协调分布式组件。该 Rust 项目展示了将编排作为代码书写，从而实现对交互模块的更安全组合。此方向为用形式化约束建模并发系统提供了一条利用 Rust 类型系统的路线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1050293157/371f64a2-fce4-4946-a52e-d77c21b93663' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1050293157/371f64a2-fce4-4946-a52e-d77c21b93663' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50091647' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50091647' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/uk-news/2026/oct/14/sally-rooney-says-she-can-no-longer-sell-books-in-the-uk-because-of-palestine-action-support' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sally Rooney can no longer sell books in UK due to Palestine Action support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>莎莉·鲁尼因支持巴勒斯坦行动在英国无法再销售书籍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50091647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author says her books can no longer be sold in the UK as a result of her public support for Palestine Action, a development that raises questions about marketplace power, political expression, and cultural censorship. The episode highlights tensions between activist stances and distribution channels, and the broader consequences when retailers or distributors take side actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者表示因公开支持 Palestine Action，她在英国的图书无法再被销售，这一事件引发了关于市场力量、政治表达与文化审查的讨论。此事突显了维权立场与发行渠道之间的张力，以及零售商或分销方采取立场行为时带来的广泛后果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/1b024240656da78be08f29add4bb52803ea9bd23/560_0_5600_4480/master/5600.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4ce02f795de396ccf9909749ca9d1121' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/1b024240656da78be08f29add4bb52803ea9bd23/560_0_5600_4480/master/5600.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4ce02f795de396ccf9909749ca9d1121' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50091519' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Education,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50091519' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/oct/14/dating-app-scams-us-congress' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US report details extent of &#x27;devastating&#x27; dating-app scams</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国报告详述“毁灭性”交友应用诈骗的范围</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50091519'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A government report documents extensive dating-app scams that cost victims large sums and exploited emotional manipulation, fake personas, and complex money-movement schemes. The findings underscore the need for better platform safeguards, faster law enforcement responses, and clearer user education to stem a lucrative online fraud ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份政府报告记录了广泛的交友应用诈骗，受害人因情感操纵、虚假身份和复杂的资金转移链条而遭受巨额损失。报告凸显了平台需加强防护、执法需加速响应以及用户教育需改进，以遏制这一高收益的网络诈骗生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/39ad56bf99c1f8e26f0f088ecc640f415267bd2f/581_0_2917_2334/master/2917.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=76a314d0404a2d6e9019a22107e456a2' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/39ad56bf99c1f8e26f0f088ecc640f415267bd2f/581_0_2917_2334/master/2917.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=76a314d0404a2d6e9019a22107e456a2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50141436' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ranet3.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Ranet3 – High performance policy routed IPsec mesh SD-WAN</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高性能策略路由的 IPsec Mesh SD-WAN</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50141436'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A high-performance design for policy-routed IPsec mesh SD-WAN, addressing latency, throughput, and security trade-offs for distributed networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍基于策略路由的 IPsec Mesh SD-WAN 的高性能设计，讨论分布式网络中的延迟、吞吐和安全取舍。</p>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50131053' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50131053' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(474) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.davepacheco.net/blog/2025/developing-for-debugging-dns-propagation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Developing for Debugging (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以调试为核心的开发（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50131053'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developing for debugging argues for building software with debugging in mind, including DNS propagation realities, observability, and reproducible environments to reduce toil.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Developing for Debugging》强调在开发阶段就把调试考虑进来，包括 DNS 传播现实、可观测性和可重复的执行环境，以降低调试成本。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50098815' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50098815' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/news/flock-ceo-doubles-down-if-you-want-a-crime-free-society-be-ready-to-give-up-your-privacy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock CEO: If you want a crime-free society, be ready to give up your privacy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock CEO：若要无犯罪社会就必须放弃隐私</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50098815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50098815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stoke.money' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Stoke Money – Personal finance app built around a cash flow forecast</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Stoke Money —— 以现金流预测为核心的理财应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50122776'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stoke Money centres personal finance around a cash-flow forecast rather than static budgets, predicting future balances and helping users manage irregular income and upcoming bills. That approach is especially useful for freelancers and gig workers who need forward-looking visibility rather than month-to-month accounting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stoke Money 把个人理财的核心放在现金流预测上，而非静态预算，预测未来余额并帮助用户管理不规则收入和即将到期的账单。对于自由职业者和零工经济从业者而言，这种前瞻性的可视化比按月记账更有用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(485) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pydantic.dev/articles/dynamic-shredding-2026-01-26' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shredding JSON columns gave Logfire up to 1000x faster queries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 JSON 列分解后，Logfire 查询快了多达 1000x</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50048490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes shredding JSON into relational columns to accelerate queries on JSON-heavy workloads, achieving up to 1000x faster performance. This technique aligns with columnar storage and schema-on-write patterns to unlock speedups in analytics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过将 JSON 列拆解成关系型列，显著提升对 JSON 密集型工作负载的查询速度，表现达到最高 1000x 的提升。这种做法与列式存储和写时模式相吻合，能在分析场景中带来极高的性能收益。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50142772' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(490) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thebignewsletter.com/p/how-the-federal-reserve-secretly' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Fed Is Quietly Inflating the AI Bubble While Hiking Your Electric Bill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美联储如何悄悄推高 AI 泡沫，同时抬升你的电费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50142772'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that easy monetary policy is fueling AI hype and asset prices while energy costs rise for households. It explores the macroeconomic costs of the AI boom.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出宽松的货币政策推动了与 AI 相关的投资热潮，推高了资产估值。与此同时，能源价格上涨让普通家庭的电费负担加重，揭示了 AI 热潮的宏观经济代价。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50138156' data-tags='Gaming,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50138156' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://knowledge.edge-online.com/p/the-game-industry-is-broken-can-we-fix-it-tim-sweeney-edge-crash' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The game industry is broken – can we fix it?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>游戏行业已经崩坏——我们能修复吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50138156'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues the game industry suffers from systemic issues like monetization pressures, crunch culture, and platform fragmentation. It suggests reforms such as stronger indie support, fair revenue sharing, and open ecosystems to restore sustainable creativity and consumer trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出游戏行业在商业模式、加班文化和平台碎片化等方面存在系统性问题，并提出改革方向，如增强对独立开发者的支持、建立更公平的分成模式、推动开放生态，以重建可持续的创作氛围与消费者信任。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.beehiiv.com/cdn-cgi/image/fit=scale-down,quality=80,width=1200,onerror=redirect/uploads/asset/file/88ed4e95-8fd2-4c26-b6d7-0af8dc34daaa/lead_image.jpg?t=1789462036' data-full='https://media.beehiiv.com/cdn-cgi/image/fit=scale-down,quality=80,width=1200,onerror=redirect/uploads/asset/file/88ed4e95-8fd2-4c26-b6d7-0af8dc34daaa/lead_image.jpg?t=1789462036' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50100640' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50100640' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://infino.ai/blog/search-index-on-s3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What changes when your search index lives on S3?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当你的搜索索引驻留在 S3 上会发生哪些变化？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50100640'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Storing a search index on S3 changes cost, scalability, and latency considerations, with benefits of durability and easy backups against traditional on-prem indexes. It also raises questions about data locality, performance, and vendor lock-in trade-offs that developers must weigh.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将搜索索引托管在 S3 上会改变成本结构、可扩展性和数据本地性。它带来持久性与易备份的好处，但也引入延迟、数据本地性及供应商锁定等取舍，需开发者权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://infino.ai/blog-covers/infino.png' data-full='https://infino.ai/blog-covers/infino.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jlJyuJhDnlc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Martin Odersky – Keynote: The End of Programming? [video]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50062025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50062025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50062025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Odersky questions whether programming as a craft is nearing its end, exploring alternative models, AI-assisted coding, and shifts in language design and tooling. A provocative glimpse into the future of software development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Martin Odersky 质疑编程作为一门技艺是否走向尽头，探讨替代模型、AI 辅助编程以及语言设计与工具的演变，为软件开发的未来提供思考。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(494) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/11/opinion/clutter-spring-cleaning-psychology.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neuroscience can make decluttering easier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>神经科学能让收纳整理变得更容易</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040317' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040317' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50040317'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neuroscience-informed strategies claim to make decluttering easier by aligning habits with how the brain rewards attention. Practical tips include reducing friction in the environment and building micro-habits that reinforce progress. The piece reframes tidying as a cognitive-behavioral task rather than a purely motivational one.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>神经科学观点认为，通过对齐习惯与大脑奖励机制和注意力运作，可以让整理杂物变得更容易执行。提出的实用建议包括降低整理时的阻力、建立能巩固进步的微习惯等。文章将整理从单纯的动机问题转化为认知行为层面的任务。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50145295' data-tags='Open Source,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-50145295' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(495) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Netflix/vmaf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Netflix vmaf: Perceptual video quality assessment based on multi-method fusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Netflix VMAF：基于多方法融合的主观感知视频质量评估</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50145295'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netflix VMAF blends several perceptual quality metrics into a single score, improving correlation with viewer experience across encoding settings. The project shows how multi-method fusion enhances streaming QA and codec evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Netflix VMAF 将多种感知质量指标融合为一个综合分数，在不同编码设置下与用户观感的相关性更高，提升了流媒体的质量评估与编解码评估能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/752b05b19e23a5fbcfd5c63c7b15ebe054cca30897435a2e280a301cc09d4589/Netflix/vmaf' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/752b05b19e23a5fbcfd5c63c7b15ebe054cca30897435a2e280a301cc09d4589/Netflix/vmaf' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(498) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/omnipty-terminal/omnipty' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: OmniPTY – Opinionated terminal emulator for macOS and Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：OmniPTY – 面向 macOS 与 Linux 的定向终端仿真器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50145628'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: OmniPTY is an opinionated terminal emulator for macOS and Linux, delivering a streamlined, plugin-friendly terminal experience with optimized PTY handling. Aimed at power users who value speed and control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：OmniPTY 是面向 macOS 与 Linux 的定向终端仿真器，提供简洁的终端体验、插件友好、优化的 PTY 处理，面向追求速度与控制的高阶用户。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(500) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jetp.ras.ru/cgi-bin/dn/e_023_05_0893.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Propagation of Sound in Strongly Viscous Liquids (1966) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在强粘性液体中的声传播（1966）[PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-50126457'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A classic JETP paper analyzes how sound waves propagate in strongly viscous liquids, detailing attenuation, velocity changes, and viscous damping. The results inform fluid dynamics understanding and have implications for material science and acoustics research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇1966年的经典论文系统分析在强粘性液体中声波的传播特性，涵盖衰减、速度变化及黏性阻尼等现象。研究有助于深化对流体动力学的理解，并对材料科学和声学研究具备参考价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
